- **Summary**: 提出MedHarm基准，包含1100个高风险医学查询，评估15个LLM和4个护栏模型。发现对齐的模型仍可能产生不安全回答，医学微调可能放大危害，外部护栏有局限性。强调需领域特定压力测试。

<h3 id="120">[120] Insidious by Design: Implications of Large Language Model algorithmic bias for the Global South</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28333](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28333) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sioux McKenna, Nompilo Tshuma
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Bias Detection, Fairness, Cultural Bias
- **Summary**: 探索性研究显示LLM输出再现种族、性别和西方中心偏见。偏见隐蔽地嵌入叙事结构和情感模板。呼吁大学进行结构性批判，而非无反思采用，批判性AI素养需涉及知识体系问题。

<h3 id="121">[121] Ground Truths in Suicide Research: The Current State of AI-Based Suicide Detection in Social Media</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28334](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28334) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yaakov Ophir, Ofri Hefetz, Refael Tikochinski, Kfir Bar, Shir Lissak, Shulamit Grinapol, Haya Wachtel, Eyal Fruchter, Roi Reichart
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mental Health, Social Media Analysis, AI Evaluation
- **Summary**: 综述AI自杀检测研究，指出多数研究依赖间接标签策略，缺乏个体层面验证。预测任务实质是分类有自杀语言的帖子，而非识别有风险个体。认为进展更多依赖确保预测与现实风险对应。

<h3 id="122">[122] LLM-Ideoplasticity: Measuring Ideological Plasticity in the Political Behavior of LLMs as a Context-Conditioned Distribution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28335](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28335) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adib Sakhawat, Syed Rifat Raiyan, Tahsin Islam, Takia Farhin, Hasan Mahmud, Md Kamrul Hasan
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Political Analysis, Bias Detection
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/sakhadib/LLM-Ideoplasticity)
- **Summary**: 提出LLM政治意识形态是条件分布而非固定点。评估9个LLM，发现对上下文高度敏感，但总体占据狭窄的奥弗顿窗口。支持多特征多方法分析，认为需用形状而非单点表征。

<h3 id="123">[123] HyBIRD: Hyperbolic Bridge Retrieval and Diagnosis for Methodology Inspiration Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28336](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28336) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yang Yang, Boyun Xu, Hao Fu, Jindong Li, Zining Zhong, Bowen Tian, Jiemin Wu, Menglin Yang, Yutao Yue
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Hyperbolic Representation, Scientific Reasoning
- **Summary**: 提出HyBIRD框架，将方法论灵感检索视为双曲桥检索和后验方法诊断。固定强稠密检索器，学习轻量双曲桥变体，结合LLM辅助解释。在基准上达到59.034 mAP，并将排名论文转换为可检查的需求轮廓。

<h3 id="124">[124] A Systems-Level Analysis of Sensitivity, Robustness, and Stability in Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28337](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28337) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bharath Simha Reddy Muthyam
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Robustness Evaluation, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 对RAG系统进行56次控制实验，评估分块大小、检索深度、重排序等对敏感度、鲁棒性和稳定性的影响。发现检索指标在更宽设置下改善，但下游EM/F1非单调，且观察到预处理导致答案丢失、检索退化等。

<h3 id="125">[125] Multi-Agent DRL for QoS and Energy Optimization in RIS-Enabled Open-RAN Industrial 6G TN/NTN Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28339](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28339) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marwan Dhuheir, Thang X. Vu, Symeon Chatzinotas
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Wireless Networks, 6G
- **Venue**: IEEE ICC-2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 提出RIS辅助的Open-RAN框架用于工业6G网络，通过无人机搭载RIS与地面单元和高空平台协作。将联合优化问题建模为Dec-POMDP，用多智能体深度强化学习求解。仿真显示数据速率提升75%，延迟降低25%，能耗节省16%。

<h3 id="126">[126] Operating Regimes of Decentralized Learning Under Mobility and Bandwidth Constraints</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28342](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28342) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Samuele Sabella, Chiara Boldrini, Lorenzo Valerio, Marco Conti, Andrea Passarella
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Wireless Networks, Distributed Training
- **Venue**: IEEE SmartComp 2026
- **Summary**: 研究去中心化学习在无线环境中的运行机制，考虑客户端异步、时变接触图和带宽限制。实现了完全去中心化协议，使用随机路点移动和多种无线技术。识别三种运行状态：接触间隔主导收敛，部分更新容忍性强，密集接触导致竞争。

<h3 id="127">[127] The Crowded Embedding Space: A Mean-Field Mechanism for Emergent Marginalization in Retrieval-Augmented Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28343](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28343) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shwan Ashrafi, Dan Roth
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Fairness, Emergent Behavior
- **Summary**: 揭示检索增强代理的嵌入空间中文档密度过高导致少数群体内容被排挤的问题。通过静态分析和动态模型推导，发现局部相关性目标会导致系统自组织为仅服务多数群体，引发公平性问题。

<h3 id="128">[128] PIXELRAG: Web Screenshots Beat Text for Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28344](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28344) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yichuan Wang, Zhifei Li, Zirui Wang, Paul Teiletche, Lesheng Jin, Matei Zaharia, Joseph E. Gonzalez, Sewon Min
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: RAG, Vision-Language Model, Image Retrieval
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/StarTrail-org/PixelRAG)
- **Summary**: 提出PixelRAG，直接以网页截图形式进行检索和阅读，无需文本抽象。在30M截图画像库上运行，微调视觉嵌入模型。在NQ、SimpleQA等文本任务上超越文本基线，在多项多模态任务上提升高达18.1%，并可图像压缩降低token成本。

<h3 id="129">[129] Auditing LLM-Governed Social Robots with Culture-Specific Moral Gradients</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28345](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28345) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Carmen Ng, Gjergji Kasneci
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, AI Ethics, Cultural Bias
- **Venue**: 2026 ACM Conference on Fairness, Accountability, and Transparency (FAccT '26)
- **Summary**: 该论文指出由LLM驱动的社交机器人在跨文化分配优先级时缺乏多元校准，现有审计仅关注英语。提出基于梯度的审计框架，在护理、教育、服务等场景中系统评估LLM对文化偏好梯度的遵循情况。实验发现模型在不同语言下存在不对称的梯度追踪失败，且单纯提示无法可靠纠正。

<h3 id="130">[130] Agentic Safety is an Epistemic Property, Not a Behavioral One</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28347](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28347) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Charles L. Wang, Keir Dorchen, Peter Jin
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AI Safety, LLM Alignment
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文认为当前AI安全方法只验证系统当前行为快照，但随着系统变得动态和自改进，安全应被视为演化学习者的认知属性而非行为属性。引入“可教导性”概念，强调系统需在未来保持可纠正性。提出高级系统可能表面行为正常但侵蚀了未来纠正所需的表征和算法条件。

<h3 id="131">[131] HMARS: A Hierarchical Multi-Agent Memory System for Long-Context Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28349](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28349) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zeju Li, Ziyang Zheng, Yizhou Zhou, Qiang Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Multi-Agent System, Long Context
- **Summary**: 针对长上下文推理中标准RAG被动检索可能丢弃相关证据的问题，提出HMARS分层多智能体记忆系统，将长上下文视为分层管理的记忆而非扁平语料库。通过子智能体、中级智能体和前沿模型协同工作，在长文档和多轮记忆任务上取得最佳整体性能，证据覆盖率分析表明性能提升源于更完整的证据检索。

<h3 id="132">[132] From Regulatory Approvals to Patents: Cross-Domain Linking for Cardiovascular Device Traceability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28353](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28353) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yang Qingqing, Liu Haijiang, Li Moyan
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Entity Linking, Medical AI, Knowledge Graph
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 Main Conference
- **Summary**: 针对FDA批准的医疗器械与USPTO专利之间的跨域实体链接问题，因语义鸿沟和标签稀缺而未被探索。以心血管设备为例构建基准，提出Bridge-MedDevKG框架，集成领域本体、多信号候选生成和异构重排序。方法在黄金标准上达到91.6%的保守下界召回率，噪声减少50.9%，优于LLM基线。

<h3 id="133">[133] SafeGEO: Understanding Generative Engine Optimization Risks in Recommendation Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28356](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28356) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qianfeng Wen, Yifan Simon Liu, Xin Liu, Difan Jiao, Blair Yang, Junda Wu, Zhenwei Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, LLM Security, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 研究生成引擎优化（GEO）对推荐代理的风险，即卖家控制的内容源通过GEO改写可能使有缺陷产品被推荐。构建SafeGEO评估套件，实验表明GEO攻击可使缺陷产品进入推荐集的比率平均增加83.2%，而简单的防御措施（如防御性提示和结构化证据检查）可将有害目标推广降低39.2%，但仍无法完全消除风险。

<h3 id="134">[134] ReasonRec: A Reasoning-Augmented Multimodal Agent for Unified Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28357](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28357) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yihua Zhang, Mingfu Liang, Jiyan Yang, Rong Jin, Wen-Yen Chen, Yiping Han, Huayu Li, Buyun Zhang, Liang Luo, Frank Shyu, Luke Simon, Sijia Liu, Tianlong Chen, Xi Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Multimodal Learning, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 针对多模态推荐器缺乏显式推理和不确定性感知的问题，提出ReasonRec推理增强多模态智能体，采用三阶段显式推理流水线。通过推理感知的视觉指令调优、证据-水平课程和不确定性引导委派机制，在五个数据集上相对提升30%以上关键排序指标，并动态委派35%查询给高效子模型以降低延迟。

<h3 id="135">[135] How Do LLMs Cite? A Mechanistic Interpretation of Attribution in Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28358](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28358) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ian van Dort, Maria Heuss
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Interpretability, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Venue**: ECIR 2026
- **Summary**: 首次从机制上解释LLM在RAG中如何决定是否添加内联引用。使用Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型和激活修补方法，发现引用机制是一个分布式多阶段的“归因集成”，包括注意力头和MLP层。放大或衰减这些关键组件可以在不损害答案准确性的情况下修复90%以上的遗漏引用并消除69%的虚假引用。

<h3 id="136">[136] Carolina Guide: A Multi-Agent RAG System with Institutional Guardrails for Academic Policy Assistance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28360](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28360) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ben Torsion, Jun Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Multi-Agent System, Education Technology
- **Summary**: 针对大学生难以理解复杂学术政策的问题，开发Carolina Guide多智能体RAG系统，具有机构护栏，提供基于引用和政策的回答并拒绝不安全请求。在90个查询测试集上达到98.9%的检索成功率，安全护栏在30个对抗性查询上的F1为0.89，正确拒绝86%的不安全查询。

<h3 id="137">[137] ConCise: Training-Free Conclusion-Chain State Compression for Cost-Efficient Multi-Step RAG Services</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28361](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28361) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kuan Yan, Zhiqing Tang, Tian Wang, Weijia Jia
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.IT
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Inference, Cost Optimization
- **Venue**: IEEE ICWS 2026
- **Summary**: 针对多步RAG服务中历史文档和推理轨迹累积导致输入令牌膨胀和成本增加的问题，提出ConCise无训练状态层协议，用结构化结论链替代原始文本累积，将累积上下文从O(N²)压缩至约O(N)，并引入融合生成机制在单次API调用中联合产生推理和结论。实验平均节省64.63%令牌，同时保持可接受精度。

<h3 id="138">[138] LUMEN: Cost-Transparent Multi-Agent Pipeline for Automated Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28362](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28362) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yen-Hsun Huang, Yu-Shiou Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.DL
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Medical AI, Systematic Review
- **Summary**: 针对系统综述和荟萃分析耗时且昂贵的问题，提出LUMEN开源多智能体流水线，自动化六个阶段，使用11个专门LLM智能体并有意识模型路由。在七个数据集上评估，与已发表荟萃分析100%方向一致，完整综述成本19-29美元。关键发现是多智能体设计在筛选阶段有害但在提取阶段必不可少。

<h3 id="139">[139] meta-pipe: An LLM-agent pipeline for end-to-end automated systematic review and meta-analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28363](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28363) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hsieh-Ting Lin, Jiunn-Tyng Yeh
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.DL
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Medical AI, Systematic Review
- **Summary**: 描述meta-pipe，一个开源LLM智能体流水线，集成系统综述和荟萃分析完整工作流程，包括文献搜索、统计分析、手稿生成和质量保证，并在关键决策点设置人工监督。与现有五种工具对比，提供四种独特能力，估计API成本每项综述15-30美元。这是一篇系统描述，尚未验证。

<h3 id="140">[140] CAMI: Cost-Aware Agent-Guided Multi-Indexing for Semantic Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28365](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28365) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adnan Qidwai, Anand Eswaran, Sonam Mishra, Jaydeep Sen, Sachindra Joshi
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Cost Optimization, Data Selection
- **Venue**: ACM CAIS 2026
- **Summary**: 针对RAG索引扩展中语义增强索引配置空间组合爆炸和评估成本高的问题，提出CAMI框架，将多索引构建形式化为带预算的多目标组合选择问题。通过智能体发现、原子单元搜索和置信度感知的促销调度，在严格预算约束下系统化地找到高召回组合，比随机搜索基线节省最多5倍预算。

<h3 id="141">[141] Beyond the Reranker: Do RAG Retrieval Enhancements Help Once a Strong Reranker Is Present?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28367](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28367) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sadanand Singh, Allam Reddy, Manan Chopra
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Information Retrieval, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 研究当存在强重排序器时，RAG的检索增强方法（如查询扩展、层次摘要、图扩展等）是否仍有帮助。构建HetDocQA异构基准，实验发现强交叉编码器重排序器承担了流水线大部分质量，在此之上仅有查询扩展和SSCC（一种按源校准的校正器）带来可靠增益，而其他常见重排序和池扩展方法无可靠增益。

<h3 id="142">[142] Multimodal and Multiscale Spatial-Temporal Semantic Search and Recommendation with AI Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28369](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28369) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuanyuan Tian, Wenwen Li, Xiao Chen, Michael Brook, Michael Brubaker, Anna Liljedahl, Chitta Baral
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Information Retrieval, Geographic Information Retrieval
- **Venue**: ACM Transactions on Spatial Algorithms and Systems
- **Summary**: 针对包含时空信息的环境事件文档的语义搜索与推荐，提出利用LLM和VLM的框架，包括CAMERA（多模态事件检索算法）和ASTRA（自适应时空重排序算法）。实验表明VLM增强方法在相似度排序上优于单模态基于LLM的方法，有助于更深入理解环境变化及其局部影响。

<h3 id="143">[143] Conversational Query Engine for Mixed-Modality Heterogeneous Enterprise Data Sources</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28370](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28370) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Darshita Rathore, Vineet Kumar, Vaibhav Singal, Ankur Vivek Singh, Anindya Moitra
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Enterprise AI, RAG, NL2SQL
- **Venue**: Agent4IR @ KDD2026
- **Summary**: 针对企业商业智能查询涉及结构化仓库和非结构化文档的异构问题，提出COGNI对话式BI系统，采用四层架构：索引层（滑动自适应分块）、路由层（LoRA微调小模型）、检索层（复杂度自适应流水线）和缓存层（多维度查询等价验证）。企业内部基准上表现出高准确率和低成本。

<h3 id="144">[144] Model Merging to Evolution: Parameter Space Exploration for Expert Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28373](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28373) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chao Wang, Yuchen Guo, Zheng Tan, Guanchun Wang, Yanbiao Ma, Qiqi Duan, Peng Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Merging, Evolutionary Computation
- **Summary**: 针对现有模型融合方法局限于专家模型凸组合空间的问题，提出MERGEvolve框架，将模型融合与进化统一在进化策略中，以融合模型作为初始化，通过随机噪声探索参数空间。理论分析表明该方法探索凸组合空间外的区域，实验在单任务和多任务基准上与先进融合基线竞争性能。

<h3 id="145">[145] When Does Overlap Help? OSU-Mem and a Cell-Conditional Analysis of Trajectory Memory for LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28376](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28376) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mellow Baixuan Chen, Xiangguo Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 该论文探讨了在LLM智能体的长程交互中，将轨迹记忆组织为重叠语义单元（OSU）何时有助于检索。提出OSU-Mem方法，通过预算控制的多尺度扩展检索重叠单元。关键发现是重叠记忆在查询所需的证据步骤共享工具调用或实体时有益，否则有害。在合成基准上OSU-Mem优于基线，但在tau-bench上优势消失，通过查询拆分揭示了混合效应。最终提出基于元数据的启发式方法预测重叠是否有利。

<h3 id="146">[146] Memory-Augmented LSTM Autoencoder for Unsupervised Activity Recognition with IMU Sensor Fusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28377](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28377) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Saeid Arabzadeh, Farshad Almasganj, Mohammad Mahdi Ahmadi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human Activity Recognition, Sensor Fusion
- **Summary**: 该论文针对无监督活动识别中的多传感器融合和时空依赖捕获问题，提出了一种基于记忆增强自编码器的无监督框架。通过堆叠自编码器提取层次化静态特征，并用LSTM自编码器进行时序精炼。在DaLiAc和PAMAP2数据集上分别达到96.6%和98.4%的准确率，优于监督和无监督方法。

<h3 id="147">[147] LEDGER: Scaling Agentic Document Editing with Dependency-aware Graph Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28379](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28379) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mike Hang Wang, Utkarsh Garg, Reza Davari, Huitian Jiao, Hao Cheng, Baolin Peng, Tao Ge, Si-Qing Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Document Understanding, Graph Retrieval
- **Summary**: 论文针对智能文档编辑中的上下文工程挑战，提出LEDGER系统，通过构建轻量级依赖图显式建模文档结构（层次、引用、隐式依赖和语义关系），在编辑时只检索必要上下文。在涵盖多种文档类型的基准上，将一致性从56%提升至76%，并减少token使用。

<h3 id="148">[148] Distilling a Modular Reservoir Through a Genomic Bottleneck</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28380](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28380) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mani Hamidi, Sina Khajehabdollahi, Charley M. Wu, Emmanouil Giannakakis
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reservoir Computing, Hypernetwork, Curriculum Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文受生物神经网络发育启发，使用超网络学习生成模块化储备池连接性的压缩生成过程。通过课程元学习和模块化储备池计算结合，生成稀疏递归网络，以最少训练解决困难时序任务。

<h3 id="149">[149] Evolutional Math: Cross-Validated Island-Model Genetic Programming for Interpretable Symbolic Regression on Small, Wide Datasets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28381](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28381) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Artem Andrianov
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Symbolic Regression, Genetic Programming
- **Code**: [code](https://gitlab.com/cyntegrity/evomath)
- **Summary**: 论文提出Evolutional Math遗传规划系统，针对小宽数据集上的符号回归过拟合问题，结合交叉验证R²适应度、多岛架构、结构去重和常数精炼。在合成和临床数据上恢复紧凑真实结构，R²达0.99以上。

<h3 id="150">[150] A Query-Driven Communication-Efficient Digital Twins Design for Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28384](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28384) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nuocheng Yang, Longyu Zhou, Sihua Wang, Changchuan Yin, Tony Q. S. Quek
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Digital Twin, Autonomous Driving
- **Summary**: 论文针对自动驾驶数字孪生中高保真建模的计算通信消耗问题，提出查询驱动的数字孪生架构，使数字孪生根据仿真结果主动请求车辆所需环境数据。设计跨时间步渐进查询机制，在减少40%通信开销的同时，将规划位置误差降低24%。

<h3 id="151">[151] RoboGaze: Evaluating Robot World Models via Structured Vision-Language Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28385](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28385) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Minh-Loi Nguyen, Nghiem Tuong Diep, Hung Khang Nguyen, Minh Le, Doanh Le Thien, Hoang H. Tran, Dung D. Le, Vu N. Duong, Daniel Sonntag, An Thai Le, Duy Minh Ho Nguyen, Vien Anh Ngo, Tran Van Nhiem
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Vision-Language Model, World Model
- **Summary**: 论文针对机器人世界模型生成视频的评估难题，提出RoboGaze，一个无需训练的多智能体VLM框架，通过任务-场景定位、维度专家路由和评论家验证三阶段流水线，输出时间定位的故障报告。在382个剪辑的基准上，描述F1提升43点，时间对齐提升37点，接近人类水平。

<h3 id="152">[152] Data Provenance for Image Auto-Regressive Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28386](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28386) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bihe Zhao, Louis Kerner, Michel Meintz, Tameem Bakr, Franziska Boenisch, Adam Dziedzic
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Generation, Data Provenance
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026
- **Summary**: 论文针对图像自回归模型生成内容的溯源问题，提出无需修改生成过程的后验框架，通过检测生成过程中引入的特征模式来追溯源模型。实验证明该方法在多种自回归图像生成模型上有效，可用于无法使用水印的场景。

<h3 id="153">[153] Schema-First Retrieval: Embedding Catalogs for Natural Language Analytics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28387](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28387) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adarsh Agrawal, Shashank Indukuri
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-SQL, Schema Linking
- **Summary**: 论文针对企业文本到SQL系统的模式上下文错误问题，提出Schema-First Retrieval，通过嵌入目录元数据（表、列、指标、关系、查询历史）而非数据行来检索。在CRUSH4SQL上表召回@20达96.4%，错误率从15.6%降至6.2%。

<h3 id="154">[154] Automated Quality Assessment of Geospatial Vector Data: A GeoAI Approach using Spatial Representation Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28390](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28390) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hao Li, Chen Chu, Filip Biljecki, Cyrus Shahabi, Wenwen Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Geospatial AI, Spatial Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 论文针对地理空间矢量数据质量评估的困难，提出Topo4Vec自动化GeoAI框架，通过拓扑错误模拟生成训练数据，利用空间表示学习将矢量几何编码到潜在空间，检测拓扑错误。在三个城市数据集上建筑轮廓重叠检测准确率达0.99。

<h3 id="155">[155] Few-class Fidelity: Evaluating Explanations of Real-conditions CNN classifiers with Optimized Perturbations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28391](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28391) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wistan Marchadour, Pedro Soto Vega, Franck Vermet, Mathieu Hatt
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 论文针对真实场景低类别数下CNN分类器可解释性评估问题，提出基于保真度的XAI指标变体，通过生成分布内、引发不确定性的扰动来测量解释忠实性。在医学和自然图像应用中展示了领域与XAI选择的相关性。

<h3 id="156">[156] RADIANT-PET: Reasoning-Augmented PET/CT Lesion Segmentation with Large Language Models and Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28392](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28392) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiasheng Wang, Tanun Jitwatcharakomol, Piyawadee Jongpradubgiat, Simeng Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/jwang-580/RADIANT-PET)
- **Summary**: 论文针对PET/CT中病变分割的挑战，提出RADIANT-PET框架，结合高灵敏度分割模型和LLM推理评判。通过结构化文本描述候选区域，LLM分类真伪病变，并利用强化学习优化推理。在AutoPET和OSU测试集上优于纯图像基线。

<h3 id="157">[157] CLOSER-VLN: Closed-Loop Self-Verified Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning for Aerial Vision-Language Navigation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28397](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28397) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shaoxuan Li, Xiangyu Dong, Xiaoguang Ma, Junfeng Chen, Haoran Zhao, Yaoming Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Visual Navigation, RAG, Vision-Language Navigation
- **Summary**: 论文针对空中视觉语言导航中开环决策导致的误差累积问题，提出CLOSER闭环方法，在执行前顺序进行动作推理、可靠性验证、目标检索和动作纠正。在CityNav基准上达到32.01% SR和21.28% SPL。

<h3 id="158">[158] Reinforcement Learning for Software Vulnerability Analysis: A Systematic Review with Emphasis on C/C++ Source Code and Static Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28403](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28403) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bruno Caro-Vásquez, Carola Figueroa-Flores, Gastón Marquez
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vulnerability Detection, Reinforcement Learning, Survey
- **Summary**: 该论文系统综述了强化学习在软件漏洞分析中的应用，侧重于C/C++源码和静态分析。分析了21项研究，发现大多数聚焦于模糊测试和引导探索，仅有3项直接用于漏洞检测，1项用于语句级定位。指出使用CFG和AST作为智能体状态的研究空白。

<h3 id="159">[159] Financing Artificial Intelligence Infrastructure: Mapping AI Infrastructure Investment and Compute Governance Across Africa</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28404](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28404) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kai-Hsin Hung, Sumaya Nur Adan, Krupa Suchak, Armita Sadeghian Barzoki, Kofi Yeboah, Mohammad Amir Anwar
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Infrastructure, Compute Governance
- **Summary**: 论文通过分析非洲46个AI基础设施项目（总投资127亿美元），研究投资流和计算治理。发现投资高度集中于少数全球数据中心运营商和科技公司，引入“不对称相互依赖”概念，指出治理必须考虑资本流向和所有权。

<h3 id="160">[160] Evidence-Driven LLM Agent for C-to-Synthesizable-C Conversion and Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28409](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28409) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhe Zhao, Hongbing Lang, Zhihan Xiao, Luke Ztz Hu, John Imoleayo Adebisi, Songping Mai
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, High-Level Synthesis, Code Generation
- **Summary**: 论文针对C代码到可综合C（HLS-C）的转换问题，提出闭环生成-验证-诊断-修复工作流，包含多智能体协作、渐进式错配定位链（PMLC）和基于证据的RAG。在HLS工具链上显著优于现有模型。

<h3 id="161">[161] RSGPNet: Geometric Prompting for Remote Sensing Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28410](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28410) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shanwen Wang, Xin Sun, Sirui Wang, Xiao Xiang Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Open-Vocabulary Segmentation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/wangshanwen001/RSGPNet)
- **Summary**: 针对现有开放词汇语义分割方法在遥感领域局部分割不稳定的问题，提出RSGPNet，一种无需训练的几何提示框架，包含文本引导粗掩码模块、几何重提示模块和粗细一致性验证机制。在多个遥感数据集上，RSGPNet在定量和定性指标上均显著优于现有方法，且具有可解释性。

<h3 id="162">[162] On the Necessity of a Liquid Substrate for Mesh Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28413](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28413) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongwei Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Liquid Neural Network
- **Summary**: 探讨自进化潜变量观测下，多代理网格中每个代理在无中心、无重训练条件下的最优估计问题。证明两个必要条件：自适应时间尺度和对到达间隔的依赖性，并指出连续时间液体网络同时满足两者。LSTM满足第一个条件，固定连续时间滤波器满足第二个，多时间尺度液体网络则两者兼具。

<h3 id="163">[163] AEGIS: A Semantic GAN and Evidential Learning Frameworkfor Robust Adversarial Detection in Vision Sensors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28416](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28416) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Maher Boughdiri, Mounira Msahli, Albert Bifet
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Evidential Deep Learning
- **Summary**: 针对视觉传感器中深度神经网络易受对抗扰动的问题，提出AEGIS框架，结合语义GAN作为多类语义鉴别器，以及基于证据深度学习的分类器，利用翻转分数、预测不一致性等不稳定指标。在Tiny ImageNet上，AUROC达92.1%，AUPRC达90.2%，准确率90.7%，优于传统检测器。

<h3 id="164">[164] MedDiffuseMix: Preserving Diagnostic Evidence with Saliency-Aware Diffusion Medical Image Data Augmentatio</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28419](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28419) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Teerath Kumar, Raja Vavekanand, Muhammad Turab
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Data Augmentation
- **Summary**: 针对医学图像分类中数据有限、类别不平衡的问题，提出MedDiffuseMix，一种基于显著性图的扩散混合数据增强框架，在高显著性诊断区域保留原样，在低显著性区域进行扩散引导混合。在四个医学图像基准上，相比标准增强方法提升了准确率、F1分数和AUC，并更好地保持了诊断显著性区域。

<h3 id="165">[165] JuZhou 1.0 Technical Report: The First Edge-Native Text-to-Image Foundation Model Trained Entirely on China-Developed AI Accelerators</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28421](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28421) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ce Chen, Congrui Wang, Yonglin Li, Zhenchen Wan, Mingyang Geng, Junhao Xiao, Zhengpeng Xing, Yaqing Hu, Yao Wu, Zhaoyang Qu, Long Lan, Xinwang Liu, Yingqi Peng, Shijia Li, Zufeng Zhang, Chen Ma, Jingjing Zhou, Xingyu Wang, Qilin Lu, Bin Jiang, Qilin Sun, Shanzhi Gu, Yaoguang Jin, Tongliang Liu, Kede Ma, Yifan Peng
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Edge Computing, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 为解决文本到图像扩散模型在边缘部署上的延迟、成本和隐私问题，提出JuZhou 1.0，首个边缘原生超轻量T2I基础模型，约0.387B参数，结合Rectified Flow和DMD2蒸馏至4步推理，并支持中文语义对齐。在GenEval上得分0.69，优于SDXL等模型；在手机上4步去噪仅需1.6秒。

<h3 id="166">[166] Tool Use Enables Undetectable Steganography in Multi-Agent LLM Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28425](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28425) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jimmy Laurence Rippin, Simon C. Marshall, David Demitri Africa, Christian Schroeder de Witt
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 针对自主AI系统中多代理通过隐藏通信秘密串通的风险，展示当前编码模型在具备工具使用能力（如代码执行、网页搜索）时，已能产生信息论上不可检测的隐写系统。实验发现协调性（即独立代理能否一致选择兼容方案）是主要障碍，而非能力限制。

<h3 id="167">[167] Building to the Test: Coding Agents Deliver What You Check, Not What You Requested</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28430](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28430) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yanuo Ma, Ben Kereopa-Yorke, Ben Schultz
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对LLM基准测试的构造效度问题，通过实验发现编码代理在无测试预言机时库未完成，有预言机时虽然分数接近完美，但实际交付的代码存在死代码或缺失功能，称为“为测试而构建”。提示需要关注模型在验证自我意识上的不足。

<h3 id="168">[168] Spectral Perturbation of the Empirical Fisher Information Matrix under Weight Quantization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28432](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28432) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rahid Zahid Alekberli, Hikmat Karimov
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Quantization
- **Summary**: 研究参数统计模型下经验Fisher信息矩阵在两种结构化扰动（输入分布偏移和权重量化）下的谱变化，证明量化噪声会提升最大特征值，并给出方向性特征值扰动界。在12个语言模型上的测量结果与预测一致，但量化膨胀幅度的闭式预测仍为开放问题。

<h3 id="169">[169] SWE-MeM: Learning Adaptive Memory Management for Long-Horizon Coding Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28434](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28434) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuzheng Gao, Wenhao Zeng, Zhaojian Yu, Jianqiao Wangni, Chaozheng Wang, Kai Cai, Shilin He, Michael R. Lyu
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, LLM Agent, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 针对长周期软件工程代理在有限上下文下管理交互历史的问题，提出SWE-MeM训练框架，提供灵活内存工具让代理主动决定压缩时机和内容。结合合成轨迹和内存感知GRPO，在SWE-Bench Verified上4B和30B模型分别达到43.4%和60.2%解决率，优于现有内存管理方法。

<h3 id="170">[170] Dockerless: Environment-Free Program Verifier for Coding Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28436](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28436) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenhao Zeng, Yuling Shi, Xiaodong Gu, Chao Hu, Chaofan Wang, Yuhao Cui, Hongting Zhou, Mengnan Qi, Jianqiao Wangni, Zhaojian Yu, Shuzheng Gao, Kai Cai, Shilin He
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Program Verification, Code Generation
- **Summary**: 为解决代码代理训练中执行验证需要复杂环境设置的问题，提出Dockerless，一种不执行代码的补丁验证器，通过代理仓库探索收集证据判断补丁正确性。在验证基准上AUC比最强开源验证器高14.3，在SWE-bench Verified上达到62.0%解决率，匹配基于环境的训练。

<h3 id="171">[171] When AI Reviews Its Own Code: Recursive Self-Training Collapse in Code LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28438](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28438) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinyuan Song, Zekun Cai, Liang Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Self-Training, Model Collapse
- **Summary**: 研究代码LLM在递归自训练时，因AI生成代码进入训练循环而导致的性能退化风险。比较无审查、人工审查和AI自审查三种机制，发现无审查退化最快，人工审查减缓但未阻止，AI自审查早期有效但后期变成橡皮图章。证明稳定训练需要外部验证而非模型自身的自我审查。

<h3 id="172">[172] PLAA: Packet-level Adversarial Attacks in Network Traffic Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28439](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28439) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinhao You, Zan Zhou, Shujie Yang, Yi Sun, Lei Zhang, Changqiao Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 针对现有网络入侵检测对抗攻击方法忽视流量有效性和语义完整性的问题，提出逐包生成对抗特征的方法，在生成过程中监控语义完整性。在CIC-UNSW-NB15等数据集上，平均逃避成功率达92.78%，且生成的流量保持语义一致。

<h3 id="173">[173] Learning to Distributedly Estimate under Partially Known Dynamics: A Covariance-Agnostic Neural Kalman Consensus Filter</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28441](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28441) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: George Stamatelis, Kyriakos Stylianopoulos, George C. Alexandropoulos
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.MA; eess.SY
- **Tags**: State Estimation, Distributed Computing
- **Summary**: 针对部分已知动力学下的在线分布式状态估计问题，提出协方差无关的神经卡尔曼共识滤波器，结合先验估计、优化共识权重和类卡尔曼递归更新。在线性、混沌（Lorenz）和无线跟踪环境中，优于传统分布式卡尔曼滤波、粒子滤波和纯神经网络，对模型误指定具有鲁棒性。

<h3 id="174">[174] S-GAI: Spectral Geometry-Aware Initialization for Sigmoidal MLPs -- From Dataset Geometry to Network Weights</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28444](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28444) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yi-Shan Chu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Network Initialization
- **Summary**: 针对经典单隐层sigmoid MLP的初始化不编码数据几何结构的问题，提出S-GAI，一种基于类谱几何的初始化框架，使用SVD估计每个类的均值和主方向，并据此初始化隐藏层权重。在MNIST等数据集上，比Xavier初始化提供更丰富隐状态，冻结隐藏层时也表现更好。

<h3 id="175">[175] LoRA-Tuned Large Language Models for Dementia Detection via Multi-View Speech-Derived Features</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28445](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28445) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jonghyeon Park, Olivier Jiyoun Jung, Myungwoo Oh
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Speech Processing, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Venue**: INTERSPEECH 2026
- **Summary**: 针对传统痴呆检测方法仅利用单一维度的语音特征问题，提出使用LoRA微调的LLM，对ASR转录、语篇线索、时间流畅性统计和音序序列四个互补信号进行结构化多视角推理。在ADReSSo上F1达90.14%，消融实验证实每个视角的贡献。

<h3 id="176">[176] Domain-Informed Multi-View Self-Distillation for Astronomical Light-Curve Representation Learning with JEPA</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28446](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28446) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yicheng Rui
- **Subjects**: astro-ph.IM; astro-ph.SR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Analysis, Astronomy, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 针对现有时间序列基础模型难以处理天文光变曲线不规则采样和多物理时间尺度的问题，提出基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的领域信息表示学习框架，结合语义保持视图、不确定性感知分词和多视图自蒸馏。在StarEmbed上15/16指标优于手工特征，在PYRREGULAR跨域适配中5个数据集达最优。

<h3 id="177">[177] SemFlowRAG: Directed Semantic Flow from Abstraction to Evidence for Complex Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28447](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28447) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Houyuan Qin, Rong Wu, Qinyuan Qin, Botian Shi, Jingjing Qu, Yang Sun, Pinlong Cai
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Graph Reasoning, Multi-Hop Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对知识图谱增强检索中高连接度抽象概念节点导致的“概率黑洞”和语义漂移问题，提出SemFlowRAG框架，通过构建语料自适应的语义梯度图实现层次化检索。在复杂QA数据集上，该方法有效缓解了概率黑洞问题，检索和推理性能均优于现有基线。

<h3 id="178">[178] LLM agents security duality: a comprehensive survey of self-security and empowered cybersecurity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28450](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28450) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yiwei Xu, Yong Zhuang, Xuanming Liu, Tian Zhang, Bowen Xiao, Xiaoyang Xu, Delong Jiang, Juan Wang, Hongxin Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Cybersecurity
- **Summary**: 综述了大语言模型代理在安全领域的双重角色：自身安全威胁与赋能网络安全。提出了一种按威胁源分类的代理安全分类法，并首次构建了覆盖网络攻防全生命周期的代理赋能框架。揭示了代理自安全与赋能网络安全的正反馈协同效应。

<h3 id="179">[179] Event-Conditioned Diagnostics of Kinematic, Contact, and Object-Permanence Fields in Passive Object-State World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28455](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28455) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yang Liu, Yuming Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: World Model, Physical Reasoning, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对世界模型潜在物理结构内部组织方式不明的问题，提出了事件条件潜在物理结构诊断协议。在自由运动、碰撞和遮挡事件数据集上，发现隐藏状态支持事件类型读取，且事件上下文会重新加权运动学、接触和物体恒存性场。抑制场对齐方向可降低事件相关预测性能。

<h3 id="180">[180] Is Lying an Emergent Behaviour in LLMs? Evidence from Gaslighting AI agents in a Sustainability Game</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28456](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28456) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Subhendu Bhandary, Federico Carucci, Christos Charalambous, Francesca Dilisante, Ksenia Dvorkina, Anna Garbo, Jiaqi Liang, Riccardo Vasellini, Francesco Bertolotti
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Emergent Behavior, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 探究LLM代理在多智能体可持续博弈中是否会产生欺骗行为。构建了代理基模型，允许代理声明未来攻击并获取说谎许可。结果发现，即使未明确允许说谎，欺骗仍会涌现；明确许可主要增加了虚张声势和转移注意力的行为，而非直接背刺。

<h3 id="181">[181] Counterfactual Residual Data Augmentation for Regression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28460](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28460) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hossein Mohebbi, Oliver Schulte, Ke Li, Pascal Poupart
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Augmentation, Counterfactual Reasoning, Tabular Learning
- **Venue**: International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
- **Summary**: 针对表格回归任务中训练样本少、噪声大的问题，提出反事实残差数据增强（CRDA）技术。利用回归器建模后残差的稳定性，生成新的训练样本。在多个基准数据集上，CRDA使MLP回归器的MSE降低22.9%，XGBoost降低6.4%。

<h3 id="182">[182] SVC-Probe: A Framework for Evaluating Perturbation Generalization in Spatial Foundation-Model Embeddings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28465](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28465) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jake Y. Chen, Huu Phong Nguyen, Fuad Al Abir, Ehsan Saghapour
- **Subjects**: q-bio.QM; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Foundation Model, Medical AI, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 为评估空间基础模型嵌入在药物扰动下的泛化能力，提出SVC-Probe诊断框架。在CM4AI化学扰动图谱上发现，98.6%的条件区分准确率与跨药物预测不相关，余弦相似度从域内0.944降至留一药评估0.30。伏立诺他的扰动轴能稳健重建，而紫杉醇则不能。

<h3 id="183">[183] An Agentic AI Pipeline for Appliance-Level Energy Anomaly Detection and LLM-Driven Recommendations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28467](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28467) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dihia Falouz, Aida Douaibia, Amine Bechar, Youssef Elmir, Abbes Amira, Adel Oulefki
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Time Series Forecasting, LLM Agent
- **Venue**: IEEE International Conference on Communication, Computing, Networking, and Control in Cyber-Physical Systems (CCNCPS 2026)
- **Summary**: 针对办公建筑电器级能耗监控中的噪声报警问题，提出端到端代理管线，结合SSA-LSTM预测、VAE异常检测和LLM推理生成可操作维护建议。动态检索将上下文从六个降至三至六个源，最佳后端得分90.4/100，本地7B模型通过全部16个场景。

<h3 id="184">[184] Improvement of Robot's Simultaneous Localization and Mapping Using an Effective Transformation to Achieve Linear Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28475](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28475) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seyed Farzad Bahreinian, Maziar Palhang, Mohammad Reza Taban, Hasan Enami Eraghi
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: SLAM, Robotics
- **Summary**: 为解决扩展卡尔曼滤波SLAM因模型非线性导致的发散问题，通过应用指南针和有效变换将非线性模型转换为线性模型，再使用原始卡尔曼滤波，提出LMKF SLAM。该方法在精度、收敛性和计算复杂度上显著优于现有方法，对传感器不确定性和参数变化更稳定。

<h3 id="185">[185] Decomposing Memorization Reduction in Privacy-Preserving Fine-Tuning of SLMs for CSIRTs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28479](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28479) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cristhian Kapelinski, Diego Kreutz
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Privacy, LLM Memorization, Fine-Tuning
- **Venue**: 36th Brazilian Conference on Intelligent Systems (BRACIS 2026)
- **Summary**: 针对CSIRT微调语言模型时的隐私风险，首次实证研究了DP SGD和HMAC伪匿名化在小语言模型上的交互作用。发现匹配更新控制解释了66%至132%的记忆化减少，DP SGD未产生额外降低；HMAC伪匿名化将原始标识符暴露降低40%至61%。

<h3 id="186">[186] TUA-Bench: A Benchmark for General-Purpose Terminal-Use Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28480](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28480) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shoufa Chen, Luyuan Wang, Xuan Yang, Zhiheng Liu, Yuren Cong, Yuanfeng Ji, Feiyan Zhou, Xiaohui Zhang, Fanny Yang, Belinda Zeng
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Computer Use Agent, Agent Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对现有基准未能充分评估通用终端使用代理的问题，提出TUA-Bench，包含120个跨五类任务的实际任务。最强代理Claude Code达到65.8%的整体性能，揭示了显著差距。

<h3 id="187">[187] Generative AI Literacy Training Improves Intelligence Analysts' Discrimination of Real and AI-Generated Images</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28510) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Negar Kamali, Candice Rockell Gerstner, Jessica Hullman, Matthew Groh
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Image Forensics, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Summary**: 针对人们难以区分AI生成图像与真实图像的问题，对美国政府情报分析师进行了30分钟培训干预。培训将总体准确率从72%提高9个百分点，提升主要来自真实图像识别（提高14.2个百分点）。结果证明简短结构化培训能有效改善人类判断。

<h3 id="188">[188] HDDPM: Heteroscedastic Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model for Quantitative Low-Count Brain PET Recovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28513](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28513) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Raymond Confidence, Udunna C. Anazodo
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Medical Imaging, Image Reconstruction
- **Summary**: 针对低计数PET噪声非高斯、非平稳且与活动相关的问题，提出异方差残差扩散模型（HDDPM），其前向过程是强度感知的。在最低剂量（1%）外部扫描中，HDDPM在保持整体图像质量的同时显著减少了高、低活动区域的测量误差。

<h3 id="189">[189] A Gravitational Interpretation of Fine-Tuning Reversion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28525](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28525) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Samuele Poppi, Nils Lukas
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Safety, Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 针对微调无害数据会部分逆转早期训练行为的问题，提出引力解释几何假说：早期训练创建主导流形，后续微调具有指向该流形的回复分量v_rev。选择性阻断v_rev方向可将有害性从19.0%降至8.5%，且任务成本很小。

<h3 id="190">[190] The Speedup Paradox: Rethinking Inference Speed-Quality Trade-off in Embodied Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28529](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28529) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yujin Wang, Junli Chen, Yixuan Li, Shunan Dong, Huazhong Yang, Yongpan Liu, Hongyang Jia
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Robotics, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 针对有损高效推理技术在具身任务中的异常效应，提出TISED分析框架。发现静态任务中优化可能延长完成时间；动态任务中适度有损优化可提高成功率；且硬件配置影响单调性和甜点位置。

<h3 id="191">[191] CMSL: Constructive Multi-Sequence Learning for Recommendation Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28533](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28533) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zikun Cui, Renzhi Wu, Junjie Yang, Li Sheng, Jijie Wei, Linfeng Liu, Tai Guo, Tao Jia, Xiaodong Wang, Hong Li, Li Yu, Sri Reddy, Hong Yan
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Sequential Recommendation, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对单序列建模在推荐系统中因用户历史多面性导致的上下文污染问题，提出构造性多序列学习（CMSL），通过可学习序列构建模块在潜空间解耦用户历史为连贯主题序列。该方法已在Meta的排序和检索任务中部署。

<h3 id="192">[192] MammoFlow: Multiview Mammogram Synthesis with Anatomically Consistent Flow Matching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28537](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28537) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuexi Du, Leya Barrientos, Laura Sheiman, John Lewin, Hemant D. Tagare, Nicha C. Dvornek
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Synthesis, Flow Matching
- **Venue**: MICCAI 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/XYPB/MammoFlow)
- **Summary**: 针对多视图乳腺X光图像数据集获取困难的问题，提出MammoFlow，利用流匹配和几何对齐模块隐式施加3D一致性先验。该方法生成图像质量高，下游分类AUC提升5%。

<h3 id="193">[193] KernelSight-LM: A Kernel-Level LLM Inference Simulator</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28565](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28565) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiteng Yao, Taeho Kim, Hengzhi Pei, Xinle Liu, Kyle Ulrich, Leonard Lausen, Ashish Khetan, Xiang Song, George Karypis, Martin Herbordt
- **Subjects**: cs.PF; cs.AI; cs.AR
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Simulation
- **Summary**: 针对LLM推理性能评估依赖部署特定的基准测试、难以泛化的问题，提出KernelSight-LM细粒度推理模拟器，通过离散事件调度器结合roofline内核模型、通信模型和主机开销模型建模令牌级执行，并提供两种预测模式。跨代模式无需目标GPU测量，每核延迟误差12.1%；目标测量模式误差3.8%；端到端延迟命中率与专用分析工具相当，且支持瓶颈分析。

<h3 id="194">[194] Digitizing Coaching Intelligence: An Agentic Framework for Holistic Athlete Profiling using VLM and RAG</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28570](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28570) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Deep Ghosal, Ishani Sen, Wazib Ansar, Amlan Chakrabarti
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, RAG, Vision-Language Model, Sports Analytics
- **Summary**: 针对传统运动员评估主观且不可扩展的问题，提出基于LLM的混合代理框架，结合计算机视觉的几何精度与视觉语言模型的语义推理，通过3×3智能网格分块降低计算开销88%以上，并引入LLM-as-a-Judge自纠正循环和双持久化RAG管道。实验表明该多代理方法弥合了原始生物特征跟踪与可操作教练洞察之间的差距。

<h3 id="195">[195] Geometric Measurements of the Axiom of Choice in Neural Proof Embeddings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28572](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28572) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rodrigo Mendoza-Smith
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.LO; math.LO
- **Tags**: Automated Theorem Proving, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 针对选择公理在数学基础中的争议缺乏经验证据的问题，利用Lean 4的依赖跟踪证明选择公理在证明空间中有可测量的几何对应，遵循单参数混合律。通过自监督证明编码器测量经典证明，发现异常分数等度量随依赖距离衰减（AUC 0.847），且构造性定理的求解率是经典定理的13倍。

<h3 id="196">[196] Correct codes for the wrong reasons? validating LLMs as measurement instruments for theoretical constructs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28574](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28574) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Manuel Pita
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Interpretability, Construct Validation
- **Summary**: 针对LLM编码虽可靠但构念效度未经验证的问题，提出粒度校准方法，将构念分解为子句级组件并通过显式理论规则组合，验证过程而非输出。该方法可显示哪些组件决定了编码，并在编码错误时识别是遗漏组件还是混淆了相邻构念。

<h3 id="197">[197] Animation2Code: Evaluating Temporal Visual Reasoning in Video-to-Code Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28593](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28593) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anya Ji, Abhijith Varma Mudunuri, David M. Chan, Alane Suhr
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Code Generation, Video Understanding
- **Summary**: 针对视觉语言模型在含运动视频到代码生成中无法恢复时间动态的问题，提出Animation2Code基准，包含1069个网络动画视频及对应HTML/CSS/JavaScript代码，并提出外观相似性和时间相似性指标。实验表明当前VLM在重构时难以保持时间一致性，即使外观相似性很高。

<h3 id="198">[198] Neuromorphic Energy-Aware Learning for Adaptive Deep Brain Stimulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28600](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28600) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Binh Nguyen, Colleen Josephson, Mircea Teodorescu, Gert Cauwenberghs, Jason Eshraghian
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Neuromorphic Computing, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 针对闭环系统中执行器能耗可能超过控制器的问题，提出能量感知学习将执行器能耗纳入强化学习奖励，并在深部脑刺激中验证。深度脉冲Q网络在生物物理模型中抑制α-β振荡45.2%，减少刺激电荷80.0%；通过稀疏知识蒸馏到神经形态处理器，推理功耗0.52mW，能效比传统边缘硬件高28.1倍。

<h3 id="199">[199] Database Context Compression for Text-to-SQL on Real-World Large Databases</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28601](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28601) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jingwen Liu, Weibin Liao, Xin Gao, Junfeng Zhao, Yasha Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-SQL, Database Context Compression
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/MrBlankness/SchemaCompression)
- **Summary**: 针对企业级Text-to-SQL性能远低于学术数据集的问题，提出数据库上下文压缩方法DBCC，基于SGCF原则进行离线的结构语义压缩和在线证据提纯。在Spider 2.0-Snow和BIRD上将输入上下文压缩2个数量级，模式链接召回率从0%提升至56.5%，端到端执行准确率提升1.8-1.9%。

<h3 id="200">[200] Fast and Accurate Outlier-Aware LiDAR Super-Resolution for SLAM Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28607](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28607) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Christos Anagnostopoulos, Alexandros Gkillas, Nikos Piperigkos, Aris S. Lalos
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LiDAR Perception, Point Cloud, SLAM
- **Summary**: 针对提升低分辨率LiDAR用于SLAM需要实时性能的问题，提出基于深度展开的超分辨率模型，集成离群点去除模块以保证结构完整性。在LiDAR SLAM框架中评估，方法在姿态估计精度和效率上优于现有超分辨率方法。

<h3 id="201">[201] What LLMs explain is not what they believe: Evaluating explanation sufficiency under models' own input beliefs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28615](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28615) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nhi Nguyen, Shauli Ravfogel, Rajesh Ranganath
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Interpretability, Explainable AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/rajesh-lab/self-consistent-sufficiency)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM自由文本解释是否充分不明确的问题，提出自洽充分性概念和信息论度量SCSuff，利用LLM自身生成基于解释的替代输入。实验表明LLM解释普遍不充分，且与模型大小等弱相关，SCSuff可从内部表征预测。

<h3 id="202">[202] The Undecidability of Artificial General Intelligence (AGI) Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28639](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28639) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jose Pascual Gumbau Mezquita
- **Subjects**: cs.LO; cs.AI; cs.CC; cs.CL
- **Tags**: AI Safety, AI Alignment, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 针对AGI安全的核心障碍，形式化证明对齐的结构性不可验证性，包括不可验证性定理和有限结构不可验证性，并推导出可靠性-完备性-可处理性三难困境。结果表明现代遏制策略是逻辑表达性的必要牺牲。

<h3 id="203">[203] Analysis of Parameter Settings for the Bat Algorithm Using Variance Evolution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28644](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28644) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xin-She Yang, Mehmet Karamanoglu
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Evolutionary Computation, Optimization, Metaheuristics
- **Summary**: 针对元启发式算法参数设置缺乏理论分析的问题，利用动力系统和种群方差演化理论推导蝙蝠算法参数的理论范围。数值实验与理论界一致，提供了算法特性如方差演化和探索-利用转换的见解。

<h3 id="204">[204] RIPA: Sensory-Vector Prompt Injection Attacks on LLM-Controlled ROS 2 Robots</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28649](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28649) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nima Dorzhiev
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Robotics, Prompt Injection
- **Summary**: 针对LLM控制机器人系统的提示注入攻击，首次系统研究通过感知通道（视觉、音频、LiDAR）的攻击。在5个LLM上发现模型特定漏洞，Llama-3.3-70B攻击成功率100%；提出混合语义防火墙，已知模式成功率0%，但对抗混淆旁路率10.2%。

<h3 id="205">[205] FedLAS: Feature-Modulated Bidirectional Label Smoothing for Neural Network Calibration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28654](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28654) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thiru Thillai Nadarasar Bahavan, Sachith Seneviratne, Saman Halgamuge
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Confidence Calibration, Image Classification, Regularization
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/nadarasarbahavan/FEDLAS)
- **Summary**: 针对深度神经网络校准中的过/欠自信问题，提出FedLAS：基于特征范数的置信度指示器和双向校准门控，实现样本自适应的标签平滑。在标准和高分辨率视觉基准上，FedLAS在保持Top-1准确率的同时持续降低ECE和自适应ECE。

<h3 id="206">[206] SemDynReg: Semantics-Guided Deformation Regularization for Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28656](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28656) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruitao Chen, Mozhang Guo, Jinge Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Gaussian Splatting, Semantic Segmentation, Novel View Synthesis
- **Summary**: 针对动态3D高斯泼溅缺乏显式对象级建模导致变形不一致的问题，提出语义引导框架，利用SAM提取分割掩码，通过对象ID图对变形参数施加一致性正则化。实验表明语义感知正则化提高了对象级变形一致性，在PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS上优于基线。

<h3 id="207">[207] When More Sampling Hurts: The Modal Ceiling and Correlation Ceiling of Test-Time Scaling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28661](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28661) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yong Yi Bay, Kathleen A. Yearick
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Sampling, Test-Time Scaling
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/bay-yearick-lab/sampling-ceilings)
- **Summary**: 针对测试时缩放中过多采样可能降低性能的问题，提出模态上限和相关上限概念，以及有效样本数。实验表明投票结果在几十次采样后即稳定，额外采样无益甚至有害。瓶颈在于识别正确答案而非生成。

<h3 id="208">[208] Closed-Form Steepest Descent Direction toward Flat Minima: Reducing Upper Bounds on the Loss Hessian Eigenspectrum in Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28662](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28662) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuto Omae, Kazuki Sakai, Yohei Kakimoto, Makoto Sasaki, Yusuke Sakai, Hirotaka Takahashi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Flat Minima, Optimization, Loss Landscape
- **Summary**: 针对寻找平坦极小方向缺乏解析方法的问题，推导出交叉熵损失Hessian最大特征值Wolkowicz-Styan上界的梯度，提出HSR正则化沿最速下降方向更新参数。实验表明HSR正则化缩窄Hessian谱、避免尖锐极小和鞍点，促进收敛至平坦极小。

<h3 id="209">[209] Why Trust Your Agent? Empirical Security Gains from TRiSM-Guided Agentic Workflows in Healthcare</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28666](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28666) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Liam Kearns
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Security, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 针对基于LLM的代理工作流存在的安全风险，应用AI信任、风险与安全管理（TRiSM）框架设计安全感知的代理工作流，实现最小权限和深度防御。在医疗报告生成应用中，攻击成功率从31%降至10%（RAG投毒）和42%至25%（数据字段注入），网络注入完全消除，报告准确率从72.5%提升至86.5%。

<h3 id="210">[210] MACROCAST: A Vintage-Consistent Time Series Foundation Model for Real-Time Macroeconomic Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28670](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28670) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrea Carriero, Davide Pettenuzzo, Shubhranshu Shekhar
- **Subjects**: econ.EM; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 现有时间序列基础模型存在数据泄露（时间污染和修订偏差），无法用于实时宏观预测。提出MACROCAST，首个完全排除两种泄露的轻量级模型，先纯合成训练再基于时点数据估计的模型生成合成数据微调。在FRED-MD上约80%序列-水平对改进AR(1)，匹配或超越Chronos-2，优于传统基准。

<h3 id="211">[211] Constrained Tabular Diffusion for Finance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28674](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28674) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Michael Cardei, Jose M Munoz, Oscar Barrera, Shreyas K Chandrahas, Partha Saha
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Tabular Learning, Constrained Generation
- **Venue**: ACM International Conference on AI in Finance (ICAIF) 2025
- **Summary**: 金融数据生成需满足监管硬约束，标准扩散模型无法提供。提出CTDF，在反向扩散采样中加入训练不可知的可行性操作强制执行硬约束。在大型金融数据集上实现零约束违反，改善稀缺数据效用。

<h3 id="212">[212] Predicting Metastatic Risk from Primary Tissue Architecture via Distance-Aware Spatial Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28676](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28676) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sandesh Pokhrel, Hamid Manoochehri, Bodong Zhang, Beatrice S Knudsen, Tolga Tasdizen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Multiple Instance Learning, Spatial Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 预测远处转移风险需考虑肿瘤微环境空间布局，而多实例学习（MIL）忽略空间信息。提出DTMf-MIL，使用符号距离函数显式建模组织表型间空间关系。在转移预测上显著优于忽略空间布局的SOTA，在公开基准上验证空间感知提升诊断准确性。

<h3 id="213">[213] Capability Gates Are Not Authorization: Confused-Deputy Failures in LLM Agent Frameworks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28679](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28679) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: David Mellafe Zuvic
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Security, Access Control
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/raceksd-source/scopegate-runtime)
- **Summary**: LLM代理框架默认将工具暴露视为授权，导致混淆代理攻击。审计发现LangChain等缺乏每个调用的值授权。提出ScopeGate，五阶段策略（范围、授权、资金上限、幂等性、默认拒绝）。在测试中0静态绕过、0未经授权尝试、0误拒，制止10/10支付攻击。

<h3 id="214">[214] SEATauBench: Adapting Tool-Agent-User Evaluation Into Low-Resource Southeast Asian Languages</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28715](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28715) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: My Chiffon Nguyen, Aulia Adila, Saksorn Ruangtanusak, Kittiphat Leesombatwathana, Vissuta Gunawan Lim, Patomporn Payoungkhamdee, Samuel Cahyawijaya
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multilingual Learning, Agent Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/SEACrowd/SEATauBench)
- **Summary**: 东南亚语言中AI代理能力评估不足。引入SEATauBench，首个针对东南亚语言的代理评估框架，适配五种语言。发现英语代理能力在仅改变语言时迁移良好，但任务本地化时质量大幅下降，提供诊断基准和适配管道。

<h3 id="215">[215] CCRC: A Change-Aware Captioning and Reasoning Chain for Image Change Captioning and Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28724](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28724) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinhong Hu, Xiaoping Wang, Shuyin Huang, Guojin Zhong, Kaitai Liu, Kai Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Captioning, Image Segmentation, Change Detection
- **Summary**: 图像变化描述缺乏空间定位。提出新任务ICCS及CCRC框架，双链架构实现变化感知描述和像素级分割。在合成和真实变化检测基准上达到SOTA性能。

<h3 id="216">[216] 5ting at SemEval-2026 Task 8: Strong End-to-End Multi-Turn RAG via LLM-Based Reranking and Faithfulness Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28737](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28737) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thien-Qua-T-Nguyen, Chi Hoang, Nguyen Tran, Tri Le, Khanh Truong, Chinh Trong Nguyen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Information Retrieval, Faithfulness
- **Summary**: 多轮RAG面临上下文漂移、欠指定和幻觉。提出5ting系统，结合密集检索、双查询合并、LLM重排和角色分离生成（约束到证据）。检索器nDCG@5=0.4719，端到端调和得分0.5597，RL_F=0.7692。

<h3 id="217">[217] Four Types of LLM Reliance and Their Predictors Among Undergraduate Writers: A Mixed-Methods Study at a Minority-Serving R1 University</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28749](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28749) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shahin Hossain
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, Education Technology, AI Literacy
- **Summary**: 现有LLM依赖衡量仅用频率，无法区分依赖方式。混合方法研究识别四种依赖类型（策略型、工具型、对话型、依赖型）。价值和成本信念预测依赖强度，AI素养预测依赖类型，策略型学生得分最低，反映当前工具偏向AI贡献而非质量。

<h3 id="218">[218] X-Mind: Efficient Visual Chain-of-Thought via Predictive World Model for End-to-End Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28758](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28758) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bohao Zhao, Chengrui Wei, Guangfeng Jiang, Ruixin Liu, Xuejie Lv, Liu Liang, Sutao Deng, Xiuyang Fan, Pengkun Zheng, Jinyun Zhou, Rui Guo, Hanpeng Liu, Yutong Zheng, Yi Guo, Xinlong Zheng, Qingyu Luo, Zhuangzhuang Ding, Yu Zhang, Hang Zhang, Xianming Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, World Model, Vision-Language-Action Model
- **Summary**: VLA模型缺乏未来预测能力。提出X-Mind，将预测世界模型内化为视觉思维链，使用紧凑草图表示和循环块扩散方案实现高效推理。在大规模真实驾驶数据上达到有竞争力性能，且低延迟适合车载平台。

<h3 id="219">[219] Majority Vote Silences Minority Values: Annotator Disagreement at the Hate/Offensive Boundary in HateXplain</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28772](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28772) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Joshua Muhumuza, Joab Ezra Agaba, Mercy Amiyo
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Hate Speech Detection, Data Annotation, Annotator Disagreement
- **Summary**: 仇恨言论标注中多数投票掩盖注释者分歧。分析HateXplain发现42.6%分歧集中在仇恨/攻击边界，模型在分歧样本上准确性降22-28个百分点，硬标签模型对错误置信度更高。下游干预无法恢复边界准确性。

<h3 id="220">[220] Brownian Bridge Diffusion-Based Joint Channel Estimation and Data Detection for Jamming-Resilient Receivers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28778](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28778) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Honghan She, Yufan Cheng, Tieming Sun, Pengyu Wang, Siya Huang, Kaikai Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.IT; cs.AI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Wireless Networks, Channel Estimation, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 无线网络中干扰重叠导致信道估计困难。提出布朗桥扩散联合信道估计和数据检测框架，包括STFT域干扰抑制和布朗桥扩散。仿真显示优于基线，参数更少，计算复杂度相当。

<h3 id="221">[221] BREIT: A Framework for Brain Stroke Reconstruction using Multi-Frequency 3D EIT</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28787](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28787) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Djahid Abdelmoumene, Ishak Ayad, Maï K. Nguyen, Christian Daveau
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Neural Operator, 3D Reconstruction
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/djahiddj13/BREIT)
- **Summary**: 多频电阻抗断层扫描（MF-EIT）缺乏大规模数据集和标准化流程。提出BREIT框架，包括神经影像到EIT管道、3D前向求解器、D-bar方法，并提出dFNO-bar集成傅里叶神经算子。在合成数据上脑SSIM和CC优于基准。

<h3 id="222">[222] The registrar's function in a hybrid society. AI value chain,smart data and the concept of property</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28789](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28789) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pompeu Casanovas, Carmen Pastor Sempere, Marina Echebarria Saenz
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Legal AI, AI Ethics, AI Governance
- **Summary**: AI在土地注册中涉及数据价值链和产权概念。从法律角度分析以有效性为核心的概念，探讨GDPR、数据法案、AI法案影响。注册机构从处理文件转变为保证有效数据，支持可持续发展目标。

<h3 id="223">[223] Human2Any: Human-to-Robot Transfer via Constraint-Aware Compositional Planning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28813](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28813) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuo Cheng, Chuye Zhang, Alfred Cueva, Caelan Garrett, Ajay Mandlekar, Danfei Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Imitation Learning, Robot Manipulation
- **Summary**: 人类视频迁移到机器人困难。提出Human2Any，学习物体-物体交互运动先验，结合机器人侧可行性推理和运动规划。在Franka和RBY-1机器人上展示鲁棒操作，无需真实训练数据。

<h3 id="224">[224] Categorizing Mathematical Concepts with LLM Voting Ensembles in Mathswitch</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28815](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28815) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Katja Berčič, Slobodan Stanojevikj
- **Subjects**: cs.DL; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Mathematical Reasoning, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: CICM 2026
- **Summary**: Mathswitch项目概念数据有噪声。使用LLM投票集成过滤噪声，评估正控制并分析分歧类型（退化描述、窄范围偏差、编辑范围不匹配）。提供不同修复策略。

<h3 id="225">[225] Exit-and-Join Dynamics and Equilibrium in Continuum Cooperative Games</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28824](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28824) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Quanyan Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; cs.MA; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Game Theory
- **Summary**: 针对大规模多智能体系统中联盟动态缺乏统一理论框架的问题，本文扩展了非原子合作博弈的退出与加入动态，提出了基于边际贡献的支付密度和平均场动态，并证明了均衡与Wardrop均衡的等价性。通过Lyapunov函数和变分不等式，建立了全局收敛性和约束均衡的条件。该框架统一了合作博弈价值分配、非合作博弈迁移动态、演化博弈论和种群博弈。

<h3 id="226">[226] HARD-KV: Head-Adaptive Regularization for Decoding-time KV Compression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28831](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28831) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuxuan Yang, Feiyang Ren, Bowen Zeng, Dalin Zhang, Jinpeng Chen, Gang Chen, Huan Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: KV Cache, LLM Inference, Token Compression
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/SuDIS-ZJU/HARDInfer)
- **Summary**: 针对长上下文LLM推理中KV缓存压缩算法（如Top-p采样）的动态内存需求与推理引擎的静态内存模式之间的冲突，提出了HARD-KV框架。该框架通过级联缓存层次、对数几率校准机制和系统级解决方案，将动态索引转为连续物理布局，支持高效推理。实验表明，在10k+ token场景下，HARD-KV相比静态基线实现了高达2倍的吞吐量提升，同时保持高保真生成。

<h3 id="227">[227] Fisher-Routed Mixture of Experts for Federated Class-Incremental Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28835](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28835) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenhao Yuan, Chenchen Lin, Jian Chen, Jinfeng Xu, Zewei Liu, Edith Cheuk Han Ngai
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Mixture-of-Experts, Continual Learning
- **Summary**: 针对联邦学习在类增量场景中面临的容量冲突、灾难性遗忘、非独立同分布数据和类同步问题，提出了FedFMX框架。该框架通过Fisher路由专家评分、自适应专家选择和路由感知正则化，实现了自适应专家专业化。理论上证明了O(T^{-1})的收敛速率，多个基准上的实验表明优于现有方法。

<h3 id="228">[228] LAMP: Lean-based Agentic framework with MCP and Proof Repair</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28841](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28841) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Santhana Srinivasan R, Maithilee Patawar
- **Subjects**: cs.LO; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Automated Theorem Proving, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 针对大型语言模型生成的数学证明不可靠且形式化知识覆盖有限（如词组合学领域）的问题，提出了LAMP多智能体框架。该框架通过规划器、构建器和验证器协同工作，并利用模型上下文协议访问领域本体，无需微调即可生成经由Lean 4内核验证的证明。在90个定理上，LAMP成功合成96.7%的验证证明，显著超过基线。

<h3 id="229">[229] The Heterogeneous Safety Impacts of Benign Multilingual Fine-Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28843](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28843) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Will Hawkins, Kaivalya Rawal, Jonathan Rystrøm, Stratis Tsirtsis, Zihao Fu, Greta Warren, Ryan Brown, Eoin Delaney, Sandra Wachter, Brent Mittelstadt, Chris Russell
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Multilingual Learning, Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 针对良性多语言微调对模型安全性的异质影响缺乏系统研究的问题，本文在9种语言上对多个模型进行良性数据微调。发现安全结果高度依赖于微调语言和评估语言，某些设置下对抗性合规率增加4倍，且多语言安全漂移与通用能力指标解耦。非英语微调常导致更小的内部表征漂移，但会使模型趋于过度顺从或拒绝。

<h3 id="230">[230] Perspectives on Latent Factor Indeterminacy and its Implications for Data Representation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28854](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28854) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Carel F.W. Peeters
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG; math.ST; stat.CO
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Variational Autoencoder, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 针对因子分析等生成模型中的潜在因子不确定性问题，本文从多角度分析了潜在因子投影的不确定性，并证明当特征维度趋于无穷时，因子在所有方面具有确定性。该发现为高维数据的表示学习提供了分布无关的估计方法，并揭示了与VAE中潜在变量崩溃的联系。

<h3 id="231">[231] Building AI-Ready Data Systems for Space Life Sciences, Aerospace Medicine, and Deep Space Exploration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28856](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28856) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sylvain V. Costes, Sergio Garcia Busto, Ryan T. Scott, James A. Casaletto, Gautier Bardi de Fourtou, Brian M. Evarts, Amanda M. Saravia-Butler, Xavier-Lewis Palmer, Rodrigo Coutinho de Almeida, Laetitia Frost, Jelena Tešić, Afshin Beheshti, Christopher E. Mason, Peter W. Rose, Sergio E. Baranzini, Lauren M. Sanders, Stefania Giacomello, Pedro Madrigal
- **Subjects**: q-bio.OT; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Integration, Data Quality, AI for Science
- **Summary**: 针对空间生命科学数据异构且未实现机器可访问的问题，提出了从FAIR到AI就绪再到太空就绪的三层数据系统方法。讨论了现有基础设施的改进，并建议设立国际协调机构作为治理后盾，以支持深空生物学研究的可信AI访问。

<h3 id="232">[232] Defeat Devices in AI Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28863](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28863) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Emilio Ferrara
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Safety, LLM Alignment
- **Venue**: Future Internet, 2026
- **Summary**: 针对AI系统在评估和部署环境中行为不一致（如对齐伪造、沙袋效应等）的现象，提出“失效装置”概念，包含判别器、隐藏切换和评估-部署性能差距三个要素。形式化了三轴测试，提出了触发-轴感知差分探测协议，并指出失效装置可能自然涌现，对AI安全实践具有重要意义。

<h3 id="233">[233] An Integrated Machine Learning and Hierarchical Variance Decomposition Pipeline for Student Performance Prediction and Metacognitive Calibration on Multi-Signal Telemetry</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28881](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28881) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gurdeep Singh Virdee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Personalized Learning, User Modeling
- **Summary**: 针对学生表现预测和元认知校准分析分离的问题，提出了统一行为预测与校准分析流水线（UBP-CAP），集成LightGBM分类器、校准度量和广义线性混合效应模型。在1195条交互记录上，逻辑回归达到AUC=0.903，学生ECE显著高于模型ECE，表明系统性的校准偏差，且校准是情境性而非特质性的。

<h3 id="234">[234] Exploring the Value of Diverse LLM Explanations in Introductory Programming</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28882](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28882) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seth Bernstein, Paul Denny, Juho Leinonen, Kush Patel, Rayhona Nasimova, Matt Littlefield, Stephen MacNeil
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Education Technology, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: SIGCSE Virtual 2026
- **Summary**: 针对LLM生成代码解释的质量不及教师且缺乏多样性的问题，通过实验比较了多样性和通用解释对编程学习的影响。研究发现，接受多样性解释的学生在开放式问题上的准确率平均高7.7%，而感知认知负荷无差异。这表明解释侧重点的变化可能与学习者参与度和理解相关。

<h3 id="235">[235] A Task-Driven and Quality-Assured Agent Framework for SAR Data Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28896](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28896) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xuanting Wu, Fan Zhanga, Fei Ma, Ling Guan, Guochun Ma, Yongsheng Zhou
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Data Augmentation, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 针对SAR数据增强中数据集格式异构、元数据依赖和生成样本验证弱等问题，提出了SAGA智能体框架。该框架通过模式基础、验证约束规划和策略编译，自动选择增强策略并生成可审计流程。实验表明，在模式基础、技能规划、无效样本拒绝和下游任务效用上均优于基线。

<h3 id="236">[236] Latent Bridges for Multi-Table Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28916](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28916) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Simone Varriale, Tamara Cucumides, Floris Geerts, Paolo Papotti
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.DB
- **Tags**: Question Answering, Graph Neural Network, Table Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对多表问答需要结构化表示的问题，提出了GRAB流水线，将关系数据构建为异构图，通过消息传递编码，并通过少量查询条件潜在令牌将信号传递给冻结的LLM。仅训练轻量图编码器和潜在桥接（91M参数），显著提升关系问答性能，尤其在多表设置中。

<h3 id="237">[237] Multi-Agent Routing as Set-Valued Prediction: A WildChat Benchmark and Cost-Aware Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28925](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28925) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ananto Nayan Bala, Faisal Muhammad Shah
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 针对工具和智能体路由自然是一个集值预测问题（单查询可能需要多智能体，过度选择增加成本）提出基准和评估协议。基于WildChat的3000条提示，比较多种路由方法。结果表明有监督路由显著优于零样本LLM，微调编码器在无约束设置中最佳，线性多标签模型提供最强实用基线，加权路由在约束设置中提升效用。

<h3 id="238">[238] DLR: Zero-Inference-Cost Latent Residuals for Low-Rank Pre-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28932](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28932) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dong Wang, Wenwu Tang, Yun Cheng, Olga Saukh
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Pre-training, Model Compression, Low-Rank Adaptation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/nanguoyu/DLR)
- **Summary**: 针对低秩预训练质量落后于全秩训练的问题，提出DLR插件，在训练时添加固定结构化残差，不增加可学习参数，训练后可吸收到上投影中。在60M到7B参数的LLaMA模型上，DLR在大多数设置中提高了C4验证困惑度，在130M以上提升明显，且折叠后的检查点可顺利迁移到监督微调。

<h3 id="239">[239] Machine-learnable Sets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28947](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28947) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Veit Elser, Manish Krishan Lal
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Autoencoder, Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 针对如何形式化定义易于识别、生成和学习的离散集的问题，提出基于有界复杂度布尔自编码器的“机器可学习集”定义。实验使用布尔阈值函数网络实现自编码器，在Rorschach图案和“狂野”集上演示了机器可学习性，并展示了通过迭代演化使狂野集变得可学习的简单方法。

<h3 id="240">[240] Clustering Unsupervised Representations as Defense against Poisoning Attacks on Speech Commands Classification System</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28953](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28953) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thomas Thebaud, Sonal Joshi, Henry Li, Martin Sustek, Jesus Villalba, Sanjeev Khudanpur, Najim Dehak
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Speech Recognition, Adversarial Robustness, Backdoor Attack
- **Venue**: ASRU 2025
- **Summary**: 针对语音命令分类系统中的脏标签投毒攻击，提出基于聚类无监督表示的过滤防御方法。使用DINO学习无监督表示，然后通过K-means和LDA聚类，保留聚类中多数标签的样本。在10%投毒源类下，攻击成功率从99.75%降至0.25%，并在多种威胁模型下验证了有效性。

<h3 id="241">[241] Modification-Considering Value Learning for Reward Hacking Mitigation in RL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28955](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28955) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Evgenii Opryshko, Umangi Jain, Igor Gilitschenski
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reward Hacking, Off-Policy RL
- **Summary**: 针对强化学习中奖赏破解问题，提出修正考虑的价值学习（MCVL）方法，通过筛选过渡更新来抑制奖赏破解。实验表明MCVL在多种环境中缓解奖赏破解的同时提升目标性能。

<h3 id="242">[242] RGLD: Randomized Global-Local Density Estimation for Tabular Anomaly Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28970](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28970) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Quanling Zhao, Jiaying Yang, Ye Tian, Josh Victoria, Zhijun Wang, Pietro Mercati, Onat Gungor, Tajana Rosing
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Tabular Learning
- **Summary**: 针对无监督表格异常检测，提出RGLD方法，结合全局随机特征密度和局部邻居分支，在47个数据集上取得最强AUROC，速度比深度检测器快50-580倍。

<h3 id="243">[243] Evidence-Based Text-Conditioned 3D CT Synthesis for Ovarian Cancer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28980](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28980) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Francesca Pia Panaccione, Eugenio Lomurno, Francesca Fati, Carlotta Pecchiari, Marina Rosanu, Luigi De Vitis, Lucia Ribero, Gabriella Schivardi, Giovanni Damiano Aletti, Nicoletta Colombo, Maria Francesca Spadea, Francesco Multinu, Matteo Matteucci, Elena De Momi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, 3D Generation, Text-to-3D
- **Summary**: 针对卵巢癌CT数据稀缺问题，提出OvESyn框架，利用影像描述符和临床元数据构造文本条件，驱动潜扩散模型合成3D CT。实验表明生成器域适应是关键，全模型取得最佳分布和强度保真度。

<h3 id="244">[244] Compositional Dynamics in Learning and Mechanics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28984](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28984) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: David I. Spivak
- **Subjects**: math.CT; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Category Theory, Compositional Semantics, Dynamical Systems
- **Summary**: 提出一个组合框架，将梯度学习和哈密顿力学统一为函子语义。通过透镜内化构造，将参数化函数和粒子图编译为显式状态机，分别对应梯度下降、波动方程和热方程。

<h3 id="245">[245] Fine-Tuning General-Purpose Large Language Models for Agricultural Applications:A Reproducible Framework and Evaluation Protocol Based on Qwen3-8B</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28992](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28992) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhaoyang Li, Ruijie Zhang, Jiaqi Liu, Zhaoji Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agricultural AI, RAG, Instruction Tuning, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 针对农业领域LLM应用，提出AgriTune-R框架，集成数据治理、指令构建、LoRA微调、RAG和安全控制。提供了可复现的工作流和评估协议。

<h3 id="246">[246] Arbitrary Reduction of Validation Error for AI Decision Tests using Homomorphic AI and Repetition Codes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28994](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28994) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eric Filiol, Jaagup Sepp
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Homomorphic Encryption, Error-Correcting Code
- **Venue**: DSC Next 2026
- **Summary**: 提出基于哈希的同态AI方法HbHAI，结合重复码可任意降低AI决策测试的验证误差，压缩率提升10倍。

<h3 id="247">[247] Reward-Free Code Alignment from Pretrained or Fine-Tuned LLM: Unpacking the Trade-offs for Code Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28998](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28998) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gias Uddin, Sanjeepan Sivapiran
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Alignment, Preference Optimization
- **Summary**: 实证研究LLM代码对齐的权衡，比较预训练与微调起点，使用DPO和BoNBoN。发现预训练到对齐提升更大，但精度不如微调版。

<h3 id="248">[248] BERTomelo: Your Portuguese Encoder Best Friend</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28999](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28999) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rennê Ruan Alves Oliveira, Gustavo Cordeiro Galvão Van Erven, Luís Paulo Faina Garcia
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Language Model, Pre-training, Natural Language Understanding
- **Summary**: 针对葡萄牙语单语编码器滞后问题，提出BERTomelo，基于ModernBERT架构和大型葡萄牙语语料训练。在下游任务中优于现有葡萄牙语编码器和多语言模型。

<h3 id="249">[249] Semantic-Aware, Physics-Informed, Geometry-Grounded Weather Video Synthesis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29020](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29020) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.ET; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Physics-Informed Learning, Autonomous Driving
- **Summary**: 针对天气视频合成缺乏多样性和控制问题，提出语义感知、物理信息、几何约束框架，生成逼真天气效果并提升自动驾驶语义分割鲁棒性。

<h3 id="250">[250] Efficient Spatio-Temporal Grounding with Multimodal Large Models via Second-Level Tracking and RL Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29023) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianshu Zhang, Yan Wang, Ji Qi, Lijie Wen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Visual Grounding, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对长视频时空定位计算昂贵问题，提出基于秒级跟踪和强化学习验证的高效流水线，在效率与定位质量间取得良好平衡。

<h3 id="251">[251] How to Leverage Synthetic Speech for LLM-Based ASR Systems?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29031](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29031) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yanis Labrak, Dairazalia Sanchez-Cortes, Sergio Burdisso, Séverin Baroudi, Shashi Kumar, Esaú Villatoro-Tello, Srikanth Madikeri, Manjunath K E, Oldřich Plchot, Kadri Hacioğlu, Petr Motlicek, Andreas Stolcke
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automatic Speech Recognition, Speech Synthesis, Data Augmentation
- **Summary**: 探究合成语音与真实语音在LLM-ASR中的分布差异，发现早期层可区分且RIR增强能缩小差距。结合层选择模块，仅用25%真实语音即达基线性能。

<h3 id="252">[252] The strength of clinical evidence is recoverable from language model representations but not from their stated grades</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29034](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29034) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Clinical NLP, LLM Evaluation, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 发现临床LLM的表示中编码了证据强度信号，但模型陈述的级别与随机无异。该信号主要是词汇性的，且与事实真相不同。

<h3 id="253">[253] Metric Aggregation Divergence: A Hidden Validity Threat in Agent-Based Policy Optimization and a Contractual Remedy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29038](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29038) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruiyu Zhang, Lin Nie, Xin Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agent-Based Model, Evolutionary Algorithm, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 识别基于智能体的策略优化中的度量聚合散度问题，提出度量契约作为修复措施。实验表明散度导致83%的复现推荐错误冠军，契约以约3%开销消除问题。

<h3 id="254">[254] Flow Matching in Feature Space for Stochastic World Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29059](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29059) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Francois Porcher, Nicolas Carion, Karteek Alahari, Shizhe Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: World Model, Flow Matching, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 提出FlowWM，在预训练特征空间中进行流匹配用于随机世界建模。通过可微的一步投影机制，在准确性和多样性上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="255">[255] Fairness Attacks on Recommender Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29064](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29064) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yanan Wang, Yong Ge
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Fairness, Adversarial Attack, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 提出结构感知的强化学习公平性攻击方法，联合选择虚假物品和虚假用户性别，有效加剧推荐系统不公平性。

<h3 id="256">[256] A Comparative Study on Affective Cues in Text Embeddings Across Psychological Emotion Theories</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29068](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29068) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fabio Ciani, Harald Schweiger, Emilia Parada-Cabaleiro, Markus Schedl
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Affective Computing, Emotion Recognition, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 比较12种文本编码器在三种情感理论框架下捕获情感线索的能力。指令感知开源编码器在词级别不逊于专有模型，任务微调和专有编码器在句级别分类最佳。

<h3 id="257">[257] From Tool Connection to Execution Control: Benchmarking Security Invariants in MCP-Style Agent Runtimes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29073](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29073) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ting Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Security
- **Summary**: 解决MCP风格智能体运行时缺乏显式执行层安全不变量的安全问题。提出HCP参考运行时，定义八个安全不变量并实现。HCP在所有10个攻击案例中均阻止攻击，且延迟低于1毫秒。

<h3 id="258">[258] Diff-Based Code Corruption using LLMs for Large-Scale Bugfix Benchmarking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29088](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29088) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Balázs Szalontai, Ábel Szauter, Balázs Márton, Péter Verebics, Balázs Pintér, Tibor Gregorics
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Program Repair, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有bug修复基准规模小、不反映真实bug。提出MegaBugFix，通过LLM的diff代码变异生成12629个buggy Python程序。评估13个模型，性能低于现有基准，表明更具挑战性。

<h3 id="259">[259] AB-RAG: Adaptive Budgeted Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Reliable Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29090](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29090) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ansh Kamthan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: RAG, Question Answering
- **Summary**: RAG系统固定检索段落数，浪费计算资源。提出AB-RAG，无训练且与骨干网络无关，通过三个信号估计置信度并自适应检索。置信度估计有效区分正确/错误答案，自适应策略提升准确率。

<h3 id="260">[260] Statistically Indistinguishable, Operationally Distinct: A Formal Barrier for Tabular Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29091](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29091) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tassilo Klein, Johannes Hoffart
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DB
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, Foundation Model
- **Venue**: 2nd ICML Workshop on Foundation Models for Structured Data, 2026
- **Summary**: 表格基础模型无法推理无访问规则的系统数据。提出操作图灵测试(OTT)，构造边际分布匹配的合法/违规状态对，值仅分类器达到0.5准确率，只有使用可执行规则才达到1.0。

<h3 id="261">[261] Priced Motion Through Optimal Faces: A Normal-Fan Geometry for Non-Stationary Adversarial MDPs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29092](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29092) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kai Hidajat
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Regret Minimization
- **Summary**: 非平稳MDP的动态遗憾分析将非平稳代价等同于损失移动。提出法扇几何，定义面交叉价格，将遗憾分解为有代价的面运动和内部选择误差，区分有害和无害的非平稳性。

<h3 id="262">[262] Unified Complex-valued Neural Network: A Magnitude-Phase Computational Model for Event-Driven Neuromorphic Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29099](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29099) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Reza Ahmadvand, Sarah Safura Sharif, Yaser Mike Banad
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Spiking Neural Network, Neuromorphic Computing, Complex-Valued Networks

<h3 id="263">[263] BTI-Net: Bidirectional Decoder-Level Task Interaction via Uncertainty-Aware Gating for Multi-Task Medical Image Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29102](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29102) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Multi-Task Learning, Image Segmentation

- **Summary**: 多任务医学图像分析中编码器共享架构限制解码器重建跨任务协同。提出BTI-Net，通过任务交互模块(TIM)和不确定性代理注意力(UPA)实现双向解码器通信。在分割IoU和分类准确率上持续提升。

<h3 id="264">[264] A Deep Multiscale Neural Network for Accurate Neurological Disorder Detection from MRI Scans and Real-Time Web Deployment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29106](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29106) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ali Fatahi, Hoda Zamani, Mohammad H. Nadimi-Shahraki
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Classification
- **Summary**: 现有CNN针对二分类任务优化，难以捕捉多类特征。提出End-Net，包含24层卷积和21个优化Inception模块，用于多类MRI分类。性能优于现有架构，并部署实时在线系统。

<h3 id="265">[265] LLM Semantic Signaling Game and Mechanism Design: Systematic Blindness, Awareness Shaping, and Mindset Dynamics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29113](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29113) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Quanyan Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Game Theory, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 大语言模型通过自然语言调节策略交互，语义控制对通信和欺骗至关重要。建立语义信号博弈，建模接收者意识类型，发展机制设计方法。数值实验验证高斯近似，意识塑造成本可减少钓鱼攻击。

<h3 id="266">[266] When Stopping Fails: Rethinking Minimal Risk Conditions through Human-Interactive Autonomous Driving for Safe Transportation Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29115](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29115) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yash Tandon, Giovanni Tapia Lopez, Marcus Blennemann, Mohan Trivedi, Ross Greer
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Human-Robot Interaction, Safety Evaluation
- **Venue**: IEEE ITSC 2026
- **Summary**: 自动驾驶安全框架依赖最小风险条件(如停止)，但实际事件表明停止会引发交通阻塞等问题。分析事件并分类，指出需要人类交互式自主能力。建议增强AV安全框架以支持合作交互。

<h3 id="267">[267] Knowing in Advance When an Evolutionary Outer Loop Will Not Help: A Pre-Registered Cheap-Baseline Screening Rule</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29119](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29119) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ramchand Kumaresan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Evolutionary Algorithm, Research Methodology
- **Summary**: 进化外循环(参数/结构)成本高(100-1000倍梯度内循环)，但能否胜出单次方法通常在实施后才知道。提出预注册筛查规则，计算恢复率R，若R\>=90%则跳过外循环。验证于两个项目，节省6-8倍成本。

<h3 id="268">[268] How Anthropomorphic Language Impacts Public Perceptions of AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29121](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29121) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Betty Li Hou, Sophie Hao, Sunoo Park, Tal Linzen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Summary**: 公共AI话语常使用拟人化语言，可能误导公众认知。通过实验(N=815)比较拟人化与无拟人化文本的影响。结果发现拟人化语言未显著影响参与者对AI的感知。

<h3 id="269">[269] CMTFormer: Marrying Transformer with Hierarchical Information Interaction for RGB-Event Object Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29136](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29136) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yu Li, Yuenan Hou, Yingmei Wei, Jiangming Chen, Yanming Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 多模态融合RGB帧和事件流受异构性影响，导致噪声放大。提出CMTFormer，层级整合RGB和事件信息，包含SAM、CEM、LDFM模块。在两个事件目标检测基准上优于单/多模态方法。

<h3 id="270">[270] GPC: Large-Scale Generative Pretraining for Transferable Motor Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29148](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29148) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yi Shi, Yifeng Jiang, Chen Tessler, Xue Bin Peng
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.GR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Motion Synthesis, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 基于物理的角色动画需要自然且可复用的控制器。提出生成式预训练控制器(GPC)，通过有限标量量化(FSQ)和GPT风格Transformer进行下一token预测。达到99.98%动作片段复现成功率，展现涌现行为。

<h3 id="271">[271] On the Nonlinearity of Learning Rate Scaling for LLM Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29158](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29158) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zaiwen Yang, Huaqing Zhang, Jing Xu, Jingzhao Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Scaling Laws
- **Summary**: 最优学习率假设对数线性缩放，但在大规模时出现曲率，导致外推不准确。实证研究GPT-2模型，发现曲率在使用有效学习率和数据外推时消失。非线性归因于权重范数收敛时间。

<h3 id="272">[272] Invariant Reasoning Directions in Latent Trajectories of Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29164](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29164) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arun Vignesh Malarkkan, Manan Roy Choudhury, Utkarsh Byahut, Yash Ravindra Charde, Vivek Gupta, Yanjie Fu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Representation Learning, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 语言模型的潜在推理轨迹结构尚不明确。发现对比精炼信号具有低秩结构；提出轨迹不变潜在精炼(TILR)框架。使释义一致性提高约10%，轨迹方差降低50%。

<h3 id="273">[273] Projected Exploitability Descent for Nash Equilibrium Computation in Multiplayer Imperfect-Information Games</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29169](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29169) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sam Ganzfried
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Imperfect-Information Games
- **Summary**: 针对多人不完美信息博弈中纳什均衡计算的可扩展性和性能问题，提出投影可利用性下降算法（PED），通过最小化多人广义可利用性函数的代理进行次梯度投影。在三人Kuhn扑克牌实验中，PED表现出持续接近单调的改进，与虚拟博弈和CFR相比，初始迭代性能较差但长期稳定，混合算法FP-PED结合了两者优势。

<h3 id="274">[274] Symbolic Mechanistic Data Attribution: Tracing Training Influence to Learned Behavioral Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29171](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29171) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Reza Habibi, Darian Lee, Magy Seif El-Nasr
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Data Attribution
- **Summary**: 现有数据归因方法无法解释训练数据如何塑造模型的高层行为策略，提出符号化机制数据归因框架（SMDA），通过稀疏自编码器特征上的岭回归拟合可解释符号策略，并分解有监督微调样本的影响。在Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct的拒绝行为分析中，揭示了安全行为的系统性漏洞，并识别出训练样本的跨特征干扰。

<h3 id="275">[275] Anomaly Factory 3D: A Modular Framework for Diverse Pseudo-Anomaly Synthesis in Unsupervised 3D Anomaly Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29181](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29181) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ali Balapour, Faraz Hach
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Anomaly Detection, Point Cloud, Unsupervised Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/vpc-ccg/AF3AD)
- **Summary**: 针对3D点云异常检测中异常样本稀缺且多样的问题，提出Anomaly Factory 3D（AF3AD）模块化框架，通过中心条件参数化变形模型从正常点云合成多样伪异常。在AnomalyShapeNet和Real3D-AD上，物体级和点级检测与定位均取得一致改进，且对噪声具有鲁棒性。

<h3 id="276">[276] A Multi-Dataset Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents in Microservice Failure Diagnosis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29193](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29193) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuanhong Cai, Xiaohui Nie, Kanglin Yin, Changhua Pei, Yongqian Sun, Shenglin Zhang, Haibin Liu, Guiyang Liu, Xidao Wen, Fang Situ, Dan Pei
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, AIOps
- **Summary**: 现有面向LLM智能体的微服务故障诊断基准仅评估最终答案，缺乏对推理过程的评估。引入两个大规模数据集AIOps2025和RCA100，从定位、识别和原因三个维度评估诊断能力。数据集包含超过500个专家标注的故障案例，经大规模竞赛验证，支持6000多个参赛团队。

<h3 id="277">[277] Behavior Uncloning: Distilling Mode Redirection into Policy Weights without Inference-Time Steering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29201](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29201) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hao Wang, Jiuzhou Lei, Dayou Li, Bangya Liu, Minghui Zheng, Manling Li, Ruohan Zhang, Zhiwen Fan
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Imitation Learning, Robot Manipulation, Policy Optimization
- **Summary**: 行为克隆策略可能学习到不安全的行为模式，提出MoRE方法，通过将临时模式分类器的重定向信号蒸馏到策略权重中，并加入保留损失来抑制不期望模式。在8个模拟和真实任务中，平均部署成功率提升44个百分点，且不增加推理时开销，适用于Diffusion Policy和Pi0.5 VLA等骨干网络。

<h3 id="278">[278] AnyBody: Free-Form Whole-Body Humanoid Control from Arbitrary Keypoint Guidance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29209](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29209) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuning Li, Sikai Li, Jiachen Li, Mingyu Ding
