- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Humanoid Robot, Motion Control, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 现有物理仿真跟踪器依赖全身动作捕捉或分解上下身控制，提出AnyBody统一全身人形控制器，通过学习单一隐式运动表示，支持任意关键点子集驱动。通过特权教师在线蒸馏和变换器关键点编码器，实现大规模人体运动跟踪、自由形式控制及下游任务（如写字、避障）。

<h3 id="279">[279] MoPe: Motion Permanence for Robust Monocular Gaussian Mapping in Dynamic Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29237](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29237) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qixin Xiao
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: SLAM, 3D Gaussian Splatting, Dynamic Scene Understanding
- **Summary**: 当前不确定性感知方法逐帧处理动态区域，导致记忆缺失和鬼影伪影。提出运动持久性原则，并实现MoPe记忆感知不确定性滤波器，通过SE(3)变换传播历史动态后验，结合贝叶斯逻辑更新。在Wild-SLAM、Bonn和TUM序列上，跟踪鲁棒性提升，鬼影减少，动态场景增益最大。

<h3 id="280">[280] Confidence-feedback-weighted graph matching network: online-offline laser-induced damage site matching under complex interference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29255](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29255) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yueyue Han, Guanhua Chen, Hangcheng Dong, Kang Zhang, Fengdong Chen, Zhitao Peng, Fa Zeng, Qihua Zhu, Guodong Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Matching, Anomaly Detection, Industrial AI
- **Summary**: 高功率激光设施中在线检测图像与离线基准点匹配困难，存在伪损伤点和大量干扰。提出置信度反馈加权图匹配网络，通过迭代估计节点匹配置信度并反馈指导边特征聚合，配合几何一致性和难例挖掘损失。在复杂场景数据集上匹配F1达到96.36%。

<h3 id="281">[281] A Hybrid Framework for Song Lyric Annotation Based on Human-LLM Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29273](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29273) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rashini Liyanarachchi, Frank Tran, Md Mahmudul Hasan, Aditya Joshi, Erik Meijering
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Emotion Recognition, Music Information Retrieval, Data Annotation
- **Summary**: 歌词情感识别具有主观性且标注不足，探讨人类与LLM标注的对齐性。构建句子级歌词数据集，提出混合标注框架，通过预测潜在不对齐来优化人机标注效率。

<h3 id="282">[282] Manufactured Confidence: How Memory Consolidation Turns Hearsay into Confident Facts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29279](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29279) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alex Kwon
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Agent Safety, LLM Memory
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/collapseindex/manufactured-confidence)
- **Summary**: LLM智能体的记忆压缩会导致随意言论被升级为确信事实，带来安全风险。实验表明，短语的置信度而非来源决定智能体的信任程度，诸如“报道称”等措辞被当作肯定事实。提出在记忆中保留试探性措辞而非升级的修复方法，但攻击者仍可写入虚假信息。

<h3 id="283">[283] Deterministic Decisions for High-Stakes AI. A Zero-Egress Pipeline with the Deployability of RAG and the Accuracy of Machine Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29280](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29280) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Craig Atkinson
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Evaluation, Intervention Bias
- **Summary**: 该论文发现零样本大语言模型在教育咨询中存在的干预偏差（倾向推荐干预），提出使用监督策略学习（如决策树、XGBoost）消除偏差，在OULAD数据集上实现近零校准误差，macro-F1达0.79，且决策延迟低于5毫秒。

<h3 id="284">[284] Covering the Unseen: Information Demand Coverage Optimization for Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29328](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29328) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bingxue Zhang, Jianying Jia, Feida Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Question Answering, Submodular Maximization
- **Summary**: 现有RAG上下文选择采用top-k排序，在复杂查询中过度覆盖单一语义而忽略关键子问题。提出GeoRAG，将上下文选择重构为信息需求覆盖优化，通过Sinkhorn-Wasserstein距离和需求加权设施选址目标实现。在6个开放域QA基准上，精确匹配提升6.5-7.5点，优于MMR、DPP等方法。

<h3 id="285">[285] AMR: Adaptive Modality Routing for Multimodal Polyglot Speaker Identification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29335](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29335) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chuxiao Zuo, Yao Zhu, Minqiang Xu, Manhong Wang, Yunke Zhang, Fei Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Speaker Identification, Multimodal Learning, Audio-Visual Learning
- **Summary**: 多模态说话人识别面临模态缺失和语言不匹配挑战，提出自适应模态路由（AMR）框架，包含模态适配器、可训练路由器和模态感知训练策略。在POLY-SIM 2026评价集上，平均准确率99.07%，超越基线32.73%。

<h3 id="286">[286] Adaptive Financial Transformer with Regime-Gated Attention for Stock Return Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29347](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29347) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dishan Sarkar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Financial AI, Time Series Forecasting, Transformer Architecture
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Dishan18/FinancialTransformer)
- **Summary**: 金融市场非平稳性使传统Transformer统一处理所有特征效果不佳，提出自适应金融Transformer（AFT），包含市场制度编码器、自适应门控网络和上下文模块，将95个特征分组为11个语义类别。实验显示预测性能竞争力强，复杂度降低15.2%，参数效率提升。

<h3 id="287">[287] Fast Enough to Act: Spatio-Temporal Visual Token Merging for Low-Latency Robotic VLMs and VLAs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29350](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29350) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junzhou Chen, Jindong Wang, Gang Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Token Compression, Vision-Language Model, Robotics
- **Summary**: 视觉语言模型处理视频和高分辨率图像产生大量视觉令牌，导致推理延迟高，阻碍实时机器人控制。提出ST-Merge，一种即插即用、无需训练的令牌融合框架，通过3D时空坐标和多重队列并行匹配机制融合冗余令牌。在Qwen2.5-VL上实现2倍加速，精度损失仅1%；在π0.5 VLA上实现8.3倍加速。

<h3 id="288">[288] Dynamic Parsing and Updating Natural Language Specification using VLMs for Robust Vision-Language Tracking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29357](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29357) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiao Wang, Liye Jin, Dan Xu, Yuehang Li, Lan Chen, Yaowei Wang, Yonghong Tian, Jin Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Tracking, Object Tracking, Vision-Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Event-AHU/Open_VLTrack)
- **Summary**: 现有视觉语言跟踪方法存在目标更新错误、背景干扰和幻觉问题。提出语言依存解析机制，精确提取跟踪主成分（目标对象、语义概念、背景上下文），并利用Qwen-VL进行组件感知的文本描述更新。在TNL2K、LaSOT等基准上取得一致优越性能。

<h3 id="289">[289] Solver-Verified Formulation Generation and Selection for Multi-Warehouse Inventory Allocation Using Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29366](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29366) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jintao Xu, Yingzheng Ma, Jiong Dong, Yongzhi Qi, Jianshen Zhang, Dongyang Geng, Anni Zhang
- **Subjects**: math.OC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Combinatorial Optimization, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对多仓库库存分配场景中需求表述半结构化、难以直接转化为优化模型的问题，提出ORLA框架，利用LLM生成候选规划模型，通过求解器反馈进行验证和选择。在29个生产数据集上，ORLA相比现有方法提升分配准确率4.5个百分点。

<h3 id="290">[290] DR-GS: Physically-Based Deformable and Relightable 2D Gaussians</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29379](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29379) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaxin Li, Tong Wu, Yi Wei, Tailin Wu, Li Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.GR
- **Tags**: Gaussian Splatting, 3D Reconstruction, Novel View Synthesis
- **Summary**: 针对现有高斯溅射方法在动态形变和重光照下物理不一致的问题，提出DR-GS框架，显式解耦几何、光照和材质。实验表明在静态重建、动态形变和重光照任务上达到领先视觉质量。

<h3 id="291">[291] Learning to Adaptively Allocate Gaussians for Arbitrary-Scale Image Super-Resolution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29400](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29400) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Giulio Federico, Giuseppe Amato, Claudio Gennaro, Fabio Carrara, Marco Di Benedetto
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.GR
- **Tags**: Image Super-Resolution, Gaussian Splatting
- **Summary**: 针对任意尺度超分辨率中传统模型依赖固定尺度插值的问题，提出QuADA-GS，通过神经路由架构动态分配高斯原语，结合层次指针卷积实现高效非网格通信。在保持低延迟和小内存的同时达到SOTA性能。

<h3 id="292">[292] Self-Organized Conformal Prediction: Reducing Regional Coverage Gaps with Unsupervised Group Discovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29403](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29403) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Louis Berthier, Ahmed Shokry, Maxime Moreaud, Guillaume Ramelet, Aymeric Dieuleveut
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Conformal Prediction, Uncertainty Estimation, Self-Organizing Map
- **Summary**: 针对共形预测中均匀校准掩盖区域欠覆盖的问题，提出自组织共形预测（SOCP），利用自组织映射发现输入空间分组并进行局部校准。在8个基准上，SOCP将区域覆盖缺口平均降低7.1%，仅增加6.2%集合大小。

<h3 id="293">[293] LC-ICL: Label-Guided Contrastive In-Context Learning for Robust Information Extraction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29407](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29407) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiao You, Tianwei Yan, Shan Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: In-Context Learning, Information Extraction, Named Entity Recognition
- **Summary**: 针对少样本信息提取中仅使用正例演示的局限，提出LC-ICL，结合正负样本构建上下文学习示例，利用错误原因标签增强模型辨别能力。实验表明在命名实体识别和关系抽取任务上优于先前方法。

<h3 id="294">[294] Can Machines Really See Objects in Images? A Study Based on Syntactic Distance and Visual Self-Referential Instances</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29416](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29416) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xingyu Peng, Junran Wu, Yue Hou, Zhongliang Qiao, Jiaheng Liu, Shangzhe Li, Jichang Zhao, Wenjun Wu, Xianglong Liu, Yongxin Tong, Li Dong, Ke Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision Transformer, Object Recognition, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 针对视觉模型是否真正理解物体的问题，通过句法距离构建视觉自参照任务，发现当局部统计线索不可靠时，ResNet和ViT的准确率会崩溃到随机水平。表明当前架构在全局概念任务上存在结构性能力边界。

<h3 id="295">[295] LLMography: Transforming Human-AI Conversations into Traceability, Oversight, and Auditability Indicators</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29437](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29437) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammed Bousmah
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Human-AI Interaction, AI Transparency, Provenance
- **Summary**: 针对当前仅关注AI输出检测而忽略交互过程的问题，提出LLMography框架，将人机对话转化为可衡量的溯源、贡献度和可审计性指标。在19份审计报告上，平均人类指导得分86.8，提示质量81.9。

<h3 id="296">[296] Closing the Activation-Cone Blind Spot: Response-Time Probing and Unified Defense</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29441](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29441) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Subhadip Mitra
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.ET; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Jailbreak Attack, Adversarial Robustness
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/bassrehab/response-time-probing)
- **Summary**: 针对推理时安全防御对预填充攻击的结构性盲点，证明基于单层激活对齐的防御对预填充攻击无效，并提出响应时间探测方法，在7个模型上AUROC达0.97-1.00，结合停顿与AlphaSteer达到0.994防御成功率。

<h3 id="297">[297] Bridging VideoQA and Video-Guided Agentic Tasks via Generalized Keyframe Extraction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29445](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29445) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sunqi Fan, Qingle Liu, Runqi Yin, Meng-Hao Guo, Shuojin Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, GUI Agent, Keyframe Extraction
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/VG-GUI-TASKER/VG-GUI-TASKER)
- **Summary**: 针对现有视频问答基准仅评估浅层视觉线索而忽略深层知识迁移的问题，提出VG-GUIBench基准和TASKER关键帧提取算法。TASKER在EgoSchema和NExT-QA上分别提升2.0%和1.8%。

<h3 id="298">[298] Resonant Brane Splatting for Arbitrary-Scale Super-Resolution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29453](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29453) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Giulio Federico, Giuseppe Amato, Claudio Gennaro, Fabio Carrara, Marco Di Benedetto
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.GR
- **Tags**: Image Super-Resolution, Gaussian Splatting
- **Summary**: 针对高斯溅射在任意尺度超分辨率中平滑原语建模边缘的瓶颈，提出谐振膜溅射（RBS），用Brane原语表达空间变化颜色，内部高斯-埃尔米特模式捕捉高频。实验表明在重建质量和速度上优于先前方法。

<h3 id="299">[299] Interpretable Inverse Design of Metal-Organic Frameworks with Large Language Model Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29459](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29459) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kyungmin Nam, Seunghee Han, Jihan Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Material Discovery, Inverse Design
- **Summary**: 针对金属有机框架逆设计空间大且模型缺乏可解释性的问题，提出LLM4MOF闭环框架，利用语言模型代理推理化学、构建候选并仿真测试。在六个任务上，400次评估内聚焦于高性能结构，并生成了新的MOF。

<h3 id="300">[300] Rank-Aware Hyperbolic Alignment for Vision-Language Dataset Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29464](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29464) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jongoh Jeong, Sun-Kyung Lee, Kuk-Jin Yoon
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dataset Distillation, Vision-Language Model, Hyperbolic Learning
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 针对视觉语言数据集蒸馏中欧氏空间全维度对齐过于严格的问题，提出秩感知双曲对齐（RAHA），在双曲空间中对齐共享语义并保留模态私有多样性。实验表明在固定预算下具有竞争力的跨模态检索性能。

<h3 id="301">[301] A Posteriori Error Analysis for Decoupled Neural Approximations of Fully Coupled FBSDEs with Control Mismatch</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29474](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29474) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xichuan Zhang
- **Subjects**: math.OC; cs.AI; math.NA
- **Tags**: Scientific Computing, Neural PDE, Neural Operator
- **Summary**: 针对全耦合前向-后向随机微分方程神经近似中的控制失配问题，提出后验误差分析框架，得到仅依赖于终端缺陷、路径残差和控制失配的可计算误差界。数值实验验证了诊断指标的有效性。

<h3 id="302">[302] CRAFT: Counterfactual Credit Assignment from Free Sibling Rollouts for Self-Distilled Agentic Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29476](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29476) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zibin Meng, Kani Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent, Credit Assignment
- **Summary**: 针对自蒸馏智能体强化学习中信用分配仅基于实现轨迹且符号盲目的问题，提出CRAFT方案，利用GRPO的兄弟轨迹实现反事实符号信用分配，并引入非对称控制器和逐token KL惩罚极化。实验表明在多种环境和规模下均有提升。

<h3 id="303">[303] To Reason or to Fabricate: Reasoning Without Shortcuts via Hint-Anchored Pairwise Aggregation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29481](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29481) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiuheng Lin, Chen Zhang, Yansong Feng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Data Contamination
- **Summary**: 针对强化学习中预训练数据重叠导致模型记忆答案并编造推理的问题，提出HIPPO框架，通过提示注入故意触发重叠行为，利用成对奖励模型区分真实推理与捷径。实验表明显著提升推理泛化能力。

<h3 id="304">[304] Reported Confidence in LLMs Tracks Commitment More Than Correctness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29490](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29490) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dharshan Kumaran
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Uncertainty Estimation, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Summary**: 针对LLM口头置信度报告是否反映正确性的问题，采用两阶段弃权范式实验，发现口头置信度预测弃权决策远优于预测正确性，而token对数概率则相反。结果表明口头置信度是行为导向的承诺准备信号，而非可靠性指标。

<h3 id="305">[305] The Verbose Context Problem in Medical Records</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29503](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29503) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shiva Kaul, Min-Gyu Kim, Anjum Khurshid, Sriram Vishwanath
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Long Context, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 医疗记录中结构化概念的冗长上下文问题导致群体健康分析需处理超过40万token。本文提出PopMedQA基准测试和neopatient库，用于生成人工患者记录。实验表明领域无关方法无法缓解该问题。

<h3 id="306">[306] SAKE: Software Architectural Knowledge Evaluation Benchmark for Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29520](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29520) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tiziano Santilli, Francesco Daghero, Mayhar Tourchi Moghaddam
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.DB
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Software Engineering, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 大型语言模型在软件架构推理能力上缺乏评估。本文提出SAKE基准，包含2154个专家策划的多选题，覆盖8个架构类别。评估11个模型发现整体准确率高但不同类别差异明显。

<h3 id="307">[307] MotionAtlas: Detailed Region Captioning for Motion-Centric Videos</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29531](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29531) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weisong Liu, Haochen Wang, Kuan Gao, Yuhao Wang, Yikang Zhou, Zhongwei Ren, Jacky Mai, Anna Wang, Yanwei Li, Jason Li, Zhaoxiang Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Vision-Language Model, Video Captioning
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 现有视频描述方法缺乏区域级运动理解。本文提出MotionAtlas系统，包含人工标注基准、可扩展数据管道和视频MLLM，实现区域感知运动描述。在通用运动基准上平均提升5.2个百分点。

<h3 id="308">[308] SemJoin: Semantic Join Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29532](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29532) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Christopher Gou, Aditya Banerjee, Jiaxuan Wang, Chunwei Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Database, Optimization
- **Summary**: 语义连接需要大量LLM调用，现有方法固定单一策略。本文提出基于LLM代理的决策管道，动态选择聚类连接或分类器策略。在三个数据集上F1分数比自适应块连接高20-33点，token成本更低。

<h3 id="309">[309] RESOURCE2SKILL: Distilling Executable Agent Skills from Human-Created Multimodal Resources</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29538](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29538) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yijia Fan, Zonglin Di, Zimo Wen, Yifan Yang, Mingxi Cheng, Qi Dai, Bei Liu, Kai Qiu, Yue Dong, Ji Li, Chong Luo
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multimodal Learning, Skill Learning
- **Summary**: 现有技能库多基于手工编写或代理轨迹，未充分利用多模态资源。本文提出RESOURCE2SKILL框架，从教程视频、代码等资源中提取可执行技能，构建多模态技能维基。在七个领域平均提升11.9个百分点。

<h3 id="310">[310] Em-ergence of the em-dash: a population-level rise in em-dash frequency in medRxiv preprints at the dawn of the large-language-model era</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29540](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29540) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Przemysław Czuma
- **Subjects**: cs.DL; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, AI-Generated Text Detection, Linguistic Resource
- **Summary**: 缺乏对LLM使用痕迹（如破折号）在科学文献中变化的测量。本文预注册研究分析medRxiv预印本，发现ChatGPT后破折号使用率从4.23%升至11.58%。该指标可作为群体水平指示符。

<h3 id="311">[311] Proteus: Automated Adversarial Robustness Testing for Audio Deepfake Detectors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29544](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29544) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nicolas M. Müller, Aditya Tirumala Bukkapatnam, Zohaib Ahmed
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Audio Processing, Adversarial Robustness, Deepfake Detection
- **Summary**: 音频深度伪造检测器缺乏自动化鲁棒性测试。本文提出Proteus框架，使用广度优先搜索和Q-learning发现对抗性转换链。特定转换链能可靠翻转检测结果同时保持语音质量。

<h3 id="312">[312] VISTA-DZ: Visual Semantic Trajectory Adaptation for Personalized Dilemma Zone Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29548](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29548) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chuheng Wei, Ziye Qin, Ziran Wang, Guoyuan Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DB; cs.HC; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Trajectory Prediction, Personalization
- **Summary**: 信号交叉口困境区的驾驶员决策高度个性化，现有模型依赖手工特征。本文提出VISTA-DZ框架，通过视觉轨迹和VLM生成行为轮廓进行个性化预测。在SDZ数据集上达到93.26%准确率，跨域表现良好。

<h3 id="313">[313] Coverage-Driven KV Cache Eviction for Efficient and Improved Inference of LLM</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29563](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29563) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuvendu Roy, Mengyao Zhai, Hossein Hajimirsadeghi, Golnoosh Samei
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, KV Cache, Memory Efficiency
- **Summary**: LLM推理中KV缓存内存开销大，现有驱逐策略降低token覆盖导致性能下降。本文提出K-VEC，通过跨头和跨层覆盖模块提高token保留率。在LongBench上最高提升10.35个点。

<h3 id="314">[314] TF-MoE: Time-Frequency Mixture-of-Experts for Efficient Speech Separation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29575](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29575) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qinzhe Hu, Chenda Li, Wangyou Zhang, Shujie Liu, Yan Lu, Yanmin Qian
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Mixture-of-Experts, Speech Separation
- **Summary**: 语音分离模型计算成本高，难以部署在边缘设备。本文提出TF-MoE，稀疏混合专家框架在时间和频率维度动态选择专家。在Libri2Mix上SDR比BSRNN高3.8 dB，计算成本相当。

<h3 id="315">[315] ReMAP-PET: Beyond Visual Understanding -- Learning Region-Guided Metabolic Alignment Semantics from Brain PET</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29577](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29577) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dasen Dai, Yanteng Zhang, Shuoqi Li, Yuxiang Wei, Hongjie Yu, Qingxin Zhang, Qizhen Lan, Jagath C. Rajapakse, Vince D. Calhoun
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multimodal Learning, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 现有3D脑部基础模型将PET视为通用体积数据，忽略区域代谢信息。本文提出ReMAP-PET，通过区域标准化摄取值比（SUVR）回归和对比学习学习代谢语义。SUVR平均绝对误差0.070，Recall@1 77.8%。

<h3 id="316">[316] ScAle: Attention Head Scaling as a Minimal Adapter for Spatial Reasoning in Vision Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29579](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29579) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rahul Chowdhury, Timothy A Rupprecht, Xuan Shen, Pu Zhao, Yanzhi Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Spatial Reasoning
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 视觉语言模型的空间推理改进通常需要大量参数微调。本文提出ScAle，仅需1K可训练参数学习标量系数调整激活值。在SpatialEval上相对准确率提升达134.1%，保持非空间VQA性能。

<h3 id="317">[317] The Joint Effect of Quantization and Sampling Temperature on LLM Safety Alignment: A Factorial Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29581](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29581) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hari Prasad, Ritam Pal
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Quantization, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: LLM部署中量化和采样温度通常被固定，安全性未知。本文对9个模型进行因子分析，发现量化基本安全中性，温度增加决策不稳定性，但两者交互亚加性。建议报告多采样稳定性。

<h3 id="318">[318] Bilevel Optimization for Neural Architecture Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29582](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29582) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abhishek Shukla, Ankur Sinha, Faiz Hamid
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural Architecture Search, Bilevel Optimization, Optimization
- **Summary**: 神经架构搜索（NAS）是双层优化问题。本文综述了基于采样和基于双层优化的方法，并提出辅助数学规划框架。结果表明基于双层理论的方法在准确性和效率上普遍优于采样方法。

<h3 id="319">[319] SonoCLIP: Mask-Guided Region-Aware Vision-Language Pretraining for Fetal Ultrasound Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29586](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29586) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hang Su, Chao Sun, Zhaofan Li, Wei Hu, Juhua Liu, Bo Du
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model, Contrastive Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Harrison-one/SonoCLIP)
- **Summary**: 基于CLIP的超声模型依赖全局图文对齐，对局部结构不敏感。本文提出SonoCLIP，引入掩码通道视觉提示和sigmoid对比损失，在144万张胎儿超声图像上预训练。零样本迁移性能领先。

<h3 id="320">[320] How AI settled the complexity of the oldest SGD algorithm</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29593](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29593) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Michał Dereziński, Xiaoyu Dong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.NA; math.OC; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Scientific Discovery, Optimization, Complexity Theory
- **Summary**: Kaczmarz算法是最早的随机梯度下降算法，但其最坏情况复杂度长期未知。本文利用AI模型成功确定了该复杂度。文章讲述了这一发现过程。

<h3 id="321">[321] One Scene, Two Depths: Probing Geometric Ambiguity in Monocular Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29600](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29600) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaohao Xu, Feng Xue, Xiang Li, Haowei Li, Shusheng Yang, Tianyi Zhang, Matthew Johnson-Roberson, Xiaonan Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Depth Estimation, Benchmark
- **Venue**: European Conference on Computer Vision (ECCV) 2026
- **Summary**: 单目深度估计模型通常忽略场景的层次几何结构，对于透明场景存在歧义。本文提出MultiDepth-3k基准来衡量深度层偏好和ML-SRA，并引入无训练的拉普拉斯视觉提示（LVP）来改变报告层。实验显示DAv2-L达到75.5% ML-SRA，表明模型存在互补的几何假设。

<h3 id="322">[322] Langshaw: Declarative Interaction Protocols Based on Sayso and Conflict</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29601](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29601) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Munindar P. Singh, Samuel H. Christie V, Amit K. Chopra
- **Subjects**: cs.PL; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Formal Methods, Agent Communication Protocol
- **Summary**: 现有多智能体协议语言要么过度约束，要么难以表达含义。提出Langshaw，一种基于sayso（谁优先设置属性）和nono/nogo（冲突动作）的声明性协议语言，并给出形式语义及安全性/活性判定方法。该语言能够生成适合灵活异步执行的面向消息的协议。

<h3 id="323">[323] Mechanistically Eliciting Latent Behaviors in Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29604](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29604) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrew Mack, Nina Panickssery, Alexander Matt Turner
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Mechanistic Interpretability, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 需要发现语言模型内部的可泛化扰动来揭示隐藏行为模式。提出因果扰动诱发（CPE）方法，通过张量分解无监督地发现可解释的低秩适配器（LoRA）。CPE数据高效，在Countdown任务上匹配GRPO（85% vs 87%），并能恢复密码锁定Llama3-70B的BigCodeBench性能至85%，消除对齐伪造行为。

<h3 id="324">[324] Does Role Specialization Matter for Explanation Faithfulness in Mixture-of-Experts?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29613](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29613) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yeji Kim, Housam Babiker, Mi-Young Kim, Randy Goebel
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Explainable AI, Faithfulness
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/dut0817/FL-I2MoE_Decor)
- **Summary**: 混合专家（MoE）模型中角色分解是否保证解释忠实性尚不明确。提出表示级别去相关正则化以减少专家间相似性，从而改善忠实性。实验表明，在多个多模态基准上，该方法提升了全面性、充分性和AOPC指标，同时保持任务性能。

<h3 id="325">[325] Do We Still Need Fine Tuning? Turkish Sentiment Analysis in the Era of Large Language Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29614](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29614) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sercan Karakaş, Yusuf Şimşek
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sentiment Analysis, Fine-Tuning, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: 34th IEEE Signal Processing and Communications Applications Conference
- **Summary**: 在大语言模型时代，有监督微调是否仍是土耳其语情感分析的必要手段尚未明确。比较了经典机器学习、微调预训练语言模型和提示大语言模型在土耳其电商评论数据集上的表现。结果显示，微调BERTurk在三分类任务上全面优于所有零样本大语言模型，中性类是最主要难点。

<h3 id="326">[326] Two-Stage Prompt Optimization for Few-Shot Relation Extraction: From Reasoning-Guided Search to Gradient-Guided Refinement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29639](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29639) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aunabil Chakma, Mihai Surdeanu, Eduardo Blanco
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Prompt Engineering, Few-Shot Learning, Information Extraction
- **Summary**: 自动提示优化在少样本关系抽取领域尚未充分探索。提出两阶段框架：先基于推理的优化进行广泛改进，再通过GradPO基于损失和梯度信号进行局部精炼。在FS-TACRED上使用Qwen3-4B取得最佳性能，在FS-FewRel上也具有竞争力。

<h3 id="327">[327] Fast Wireless Foundation Models with Early-Exits</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29640](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29640) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Omar Mashaal, Hatem Abou-Zeid
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Early Exit, Foundation Model, 6G Networks
- **Summary**: 无线基础模型计算成本高，且全深度执行在OOD任务上可能降级。提出早期退出框架，在冻结编码器各层附加轻量任务头，实现可变深度推理。结果实现高达93%的FLOPs减少，同时在未见任务上超全编码器精度，且固定退出策略优于传统早期退出策略。

<h3 id="328">[328] Fuzzing Large Language Models to Elicit Hidden Behaviours</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29646](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29646) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammed Abu Baker, Lakshmi Babu-Saheer
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Backdoor Detection, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 系统性地研究通过注入噪声（模糊测试）触发休眠代理后门行为。在6个后门模型上对比权重模糊和激活模糊，发现模糊测试在4/6模型上比温度采样更有效，且代理任务汤普森采样可提升效果。这是首个系统性研究，并首次展示代理任务超参数选择可迁移到真实任务。

<h3 id="329">[329] Hybrid Retriever Evolution for Multimodal Document Reasoning Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29648](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29648) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bohan Yao, Shruthan Radhakrishna, Vikas Yadav
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Document Understanding, Multimodal Learning, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 现有检索流水线无法适应多模态文档推理的逐步需求。提出失败驱动演化框架，元代理自主发现多检索器协调策略。演化后的智能体学会何时调用各检索器以及跨模态跨页面组合证据。在MMLongBench-Doc和DocBench上性能提升高达+19.6点，超越多个现有系统。

<h3 id="330">[330] Unlocking the Visual Record of Materials Science: A Large-Scale Multimodal Dataset from Scientific Literature</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29667](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29667) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Subham Ghosh, Shubham Tiwari, Mohammad Ibrahim, Abhishek Tewari
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Dataset, Materials Science
- **Summary**: 材料科学文献中的视觉记录因复合图难以被AI利用。提出MatMMExtract流水线，分解复合图并生成结构化注释，构建MatSciFig数据集（391,606个面板级图文对）。微调YOLO12-m检测器达mAP_50 0.9227，双编码器检索相比零样本CLIP在R@1上提升4.4倍。

<h3 id="331">[331] A Machine-Verified Proof of a Quantum-Optimization Conjecture</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29687](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29687) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Uri Kol, Maor Ben-Shahar, Kfir Sulimany, Dirk Englund
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.LO; math.OC
- **Tags**: Quantum Computing, Automated Theorem Proving, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 针对量子优化中FGG猜想（QAOA精确近似比）悬而未决十多年。使用大语言模型Claude Fable 5和Lean 4证明助手，实现了机器验证的证明。证明揭示了隐藏的动态对称性，为量子信息科学中的开放猜想解决铺平道路。

<h3 id="332">[332] Early Warning Signals for OpenVLA Failure under Visual Distribution Shift</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29699](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29699) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dipesh Tharu Mahato, Rachel Ren
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Vision-Language Model, Anomaly Detection
- **Summary**: 视觉-语言-动作模型在视觉分布偏移下的故障难以诊断。测试在LIBERO操作场景中，OpenVLA激活是否包含可线性解码的故障预测信息。逻辑探针在15步内达到AUROC 0.972和AUPRC 0.352，优于基线，且部分泛化到相机抖动。

<h3 id="333">[333] ARMOR: Adaptive Retriever Optimization for Low-Resource Telecom Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29706](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29706) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Heshan Fernando, Quan Xiao, Yan Xin, Tianyi Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: RAG, Question Answering, Domain Adaptation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/heshandevaka/ARMOR.git)
- **Summary**: 电信问答中证据分散，生成器微调可能过专业化。提出ARMOR方法，自适应调节检索器，联合优化RAG生成似然和InfoNCE对比目标，并正则化查询编码器。在电信检索和生成基准上改进证据检索和答案生成。

<h3 id="334">[334] SEVA: Self-Evolving Verification Agent with Process Reward for Fact Attribution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29713](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29713) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aojie Yuan, Yi Nian, Haiyue Zhang, Zijian Su, Yue Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Fact Checking, Self-Correction
- **Venue**: AI4GOOD@ICML 2026 and FAGEN@ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Justin0504/Verifiable_agent)
- **Summary**: 现有事实归属验证器仅输出二元标签，无法审计。提出SEVA，结构化验证智能体，输出证据对齐、推理链、校准置信度和六类错误诊断。通过过程奖励解决多组件输出的优势坍塌，并实现自我演化循环。SEVA-3B在ClearFacts上匹配GPT-4o-mini F1且输出更丰富。

<h3 id="335">[335] Optimizing Expert-Designed Crystal Graph Networks for Band-Gap Prediction with an Autonomous LLM Research Loop</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29717](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29717) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chenmu Zhang, Boris I. Yakobson
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Material Property Prediction, Graph Neural Network, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 优化专家设计晶体图网络进行带隙预测是材料科学的中心问题。一个通用编码智能体在无外部预训练下自主构建了MatBench带隙基准上最准确的模型，超越17个专家设计模型。分析表明它实现了已知方法（边上的元素对特征和晶体空间群嵌入）。

<h3 id="336">[336] Diagnosing and Mitigating Context Rot in Long-horizon Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29718](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29718) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shijie Xia, Yikun Wang, Zhen Huang, Pengfei Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Long Context, LLM Evaluation, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 大语言模型在长程任务中上下文增长导致能力退化（上下文腐烂）。聚焦深度搜索场景，评估四个开源模型，揭示腐烂现象：模型过早放弃或给出不确定答案。研究上下文管理和事后拒绝采样两种缓解策略，并证明可结合使用。

<h3 id="337">[337] Redefining Maritime Anomaly Detection via Equation-Grounded Synthetic Anomalies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29721](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29721) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Youngseok Hwang, Sungho Bae, Dohun Lee, Jaeeun Seo, Jeehong Kim, Wonhee Lee, Hyunwoo Park
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Time Series Anomaly Detection, Maritime Anomaly Detection
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/snudial/open-maritime-anomaly-detection)
- **Summary**: 海事异常检测面临AIS数据缺乏标签、现有方法依赖统计稀有性或专家标注等问题，忽视交互风险。提出基于方程驱动的异常分类体系（三类）和分数-合成-标注流水线，利用LLM引导的合理性分数合成异常并赋予时间戳标签。实验在多种时间序列和异常检测模型上评估，提供了系统基准。

<h3 id="338">[338] From Trait to Behavior: A Cognitive-Affective Personality System (CAPS) Perspective on Multi-Homing Intention in AIGC Platforms</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29726](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29726) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xuchao Zhang, Jihye Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI-Generated Content, User Modeling, Technology Acceptance
- **Venue**: International Journal of Human-Computer Interaction
- **Summary**: 现有研究聚焦单一AIGC平台使用，缺乏跨平台多宿主意图的理论。基于人格特质-感知-行为反应框架构建三阶段多重中介模型，整合最优刺激水平、互补性和感知价值理论。结果表明最优刺激水平通过感知互补性和感知知识价值的链式中介影响多宿主意图，社会影响显著正向，使用体验不显著。

<h3 id="339">[339] PS-PPO: Prefix-Sampling PPO for Critic-Free RLHF</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29758](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29758) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Doo Hwan Hwang, Kee-Eung Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RLHF, LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 无评论者RLHF方法对长推理轨迹全量更新，计算成本高，而前缀常包含足够信息。提出前缀采样PPO（PS-PPO），对每轨迹采样截止步长，仅反向传播前缀，通过重要性加权保持无偏梯度。在数学推理和RLHF基准上大幅降低训练计算和峰值显存，精度与强基线持平。

<h3 id="340">[340] TopoAgent: An Agentic Framework for Automated Topology Learning in Medical Imaging</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29763](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29763) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guangyu Meng, Pengfei Gu, Xueyang Li, Yiyu Shi, Erin Wolf Chambers, Danny Z. Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Agent, Topological Data Analysis
- **Summary**: 医学图像拓扑分析中现有方法默认单一固定描述符，缺乏自动选择最优描述符的智能体框架。提出TopoAgent，基于LLM的智能体框架，通过感知-推理-行动-反思循环和21个领域工具自动选择拓扑描述符和配置。系统评估15种描述符跨26数据集6分类器，无需任务特定训练。

<h3 id="341">[341] Towards Generalizable and Evidential Nuclear Magnetic Resonance-Based Molecular Structure Elucidation via Large Language Model Agent</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29776](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29776) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zheng Fang, Chen Yang, Yusen Tan, Yunpeng Zhao, Fanjie Xu, Hongxin Xiang, Hanyu Sun, Hanyu Gao, Xiaojian Wang, Wenjie Du, Yuqiang Li, Jun Xia
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Drug Discovery, Interpretability
- **Summary**: NMR谱解析依赖专家知识，现有AI方法要么无法识别新骨架（数据库检索）要么不透明（黑盒）。提出NMRAgent，LLM驱动的证据推理智能体，集成光谱分析工具与化学知识图，模拟专家演绎推理。在新骨架基准上Top-1准确率提升46.5%，Tanimoto相似度提升0.502，并成功解析天然产物结构。

<h3 id="342">[342] Mandol: An Agglomerative Agent Memory System for Long-Term Conversations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29778](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29778) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhan Zhang, Zhiyuan Guo, Ziheng Zeng, Wei Wang, Wentao Wu, Lijie Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Dialogue System, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 现有智能体记忆系统使用异构向量和图数据库导致碎片化和高延迟，RAG检索引入噪声且缺乏令牌预算控制。提出Mandol，聚合式记忆系统，统一记忆原生架构，分层记忆模型与聚合语义数据结构融合多种存储，含定量查询机制。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上取得最佳精度，检索加速5.4倍，插入加速4.8倍。

<h3 id="343">[343] FalconTrack: Photorealistic Auto-Labeled Perception and Physics-Aware Vision-Based Aerial Tracking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29783](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29783) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yan Miao, Karteek Gandiboyina, Noah Giles, Hideki Okamoto, Bardh Hoxha, Georgios Fainekos, Sayan Mitra
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Sim-to-Real, Object Tracking, 3D Vision
- **Summary**: 视觉空中跟踪需要大量标注数据，而逼真数据集依赖手动标注。提出FalconTrack，利用高斯溅射仿真器自动生成标签的流水线，结合多头感知和物理感知跟踪实现零样本仿真到现实迁移。20分钟内生成约1万张标注图像，感知模型零样本类准确率96-100%，机载系统25Hz实现100%跟踪成功。

<h3 id="344">[344] HERO: Improving the Reliability and Sensitivity of Generative Model Evaluation Using Historical Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29784](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29784) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinrui Ruan, Zhenyu Zhao, Waverly Wei, Yueshan Zhang, Zeyu Zheng, Sui Huang, Jingshen Wang
- **Subjects**: stat.ME; cs.AI; econ.EM
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Data Quality, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 生成式AI评估依赖昂贵的黄金标签，噪声银标签可能引入偏差和高方差，且评估重复进行。提出HERO框架，利用历史数据校准银标注者表现并锚定到协变量信息，降低偏差和方差。在模拟和真实基准上验证了条件有效性。

<h3 id="345">[345] What Drives the Inlier-Memorization Effect? A Theory of Outlier Detection via Early Training Dynamics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29791](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29791) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kunwoong Kim, Dongha Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Out-of-Distribution Detection, Anomaly Detection, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 无监督异常检测中内点记忆效应的理论理解有限。针对简单自编码器提供理论分析，刻画记忆效应的出现、强度和持续性，并推导增强指南。在ADBench数据集上取得最先进性能。

<h3 id="346">[346] Multi-Level Distributional Entropy for Explainable Network Intrusion Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29797](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29797) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohamed Aly Bouke, Md Shohel Sayeed, Swee-Huay Heng, Azizol Abdullah, Mohamed Othman
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Cybersecurity, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 网络入侵检测依赖聚合流统计丢失分布结构，现有熵需原始包序列。提出多级分布熵框架，从流级摘要提取可解释熵特征，无需原始包或训练数据。在四个基准上熵特征加权F1达0.708-0.989，揭示F1掩盖的失败模式，SHAP稳定性高。

<h3 id="347">[347] Accelerating Q-learning through Efficient Value-Sharing across Actions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29806](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29806) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Prabhat Nagarajan, Brett Daley, Martha White, Marlos C. Machado
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Q-Learning
- **Summary**: Q学习独立更新每个状态-动作值，效率低且初始值远离真实值。提出均值扩展层，在状态内共享动作值，学习低范数表示，无参数加入Q网络。在57个Atari游戏上提升聚合性能，增加动作间隙，减少值高估。

<h3 id="348">[348] Making Multimodal LLMs Reliable Chart Data Extractors: A Benchmark and Training Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29808](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29808) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuchen He, Peizhi Ying, Liqi Cheng, Kuilin Peng, Yuan Tian, Dazhen Deng, Yingcai Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Learning, Chart Understanding
- **Venue**: CHI'26
- **Summary**: 图表数据提取需求大，现有MLLM虽能理解图表但精确值恢复困难，尤其无标签时。构建无标签真实图表基准，提出渐进学习训练框架。7B模型实现最先进数值精度，用户研究支持混合主动工作流。

<h3 id="349">[349] How Far Can You Get Without a GPU? A Systematic Benchmark of Lightweight Hallucination Detection Across Question Answering, Dialogue, and Summarisation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29809](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29809) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kriti Faujdar, Smit Kadvani
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, LLM Evaluation, Question Answering
- **Summary**: 高精度幻觉检测依赖GPU密集推理，资源受限研究者需轻量CPU方法。系统比较五种轻量方法（ROUGE-L、语义相似度、BERTScore、NLI检测器、集成）在HaluEval基准的问答、对话、摘要任务。集成在问答最佳（F1=0.792），NLI检测器在对话领先（AUC=0.713），所有方法在摘要上接近随机（AUC 0.469-0.574）。

<h3 id="350">[350] Dual-Flow Reinforcement Learning with State-Aware Exploration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29820](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29820) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qijun Li, Zheng Fu, Qi Song, Yifei He, Weitao Zhou, Kun Jiang, Diange Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Flow Matching, Continuous Control
- **Summary**: 连续控制中多模态最优动作与不确定回报分布共存，现有高斯值估计有偏，生成策略易模式崩溃。提出Dual-Flow RL，用条件流匹配联合建模回报分布和多模态策略，引入熵-协方差探索调节器。在DeepMind Control Suite和Humanoid-Bench上达到最先进，优于先前基于扩散和流的方法。

<h3 id="351">[351] Experience Graphs: The Data Foundation for Self-Improving Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29823](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29823) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gang Liao, Yujia He, Abdullah Ozturk, Zhouyang Li, Ying Wang, Zhitong Guo, Hongsen Qin, Yaobin Qin, Tao Yang, Zewei Jiang, Dianshi Li, Jort Gemmeke, Jiangyuan Li, Liyuan Li, Nathan Yan, Masha Basmanova, Uladzimir Pashkevich, Matt Steiner, Pedro Pedreira, Rob Fergus, Anirudh Goyal, Carole-Jean Wu, Gaoxiang Liu, Andrew Witten, Daniel J. Abadi
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Database, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 长周期智能体任务产生结构化经验图，但现有框架将其视为可丢弃状态，无法查询和恢复。提出Trellis，将经验图作为一等数据库状态，智能体变为无状态计算。在Meta加速器内核优化中，跨会话重用达到约10倍加速，令牌成本降低52%。

<h3 id="352">[352] Neural Procedural Memory: Empowering LLM Agents with Implicit Activation Steering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29824](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29824) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chengfeng Zhao, Yuqiao Tan, Shizhu He, Yequan Wang, Jun Zhao, Kang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Representation Learning, Activation Steering
- **Summary**: LLM智能体缺乏持久程序记忆，现有RAG依赖显式文本指令导致文本-动作脱节。提出神经程序记忆NPM，无需训练，通过从历史对比经验中提取激活空间中的引导向量隐式驱动任务执行。在四个智能体基准上表现与显式指令基线相当，结合隐式引导和显式工作流具有互补优势。

<h3 id="353">[353] MATCH: Modulating Attention via In-Context Retrieval for Long-Context Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29844](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29844) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Linrui Ma, Chun Hei Lo, Xinyu Wang, Peng Lu, Xihao Yuan, Hanting Chen, Kai Han, Xinghao Chen, Chengjun Zhan, Hanlin Xu, Yichun Yin, Lifeng Shang, Feng Wen, Boxing Chen, Yufei Cui
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Long Context, Sparse Attention
- **Summary**: 针对长上下文Transformer中注意力机制二次计算开销以及局部注意力窗口导致长距离召回性能下降的问题，提出MATCH框架，通过高效检索系统动态集成上下文信息来增强稀疏化注意力。实验结果表明，MATCH显著提升了稀疏注意力模型在合成和真实自然语言任务上的性能。

<h3 id="354">[354] Exploring Motivations for Algorithm Mention in the Domain of Natural Language Processing: A Deep Learning Approach</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29859](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29859) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuzhuo Wang, Yi Xiang, Chengzhi Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.DL; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Text Classification, Information Extraction, Citation Analysis
- **Summary**: 针对学术论文中算法提及目的不明确的问题，提出基于深度学习的句子级框架，识别和分析算法提及动机。结果表明，深度学习模型在动机分类上优于传统模型，NLP论文中超过半数算法相关句子表示直接使用，改进动机最少，且动机多样性随时间增加。

<h3 id="355">[355] SUMO: Segment and Track Any Motion with Nonlinear State Space Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29861](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29861) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kexin Tian, Sixu Li, Keshu Wu, Yang Zhou, Zhengzhong Tu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Object Tracking, State Space Model
- **Summary**: 针对视觉目标跟踪和运动对象分割依赖视觉线索难以处理复杂非线性运动的问题，提出零样本、无需训练的框架SUMO，融合非线性状态空间模型与视觉分割。实验表明，SUMO在VOT和MOS任务上达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="356">[356] RoAd-RL: A Unified Library and Benchmark for Robust Adversarial Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29867](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29867) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adithya Mohan, Daniel Kriegl, Torsten Schön
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Reinforcement Learning, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ICECCME'26
- **Summary**: 针对对抗性强化学习中实现碎片化、评估不一致的问题，提出开源基准测试框架RoAd-RL，提供统一的策略、攻击、防御和鲁棒性度量抽象。在192种攻防配置下评估DQN、PPO和SAC，发现时间平滑防御效果最好，部分常用防御可能适得其反。

<h3 id="357">[357] ARKD: Adaptive Reinforcement Learning-Guided Bidirectional KL Divergence Distillation for Text Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29869](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29869) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zilong Liu, Xuewen Zhang, Jinrui Xing, Juyi Qiao, Huiyong Wang, Junming Jiao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Text Generation, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对知识蒸馏中单一KL散度目标无法平衡主分布拟合与长尾概率建模的问题，提出基于强化学习的自适应KL加权蒸馏框架，动态分配前向与反向KL散度权重。实验表明，该方法在Rouge-L和BertScore上持续优于贪心基线0.4-0.6个百分点。

<h3 id="358">[358] Clinical Reasoning Graphs: Structured Evaluation of LLM Diagnostic Reasoning Reveals Competence Without Consistency</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29876](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29876) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nisarg A. Patel
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; q-bio.QM
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Medical AI, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对大型语言模型诊断准确性无法区分稳定临床推理与模式匹配的问题，引入临床推理图结构化表示。分析750条推理轨迹发现，模型具有诊断能力但缺乏模式规模的推理一致性，表明应补充过程级评估。

<h3 id="359">[359] LWDrive: Layer-Wise World-Model-Guided Vision-Language Model Planning for Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29879](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29879) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chen Yang, Yuhao Wei, Ze Xu, Ziheng Zou, Shuang Liang, Delin Ouyang, Lingfeng Qi, Jie Li, Guofa Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Vision-Language Model, World Model
- **Summary**: 针对视觉语言模型生成的轨迹仅编码粗略驾驶意图且缺乏几何精度的问题，提出层式世界模型引导的驾驶框架LWDrive，通过未来帧生成监督和级联规划器逐步细化轨迹。在NAVSIM基准上达到92.0分。

<h3 id="360">[360] Trust Your Instincts: Confidence-Driven Test-Time RL for Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29892](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29892) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Siyao Chen, Jiakang Yuan, Jiaxin Wang, Tao Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language-Action Model, Reinforcement Learning, Test-Time Adaptation
- **Summary**: 针对视觉-语言-动作模型强化学习依赖外部奖励的问题，提出测试时强化学习框架T^2VLA，利用轨迹级与高置信度专家演示的相似性作为内在奖励。实验表明，其优于监督基线，接近有真实奖励的强化学习性能。

<h3 id="361">[361] SABER-Math: Automated Benchmark for Information Retrieval Evaluation in Mathematics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29894](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29894) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nikolay Georgiev, Maria Drencheva, Kseniia Ibragimova, Ivo Petrov, Dimitar I. Dimitrov, Martin Vechev
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Mathematical Reasoning, Benchmark
- **Venue**: 3rd AI for Math Workshop at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML)
- **Summary**: 针对数学信息检索缺乏专门基准且现有基准无法捕获细粒度相关性的问题，提出全自动基准SABER-Math，利用大语言模型提取摘要和主题并通过偏好竞赛生成相关性评分。评估发现现代嵌入模型优于经典方法但在代数和微积分上仍有困难，MTEB无法可靠预测数学检索性能。

<h3 id="362">[362] Child-Centric Voice Anonymization in Single and Multi-Speaker Speech via Domain-Adapted SSL Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29897](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29897) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pranav Tushar, Xiao Xiao Miao, Rong Tong
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Privacy, Domain Adaptation
- **Venue**: INTERSPEECH2026
- **Summary**: 针对语音匿名化系统在儿童语音上性能下降的问题，基于自监督学习适应儿童语音领域。实验表明，儿童领域适应提高了可懂度和感知质量，同时保持隐私保护，并扩展至多说话人场景。

<h3 id="363">[363] Critical Interval MSE: Toward Reliable Offline Validation for Robot Manipulation Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29898](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29898) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haoxu Huang, Tongsam Zheng, Yifan Chen, Jiacheng You, Yang Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Robotics, Evaluation Methodology
- **Summary**: 针对机器人操作策略验证损失与真实性能相关性差的问题，提出临界区间均方误差，将误差计算限制在任务关键片段并配合动作对齐。实验显示斯皮尔曼相关系数从-0.61提升至-0.87，为加速策略迭代提供了可靠离线验证工具。

<h3 id="364">[364] LLM-based Multimodal Personality Recognition via Facial Action Unit-Text Semantic Fusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29900](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29900) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianyi Zhang, Wei Shan, Yuan Zong, Tianhua Qi, Wenming Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Affective Computing, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 针对单一模态忽略面部线索而多模态方法丢失时间动态的问题，提出融合面部动作单元文本描述与文本回复的LLM框架。实验表明，该方法在多个特质上降低了预测误差并增强了与人类评分的一致性，具有更好的训练稳定性和可解释性。

<h3 id="365">[365] Semi-Supervised Sound Event Detection with Conditional Mixup and Embedding-Level Contrastive Loss</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29901](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29901) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nian Shao, Xian Li, Xiaofei Li
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sound Event Detection, Semi-Supervised Learning, Contrastive Learning
- **Venue**: SMC 2026
- **Summary**: 针对标注数据稀缺导致检测性能受限的问题，采用嵌入级对比损失和条件混合增强的半监督框架。在DESED验证集上达到0.645 PSDS1和0.822 PSDS2，刷新最先进水平。

<h3 id="366">[366] CW-B: Class Weighted Boosting Framework for Imbalance Resilient Multi Class Cardiac Phenotyping</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29907](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29907) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sijia Li, Xiaoyu Tan, Chen Zhan, Yuanji Ma, Haoyu Wang, Xihe Qiu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Class Imbalance, Tabular Learning
- **Summary**: 针对真实世界心脏表型数据不完整和类别不平衡的问题，提出类别加权XGBoost管道，结合折间类别平衡加权、缺失指示符增强和类别错误审计。五折交叉验证在Accuracy、Macro-F1等指标上优于树模型和神经网络基线。

<h3 id="367">[367] Pondering the Way: Spatial-perceiving World Action Model for Embodied Navigation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29908](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29908) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hong Chen, Daqi Liu, Zehan Zhang, Haiguang Wang, Tianhao Lu, Longfei Yan, Haiyang Sun, Fangzhen Li, Hongwei Xie, Bing Wang, Guang Chen, Hangjun Ye, Yihua Tan
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, World Model, Visual Navigation
- **Summary**: 针对现有世界模型规划器解耦目标意图与轨迹合成导致候选依赖和计算开销大的问题，提出任务中心联合观察-动作生成框架SWAM，单次推理同时生成RGB-D序列和动作轨迹。实验表明，在成功率、轨迹精度和推理效率上显著优于两阶段规划器，并具备零样本泛化能力。

<h3 id="368">[368] EVAF: A Test-Retest Protocol for Selective Parametric Consolidation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29916](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29916) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haoliang Han
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Continual Learning
- **Summary**: 针对长期运行语言代理需要决定哪些经验在上下文消失后保留的问题，提出Echo-Valence Attractor Field机制（EVAF）用于门控LoRA巩固，以及重测协议。实验显示，EVAF优先巩固高价值、高惊讶度的经验，在干扰后行为持久性上优于基线，且参数漂移和跨角色污染较低。

<h3 id="369">[369] Latent-CURE for Breast Cancer Diagnosis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29928](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29928) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weiyi Zhao, Xiaoyu Tan, Lu Gan, Liang Liu, Xihe Qiu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Chain-of-Thought, Multimodal Learning
- **Venue**: MICCAI 2026
- **Summary**: 针对现有乳腺癌超声诊断模型采用黑盒端到端范式，易受捷径学习影响且忽略罕见恶性指标的问题，提出Latent-CURE框架，通过非对称加权链式思维（Chain-of-Thought）进行潜在空间推理，强制模型依次推断BI-RADS形态描述符后再做出最终诊断，并采用双重非对称优化策略保护高特异性恶性描述符。实验证明该方法在数据不平衡的医学队列中提供了透明的临床证据并实现了稳健的诊断性能。

<h3 id="370">[370] Data-Efficient Multimodal Alignment for Histopathology-based Molecular Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29949](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29949) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dominik Winter, Dominik Vonficht, Loïc Le Bescond, Christian Gebbe, Marco Rosati, Richard J. Chen, Markus Schick, Ross Stewart, Nicolas Brieu
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; q-bio.GN
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multimodal Learning, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 针对H&E染色切片缺乏分子特异性而RNA测序成本高的问题，提出一种轻量级多模态对齐模块，基于冻结的组织病理学和RNA-Seq基础模型，通过对比学习实现开放词汇的分子提示，无需测序或重新训练即可预测通路活性。在多种癌症队列中检索性能提升25倍，并成功在临床试验中验证了临床实用性。

<h3 id="371">[371] Exploiting Local Flatness for Efficient Out-of-Distribution Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29952](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29952) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seonghwan Park, Hyunji Jung, Dongyeop Lee, Namhoon Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Out-of-Distribution Detection, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 针对现有基于曲率的后验OOD检测方法计算成本高的问题，提出Fold检测器，利用特征Hessian和部分特征归一化实现轻量级平坦度调制，并引入AutoFold进行自监督调优。在OOD基准上AUROC提升1.63%，FPR95降低2.30%，且计算效率与标准前向传播相当。

<h3 id="372">[372] SpreadsheetBench 2: Evaluating Agents on End-to-End Business Spreadsheet Workflows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29955](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29955) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jian Zhu, Yuzheng Zhang, Zeyao Ma, Bohan Zhang, Armin Schoepf, Daniel Woloch, Peter Yiliu Wang, Guangyu Robert Yang, Samuel Jacob, Siddharth Nagisetty, Abhiram Chundru, Jean Lin, Spencer Mateega, Jing Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, LLM Agent, Office Automation
- **Summary**: 针对现有电子表格基准只评估孤立操作而忽略端到端工作流的问题，提出SpreadsheetBench 2，包含生成、调试和可视化三类任务，基于真实商业数据构建。最佳模型整体准确率仅34.89%，调试准确率低至12.00%，揭示了当前系统在真实工作流上的不足。

<h3 id="373">[373] SWE-Together: Evaluating Coding Agents in Interactive User Sessions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29957](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29957) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yifan Wu, Zhuokai Zhao, Songlin Li, Ho Hin Lee, Jiacheng Zhu, Shirley Wu, Tianhe Yu, Serena Li, Lizhu Zhang, Xiangjun Fan, Shengzhi Li
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Agent, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对现有编程智能体基准为静态单轮任务的问题，提出SWE-Together，基于真实用户-智能体交互会话构建多轮基准，并利用基于LLM的用户模拟器重现交互。实验表明更强的智能体成功率更高且需要的干预更少。

<h3 id="374">[374] DuoMem: Towards Capable On-Device Memory Agents via Dual-Space Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29961](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29961) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Peyman Hosseini, Ondrej Bohdal, Ahmed Alajrami, Andrea Maracani, Ignacio Castro, Matthew Purver, Mete Ozay, Savas Ozkan, Taha Ceritli
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Agent, On-Device Learning
- **Summary**: 针对记忆增强型智能体依赖大模型和长上下文，难以在资源受限设备上部署的问题，提出DuoMem双重空间蒸馏框架，通过上下文空间蒸馏和参数空间蒸馏将大教师模型的过程推理能力迁移至紧凑学生模型。在ALFWorld上，4B模型成功率从4.3%提升至77.9%，接近72B教师模型的87.1%，且运行速度快3倍以上。

<h3 id="375">[375] RiverONE: Generating Knowledge-Intensive VLM by Simulated Quantum Machines</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29966](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29966) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xindian Ma, Xinyu Long, Yefei Zhang, Yanchen Liu, Xianghao Li, Yufu Wen, Yike Hu, Yuedong Zhu, Zeyang Ma, Wen Qin, Yikun Wang, Peng Yang, Monan Wang, Teng Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Quantum Computing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/THeWakeSystems/RiverOne)
- **Summary**: 针对当前量子硬件无法直接进行大规模视觉语言模型推理的问题，提出RiverONE，利用模拟量子计算在模型构建阶段生成结构化参数，构建轻量级VLM。仅1.9B参数即可在量子校准图理解任务上达到NVIDIA Ising Calibration 1的95%性能，参数不到其10%。

<h3 id="376">[376] Stabilizing Extrapolation in Looped Transformers via Learned Stochastic Stopping</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.29983](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29983) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hsun-Yu Kuo, El Mahdi Chayti, Patrik Reizinger, Wieland Brendel, Martin Jaggi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, LLM Reasoning, Algorithmic Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对循环Transformer在长度泛化上表现脆弱、分布外方差高的问题，提出在训练中引入循环次数的随机性，并分析RL-Halting作为学习随机停止策略的效果。在二进制加法等任务上，学习停止策略改善了准确率-稳定性权衡。

<h3 id="377">[377] T3R: Deeper Test-Time Adaptation for Graph Neural Networks via Gradient Rotation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30011](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30011) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Huy Truong, Alexander Lazovik, Victoria Degeler
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Test-Time Adaptation, Domain Adaptation
- **Summary**: 针对图神经网络在分布偏移下性能下降且微调需要标签数据的问题，提出T3R方法，利用多个Rotograd矩阵增强任务亲和性并通过梯度旋转实现深层测试时适应。在回归数据集上MAE降低0.172，在跨域OGB分类上相对提升至少9.37%。

<h3 id="378">[378] IBRSteG: Learning a Generalizable Steganography Framework for 3D Gaussian Splatting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30024](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30024) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fanye Kong, Hongyu Xia, Yu Zheng, Boyang Gong, Jie Zhou, Jiwen Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Steganography, 3D Gaussian Splatting
- **Venue**: IEEE Transactions on Multimedia (TMM)
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/LingXiang2023/IBRSteG)
- **Summary**: 针对3D高斯泼溅缺乏可泛化隐写框架的问题，提出IBRSteG，将3D隐写视为前馈式3D高斯嵌入过程，通过GAS网络学习场景无关的嵌入函数。实验表明该方法能有效隐藏不同场景，且容量和安全性更优。

<h3 id="379">[379] MuseBench: Benchmarking Intent-Level Audiovisual Arts Understanding in MLLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30026) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuxuan Fan, Gyusik Seo, Jing Hao, Jaemin Cho, Mohit Bansal, Jaehong Yoon
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Multimodal Learning, Creative AI
- **Summary**: 针对现有MLLM基准主要衡量感知识别而忽略创作意图推理的问题，提出MuseBench，包含4016个问题覆盖多种艺术形式。最佳模型准确率仅48.29%，远低于人类专家的87.18%，暴露了模型在创意领域专业知识的显著差距。

<h3 id="380">[380] Little Brains, Big Feats: Exploring Compact Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30062](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30062) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dari Baturova, Elena Bruches, Ivan Chernov, Roman Derunets, Arsenii Fomin, Andrey Kostin
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Small Language Model, RAG, On-Device Learning
- **Venue**: ECML PKDD 2026, Applied Data Science track
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/SibNN/SLM-RAG-EVAL)
- **Summary**: 针对小语言模型受关注较少的问题，研究其在检索增强生成系统（RAG）中的表现。实验表明基于小模型的RAG系统可在无GPU的设备上合理时间内运行。

<h3 id="381">[381] Neural Subspace Reallocation: Continual Learning as Retrieval-Based Subspace Memory Management</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30067](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30067) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Byeong Hoon Yoon
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Low-Rank Adaptation
- **Summary**: 针对持续学习中低秩适配模块被当作一次性适配器的问题，提出神经子空间重分配（NSR），将LoRA模块通过SVD压缩后存入任务知识库，并通过相似性检索重用。在Split-CIFAR-100上循环恢复时间减少10倍，在5-Datasets上遗忘最少。

<h3 id="382">[382] Online Data Selection for Instruction Tuning via Gaussian Processes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30077](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30077) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jun Wang, Quoc Phong Nguyen, Julien Monteil, Vu Nguyen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Instruction Tuning, Data Curation
- **Summary**: 针对现有在线数据选择方法受限于批约束的问题，提出GAIA框架，利用高斯过程回归建模语义空间中的连续效用流形，并采用自适应策略融合机制。实验表明GAIA显著优于现有基线。

<h3 id="383">[383] Automating the Design of Embodied AgentArchitectures</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30111](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30111) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jian Zhou, Sihao Lin, Jin Li, Shuai Fu, Gengze Zhou, Qi Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, LLM Agent, Neural Architecture Search
- **Summary**: 针对具身智能体的架构设计依赖人工直觉的问题，提出AgentCanvas运行时和KDLoop搜索过程，实现架构级搜索。在三个具身任务上取得可部署的成功率提升，但也暴露了搜索受噪声和局部最优限制。

<h3 id="384">[384] SA-VLA: State-aware tokenizer for improving Vision-Language-Action Models' performance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30113](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30113) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tengyue Jiang, Chunpu Xu, Jiayue Kang, Yao Mu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language-Action Model, Robot Manipulation, Tokenization
- **Summary**: 针对离散动作标记化忽略机器人本体状态导致动作恢复不准确的问题，提出SA-VLA状态感知动作分词器，通过交叉注意力或状态适配器将状态信息融入动作解码。在12个操作任务上平均成功率从0.29提升至0.56，在零样本仿真到真实实验中从0.15提升至0.33。

<h3 id="385">[385] Gravitational Duals from Equations of State II: Large Hierarchies and False Vacua</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30117](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30117) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Raul Jimenez, David Mateos, Pavlos Protopapas, Pau Solé-Vilaró, Pedro Tarancón-Álvarez, Pablo Tejerina-Pérez
- **Subjects**: astro-ph.CO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对强耦合量子场论中的大层级和假真空态，扩展了基于物理信息神经网络（PINNs）的全息对偶重构方法，从边界热力学数据重建体标量势。克服了数值刚度等挑战，在假真空区域实现了势函数的精确重构，证明数据驱动方法可深入理解强耦合系统结构。

<h3 id="386">[386] Hyper-Network Neural Functional Maps for Unsupervised Robust 3D Shape Matching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30131](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30131) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dongliang Cao, Florian Bernard
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Vision
- **Summary**: 现有非刚性3D形状匹配方法在处理部分性、拓扑噪声等挑战时效果不佳。本文提出超网络以无监督方式预测非线性神经功能映射（NFM），通过MLP和残差连接修正标准功能映射，并采用新颖的无监督谱对齐损失训练。该方法可无缝集成到现有管道，显著提升困难场景下的匹配精度。

<h3 id="387">[387] Query-Aware Spreading Activation for Multi-Hop Retrieval over Knowledge Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30133](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30133) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Illia Makarov, Mykola Glybovets
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: RAG, Knowledge Graph, Multi-Hop Reasoning
- **Venue**: Cybernetics and Systems Analysis (Springer)
- **Summary**: 现有Graph RAG在多跳问答中因后续遍历缺乏查询感知而受限。本文提出基于传播激活的查询感知遍历方法，通过单步语义门控实现，全程在Neo4j中以单一Cypher查询完成。在MuSiQue上精确匹配32.80，优于纯结构基线HippoRAG 5.3个点，检索延迟降低1.5至4.9倍。

<h3 id="388">[388] Estimating Grammatical Gender Directions in Contextual Embeddings under Controlled and Natural Contexts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30152](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30152) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Huanping Xiao, Yingji Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Gender Bias, Bias Mitigation
- **Summary**: 现有性别去偏方法仅操作静态词嵌入，未处理上下文表征中语法性别与社会语义偏见的纠缠。本文首次在上下文嵌入中分离语法性别，构建平衡数据集和估值器框架，提出双目标评估指标。未加权的受控上下文生成最纯净的语法性别方向，质心估计器优于判别基线。

<h3 id="389">[389] Physically-Constrained Harmonic Separation for Robust Heart and Respiratory Rate Estimation from Wrist Photoplethysmography</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30156](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30156) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nouhaila Fraihi, Ouassim Karrakchou, Mounir Ghogho
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Healthcare Monitoring, Signal Processing
- **Venue**: IEEE EMBC 2026
- **Summary**: 腕式PPG信号中运动伪影与生理信号频谱重叠影响心率和呼吸率估计。本文提出物理约束谐波分离（PCHS）框架，利用加速度计调节伪影分离，通过物理引导谐波生成器分解信号。在PPG-DaLiA数据集上优于现有方法，并生成可解释信号分解。

<h3 id="390">[390] Federated Learning with Energy-Based Structured Probabilistic Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30161](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30161) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dario Fenoglio, Daniil Kirilenko, Martin Gjoreski, Marc Langheinrich
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Probabilistic Inference
- **Venue**: Structured Probabilistic Inference Generative Modeling workshop at ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 联邦学习中使用固定或启发式权重聚合客户端更新在数据异质性下效果不佳。本文利用条件随机场（CRF）优化聚合权重，建模客户端可靠性和交互关系。实验表明在非IID异质性下持续优于多种联邦学习基线。

<h3 id="391">[391] Beyond Drug Discovery: The Nanotechnology Molecular Optimization (NMO) Benchmark</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30170](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30170) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Matthias Blaschke, Daniel Kienzle, Zsuzsanna Koczor-Benda, Julian Lorenz, Rainer Lienhart, Fabian Pauly
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CE
- **Tags**: Molecular Generation, Materials Science
- **Summary**: 现有分子生成基准偏向药物特性，限制向其他领域迁移。本文提出纳米技术分子优化（NMO）基准，用量子模拟替代代理函数，包含物理约束和崎岖适应度景观。发现先进方法在NMO上不如简单基线，并开发新基线方法，采用结构约束表征和领域无关预训练，超越现有物理性质最优结果。

<h3 id="392">[392] Few-Shot Domain Incremental Learning via Continual Vision-Language Consolidation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30190](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30190) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Naeem Paeedeh, Mahardhika Pratama, Wolfgang Mayer, Mukesh Prasad, Weiping Ding, Yew-Soon Ong
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Few-Shot Learning, Vision-Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Naeem-Paeedeh/CVLC)
- **Summary**: 现有域增量学习需要大量数据适应新域，小样本场景下易过拟合。本文提出小样本域增量学习新问题，设计连续视觉语言巩固（CVLC）算法，通过潜在空间预留和双共轭投影（DCP）参数高效微调，利用LLM生成多模板校准原型。在多个基准上提升高达16%。

<h3 id="393">[393] Forewarned is Forearmed: When Non-Sequential Embedding Turns Into an Anomaly Detector</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30196](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30196) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Elys Allesiardo, Antoine Caubrière, Valentin Vielzeuf
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Multimodal Learning
- **Venue**: LREC 2026
- **Summary**: 本文分析SONAR多模态嵌入，发现某些维度对扰动敏感可指示解码异常。利用编码解码一致性构建精确异常检测器，并探索修改特定维度以纠正异常。强调理解嵌入本身以增强多模态表示可靠性。

<h3 id="394">[394] A Multi Center Breast FNAC Whole-Slide Cytology Dataset for AI-Assisted Patch-Wise Classification Using C1 to C5 Reporting Categories</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30209](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30209) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Garima Jain, Abhijeet Patil, Surabhi Jain, Sanghamitra Pati, Amit Sethi, Sandeep Mathur, Pulkit Verma, Nishi Halduniya, Jatin Kashyap, Sharat Kumar, Simmi Kharb, Sunita Singh, Sucheta Devi Khuraijam, Sushma Khuraijam, Ratan Konjengbam, Arvind Kumar, Deepali Tirkey, Saurav Banerjee, Shivani Kalhan, Rakesh Kumar Gupta, Ranjana Solanki, Deepika Hemranjani, Shashank Nath Singh, Uma Handa, Manveen Kaur, B. G. Malathi, Yogender P., Niraj Kumari, Shruti Gupta, Indu R. Nair, Vidya C., Basumitra Das, Sunil Kumar Komanapalli, Ravindra Karle, Tanaya Kulkarni, Vandana Raphael, Biswajit Dey, Vaishali Gaikwad, Nilam Adhav
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Dataset, Cancer Diagnosis
- **Summary**: 本文介绍多中心乳腺FNAC数据集，包含321例患者、470张WSI和7398个C1-C5标注斑块。数据来自印度三级医疗中心，40倍扫描，使用两种染色方法。数据集约950GB，通过Zenodo公开，支持AI辅助斑块级分类研究。

<h3 id="395">[395] Efficient RGB-T Object Detection via Sparse Cross-Modality Fusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30215](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30215) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chao Tian, Zikun Zhou, Chao Yang, Guoqing Zhu, Zhenyu He
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Multimodal Fusion
- **Venue**: ECCV-2026
- **Summary**: 现有RGB-T检测器使用繁重双骨干和全图融合，计算成本高。本文提出稀疏融合机制，先通过轻量单模态生成高召回候选区域，再对稀疏候选融合检测。该方法在参数量和成本大幅降低下保持竞争性能，并具有高分辨率图像扩展性。

<h3 id="396">[396] Curvature-Guided Sheaf Diffusion for Unsupervised Community Detection on Heterophilic Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30249](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30249) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Feifan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Heterophilous Learning, Unsupervised Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/woodywff/cgsd)
- **Summary**: 异质图上的社区检测对无监督方法困难。本文提出曲率引导层扩散（CGSD），利用边曲率作为拓扑信号，通过曲率门控编码器和曲率感知谱聚类，结合无标签结构损失。在五个异质图基准上，在Wisconsin和Chameleon上最优，聚类器贡献显著且运行机制可解释。

<h3 id="397">[397] KnowsTFM: Knowledge-Informed Fine-Tuning of Small Tabular Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30258](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30258) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Boshko Koloski, Xiangjian Jiang, Senja Pollak, Blaž Škrlj, Mateja Jamnik, Nikola Simidjievski
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, Foundation Model, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 小型表格基础模型在专业领域因数据稀缺表现不佳，现有研究未利用领域关系知识。本文提出知识引导的微调方法（KnowsTFM），通过知识图谱结构注意力先验和低秩更新注入领域知识。在专业任务上显著增益，通用任务增益有限，持续微调可能引发预训练知识崩塌。

<h3 id="398">[398] Defending Against Harmful Supervision Hidden in Benign Samples</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30263](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30263) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bang An, Yibo Yang, Dandan Guo, Ebtisam Alshehri, Carlos Hinojosa, Bernard Ghanem
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Data Poisoning, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 现有防御检测显式有害内容，但无法发现嵌入在良性样本中的有害监督。本文提出嵌入式攻击，并设计双参考SFT（DR-SFT），通过token级正则化将DPO风格对比目标适应SFT，减轻有害微调，超越粗粒度数据过滤。

<h3 id="399">[399] Towards Continual Motion-Language Agents: LoRA Variants for Incremental Motion Understanding and Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30266](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30266) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bertram Taetz, Hugo Albuquerque Cosme da Silva, Gabriele Bleser-Taetz
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Mixture-of-Experts, Text-to-Motion
- **Venue**: CoLLAs 2026
- **Summary**: 运动-语言代理需持续学习新运动概念而不遗忘旧技能。本文基于冻结LLM主干，引入LoRA变体和混合专家架构，使用自动编码器路由器选择任务专家。在五项任务基准上，在运动到文本和文本到运动方向均实现近零遗忘，硬专家选择优于软混合。

<h3 id="400">[400] Research Entity Extraction and Topic Detection from UKRI Grant Proposals</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30304](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30304) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xingran Ruan, Angelo Salatino, Rosa Filgueira, Kara Moraw, Alexandru Marcoci, Gemma Derrick, Sarah Callaghan
- **Subjects**: cs.DL; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Information Extraction, Topic Modeling, LLM Application
- **Venue**: STI-ENID Conference
- **Summary**: 本文比较GPT-4o、Mistral等三种LLM方法从科研资助提案中提取和分类研究实体。Mistral和GPT-4o表现相当，优于DSIT-Taxonomies；Mistral主题分类准确率90.5%，远高于DSIT-Taxonomies的71.4%。Mistral是分析敏感资助数据的高效安全选择。

<h3 id="401">[401] Always-OnAgents:A Survey of Persistent Memory, State, and Governance in LLMAgents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30306](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30306) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianyu Ding, Aditya Nannapaneni, Bingfan Liu, Ling Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Survey
- **Summary**: 问题：长期运行Agent依赖持久状态，但现有研究集中于状态积累与检索，忽视治理、恢复与放弃。方法：提出六维度诊断轴和生命周期，并引入Always-On评估协议（AOEP-v0）来衡量状态变异与恢复。结果：确定了研究空白，AOEP-v0提供了具体的治理评估标准。

<h3 id="402">[402] MCP Server Architecture Patterns for LLM-Integrated Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30317](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30317) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Carson Rodrigues, Oysturn Vas
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agent Architecture, Software Engineering, LLM Application
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/rodriguescarson/mcp-patterns-icsme2026)
- **Summary**: 问题：MCP服务器快速涌现但缺乏结构化架构描述。方法：从15个服务器中归纳五种架构模式，并评估其信度、传输开销和工具选择准确性。结果：模式间Kappa=0.76，工具超过10个时选择准确率下降（Claude Haiku 4.5低于90%）。

<h3 id="403">[403] Early Cue Precision Shapes Visual Shortcut Learning in Controlled Cue-Manipulation Benchmarks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30344](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30344) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chanho Park, Woochan Lee, Janyeong Oh, Geongho Gong, Minshu Kim, Yeachan Kwak, Seongim Choi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Visual Shortcut, Shape-Texture Bias, Robustness Evaluation
- **Summary**: 问题：视觉分类器依赖低级线索，在线索冲突时失败；早期线索精度是否影响失败模式。方法：在合成任务、数字训练和CIFAR-10上操纵纹理-标签匹配概率。结果：低精度线索无法替代线索解耦；高精度纹理导致冲突准确率大幅下降。

<h3 id="404">[404] DRIFT: Difficulty Routing Self-DIstillation with Rhythm-Gated Exploration and Success BuFfer Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30345](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30345) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haisen Luo, Yiwei Liu, Haoning Wang, Dan Liu, Junxi Yin, Haotian Wang, Lei Zhang, Xiaoyu Tian, Shuaiting Chen, Yuansheng Song, Baoyan Guo, Xiongfei Yan, Bolan Yang, Chengwei Liu, Ming Cui, Jiong Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Self-Training, Curriculum Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：大语言模型缺乏稳定自我改进机制，现有方法过度优化简单问题。方法：提出DRIFT框架，包含难度路由和节奏门控，结合成功缓冲区和两阶段课程学习。结果：在五个基准上平均79.5%，超过GRPO（9.5%）和SDPO（7.5%），ToolUse达79.2%。

<h3 id="405">[405] FFAvatar: Feed-Forward 4D Head Avatar Reconstruction from Sparse Portrait Images</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30347](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30347) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jianjiang Yao, Ke Xian, Renxiang Dai, Robert Caiming Qiu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Gaussian Splatting, 4D Reconstruction, Novel View Synthesis
- **Summary**: 问题：从稀疏肖像图像快速构建高质量4D头部头像。方法：基于Transformer的3D高斯框架，使用交替注意力、稀疏到密集学习和运动细化模块。结果：高效生成高保真、可控头像，具备优越灵活性和身份一致性渲染。

<h3 id="406">[406] Residual-Guided Expert Specialization for Incomplete Multimodal Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30355](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30355) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seunghun Baek, Jihwan Park, Jaeyoon Sim, Minjae Jeong, Hoseok Lee, Won Hwa Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Missing Modality, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Summary**: 问题：现实预测系统常缺失模态，现有方法无法充分利用不完备模态的偏差。方法：提出MARS框架，基于残差信号引导专家专业化，并采用差异感知噪声正则化。结果：在分类和分割任务上超越基线，计算高效。

<h3 id="407">[407] ReactiveBFM: Reactive Closed-Loop Motion Planning Towards Universal Humanoid Whole-Body Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30362](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30362) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiao Chen, Weishuai Zeng, Xiaojie Niu, Zirui Wang, Jianan Li, Huayi Wang, Furui Xu, Jiahe Chen, Weixiang Zhong, Lihe Ding, Kailin Li, Jiangmiao Pang, Tai Wang, Tianfan Xue, Jingbo Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Humanoid Robot, Motion Planning, RL Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 问题：行为基础模型仅执行预定动作，无法应对环境变化。方法：提出ReactiveBFM，包含调度前缀采样课程、异步重规划和轨迹分块。结果：在严重扰动下成功率达93.1%，零样本实现移动目标追踪。

<h3 id="408">[408] Set-Inclusive Uncertainty Modeling for Robust Brain Tumor Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30374](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30374) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seunghun Baek, Jihwan Park, Jaeyoon Sim, Hoseok Lee, Seungjoo Lee, Won Hwa Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical Image Segmentation, Uncertainty Estimation, Missing Modality
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/atlas-sky/SIUM)
- **Summary**: 问题：多模态MRI分割中缺失模态导致不确定性。方法：将表示建模为高斯分布，方差反映缺失证据；引入集合包含策略排序不确定性。结果：在BraTS 2018/2020上优于基线。

<h3 id="409">[409] A Stochastic--Geometric Theory of Scaling Laws in Grokking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30388](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30388) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Róisín Luo, Christian Gagné, Jonas Ngnawé, Ihsan Ullah, Karyn Morrissey
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Grokking, Scaling Laws, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 问题：神经网络的延迟泛化（Grokking）机制不明。方法：理论刻画Adam优化下的壳-核拓扑结构，利用停时理论分析过渡时间。结果：推导出学习率、批大小和L2正则化的缩放定律，得到实验验证。

<h3 id="410">[410] Model Predictive Current Control with Harmonic Correction for Single-Phase AC-DC EV Charging</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30397](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30397) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Changhong Li, Bharathkumar Hegde, Biswajit Basu, Shreejith Shanker
- **Subjects**: eess.SY; cs.AI; cs.AR
- **Tags**: Electric Vehicles, Power Management, Control Theory
- **Venue**: RTSI'26
- **Summary**: 问题：电动汽车充电器谐波问题，现有MPCC离散控制且谐波抑制有限。方法：提出占空比预测MPCC，结合实时谐波估计参考。结果：THDi从11.47%降至2.85%。

<h3 id="411">[411] Beyond IID: How General Are Tabular Foundation Models, Really?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30410](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30410) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lennart Purucker, Andrej Tschalzev, Nick Erickson, Gioia Blayer, David Holzmüller, Alan Arazi, Alexander Pfefferle, Mustafa Tajjar, Gaël Varoquaux, Frank Hutter
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, Foundation Model, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 问题：表格基础模型评估局限于IID任务，挑战性场景被排除。方法：构建BeyondArena基准和Data Foundry框架，评估11个模型在142个数据集上。结果：TFMs在小型IID数据上优秀，但在非IID、大型和高维数据上树模型和深度学习模型仍占优。

<h3 id="412">[412] Can LLMs Rank? A Tale of Triads and Triage</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30412](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30412) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gaurab Pokharel, Shafkat Farabi, Patrick J. Fowler, Sanmay Das
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Social Choice Theory, Fairness
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM用于高风险排序决策，需要评估其一致性。方法：使用一致系数ζ和Kendall's τ，在无家可归者住房分配和急诊分诊中测试。结果：不同LLM一致性差异大，提供实践指南。

<h3 id="413">[413] Beyond Point Estimates for Glaucoma Visual Field Forecasting with Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30417](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30417) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marta Colmenar Herrera, Pablo Márquez Neila, Şerife Seda Kucur Ergünay, Martin S. Zinkernagel, Raphael Sznitman
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Diffusion Model, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 问题：青光眼视野预测具有不确定性，现有方法仅给出点估计。方法：使用条件去噪扩散模型生成未来视野分布。结果：生成校准良好的分布，点估计精度达到最优。

<h3 id="414">[414] Transformer Architectures as Complete Bayes Processes: A Formal Proof in the Measure-Theoretic Kernel Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30440](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30440) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haobo Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Probabilistic Inference, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 问题：Transformer架构是否实现贝叶斯推理。方法：在测度论核框架中给出形式化证明，从贝叶斯Transformer到多层堆栈。结果：证明在贝叶斯联合条件成立时，Transformer前向计算等价于贝叶斯后验更新。

<h3 id="415">[415] Translating Natural Language to Strategic Temporal Specifications via LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30441](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30441) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marco Aruta, Francesco Improta, Vadim Malvone, Aniello Murano, Vladana Perlic
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Application, Multi-Agent System, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 问题：缺乏从自然语言生成多智能体系统规范（ATL/ATL*）的方法。方法：使用LLM翻译，创建并精调数据集。结果：微调的小模型（3-7B）与强少样本API基线相当，语义准确率0.84 vs 0.86。

<h3 id="416">[416] Collective cooperation without individual fidelity in LLM agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30454](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30454) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Henrique Ferraz de Arruda, Carlos Gracia Lázaro, Alberto Aleta, Yamir Moreno
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent-Based Model, Collective Intelligence
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM代理作为人类替代需验证行为保真度。方法：在囚徒困境实验中比较LLM与人类数据。结果：宏观合作动态相似，但微观异质性和条件合作模式不同；随机代理改善部分微观测度。

<h3 id="417">[417] Field Order Should Not Matter: Permutation-Invariant Embedding Model Fine-Tuning for Structured Metadata Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30473](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30473) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aivin V. Solatorio, Olivier Dupriez, Rafael Macalaba
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.LG; econ.GN
- **Tags**: Fine-Tuning, Embedding Alignment
- **Summary**: 结构化元数据检索中，标准微调使模型依赖字段位置顺序，导致索引重建时检索质量下降。提出置换不变微调（PI-FT），在训练时随机采样字段顺序和字段丢弃，使模型关注标签而非位置。实验表明，PI-FT将顺序变化导致的nDCG@10损失从7.4点降至0.2点，微调后的118M参数模型在DevDataBench上超越text-embedding-3-large（0.707 vs 0.556）。

<h3 id="418">[418] COHORT: Collaborative Orchestration for Hardening via Offensive Replay on Emulated Topologies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30479](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30479) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chen Frydman, Aviram Zilberman, Rubin Krief, Abed Showgan, Andres Murillo, Sekiya Motoyoshi, Asaf Shabtai, Yuval Elovici, Rami Puzis
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Cybersecurity
- **Summary**: 企业网络对抗缓解通常需要数周专家工作且无法在生产环境测试。提出COHORT，一个多智能体LLM工作流，在GNS3模拟器上自动生成、实现和优化缓解方案，通过攻击重放评估有效性。在三个拓扑和四个攻击场景中，46.7%的缓解方案成功阻断攻击且保持连通性，是单智能体基线的4.4倍。

<h3 id="419">[419] Situation Perception: A Necessary Primitive to Artificial Superintelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30481](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30481) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziqin Yuan, Jaymari Chua
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.ET
- **Tags**: AGI Architecture, Cognitive Science
- **Summary**: 当前大语言模型是出色的统计模式匹配器，但缺乏通用智能，无法构建可能的内部模拟。提出“情境感知”作为通向超级智能的必要能力，包括抽象预测、长期压缩记忆和主动学习。该论文为概念性工作，提出了衡量进展的测试标准。

<h3 id="420">[420] SIMAX: A Scalable and Interpretable Framework for Multi-Fidelity and Annotated Clinician-Patient Dialogue Simulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30491](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30491) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhuhan Bao, Rui Yang, Bohao Yang, Zhiyi Liu, Sicheng Shu, Ruio Heerschap, Le Li, Doris Yang, Elisabeth Bond, Haoyuan Wang, Nicoleta Economou-Zavlanos, Joshua M. Biro, Matthew McDermott, Nan Liu, Anand Chowdhury, Kai Sun, Kathryn Pollak, Ed Hammond, Chuan Hong
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Dialogue System, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 临床沟通数据的人工编码成本高且难以规模化，真实对话和标签数据稀缺。提出SIMAX框架，从预定义场景、人物和沟通行为生成受控的临床对话数据，并带有行为标注。生成3,388个对话，UTMOS 3.03，WER 0.07，MOS 4.67，临床真实度评分3.00，下游评估验证了其有效性。

<h3 id="421">[421] McMg: A Learned Phase-Space Multi-channel Multigrid Preconditioner for Helmholtz Equation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30495](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30495) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiwei Jia, Xinliang Liu, Juntao Wang, Jinchao Xu
- **Subjects**: math.NA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Neural Operator, Scientific Computing
- **Summary**: 高频异构亥姆霍兹方程求解因算子不定性和相位误差而困难。提出多通道多重网格预条件子McMg，在粗化物理空间时通过通道维度保留局部波信息，结合神经算子和自适应平滑器。数值实验表明，在三维高频高对比度问题上，McMg所需迭代次数和时间均显著少于经典方法和神经预条件子。

<h3 id="422">[422] On the Faithfulness of Post-Hoc Concept Bottleneck Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30498](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30498) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Laines Schmalwasser, Jan Blunk, Niklas Penzel, Julia Niebling, Joachim Denzler
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Explainable AI, Concept Bottleneck Model
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 事后概念瓶颈模型可能学到语义无意义的概念，但标准准确率评估无法识别。分析了协变量偏移和标签噪声导致的概念不忠，并引入新指标解耦概念忠实性和预测准确率。实验证明新指标能检测出准确率评估遗漏的不忠行为。

<h3 id="423">[423] Informational Frustration in Neural Manifolds: Shannon Bottlenecks and the Limits of Learnability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30512](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30512) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Srinivasa Rao P., Vangmayi P Reddy
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CG
- **Tags**: Learning Theory, Grokking, Information Theory
- **Summary**: 过参数化网络泛化性能良好，但经典学习理论预测过拟合，存在理论鸿沟。提出了熵可学习性界和香农-拓扑瓶颈定理，将突击学习解释为熵释放，并设计了熵梯度下降算法。该论文为理论框架，无定量实验结果。

<h3 id="424">[424] Learning from Mistakes: Rollout-Retrieval Lifelong Policy Learning for Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30537](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30537) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cheng Gong, Haoyang Wang, Chao Lu, Zirui Li, Jianwei Gong
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Continual Learning, Imitation Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Engibacter/R2LPL)
- **Summary**: 自动驾驶策略缺乏从闭环场景中纠正和保留错误的机制。提出滚动-检索终身策略学习（R2LPL），从可恢复的策略错误中检索校正目标，并通过终身学习积累知识。在nuPlan基准上，将中等性能的规划器提升到最先进水平，尤其在长尾困难的Test14-hard分割上。

<h3 id="425">[425] TRACE: Temporal Relationship-Aware Conversational Entrainment Detection in Dyadic Speech</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30543](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30543) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sathvik Manikantan Napa Ugandhar, Hao Zhang, Alison Gunzler, Yuzhe Wang, Thomas Thebaud, Georgi Tinchev, Venkatesh Ravichandran, Laureano Moro-Velázquez
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Emotion Recognition, Affective Computing
- **Summary**: 情感感染检测对语音AI智能体很重要，但缺乏时序建模和数据集。提出了DyadEE数据集和TRACE框架，将对话交互建模为有序的声学嵌入序列，结合上下文和关系信息。在DyadEE上达到97.01%的准确率。

<h3 id="426">[426] To Tab or Not to Tab: Measuring Critical Engagement in AI Code Completion Tools Using Behavioral Signals and Attention Checks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30549](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30549) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jessica Hutchison, Ian Tyler Applebaum, Kenneth Angelikas, Kush Rakesh Patel, Phuoc Nguyen, Antonio Lazaro, Nicholas Rucinski, Rahad Arman Nabid, Stephen MacNeil
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Venue**: ITiCSE 2026
- **Summary**: 学生在使用AI代码补全工具时缺乏批判性评估。开发了Clover工具，记录交互行为并插入注意力检查，建立行为指标分类。发现更高的Tab接受率与注意力检查表现负相关，更长的停留时间与表现正相关。

<h3 id="427">[427] TraceLab: Characterizing Coding Agent Workloads for LLM Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30560](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30560) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kan Zhu, Mathew Jacob, Chenxi Ma, Yi Pan, Stephanie Wang, Arvind Krishnamurthy, Baris Kasikci
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.PF
- **Tags**: LLM Serving, LLM Agent, Code Generation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/uw-syfi/TraceLab.git;)
- **Summary**: 高效服务编码智能体需要理解实际工作负载模式，但缺乏相关数据。收集并发布了约4,300个编码会话的轨迹（来自Claude Code和Codex），包含350,000个LLM步骤和430,000次工具调用。分析显示长自主循环、长上下文短输出、多样化工具调用和高前缀缓存命中率等特点。

<h3 id="428">[428] A Multi-task Mixture of Experts Framework for Malware Classification, Packing Detection, and Family Attribution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30572](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30572) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jithin S., Roshin Sleeba C., Anvin Mariya P. B., Asmitha K. A., Vinod P., Serena Nicolazzo, Antonino Nocera
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Mixture-of-Experts, Multi-Task Learning
- **Summary**: 恶意软件分类因二进制异构、加壳和罕见家族分布而困难。提出基于混合专家（MoE）的多任务框架，同时进行恶意软件族分类、加壳检测和恶意/良性识别。Multi-Gate MoE模型达到0.9744的综合检测率，失败率2.56%，对突变具有鲁棒性。

<h3 id="429">[429] Beyond 2D Matching: A Unified Single-Stage Framework for Geometry-Aware Cross-View Object Geo-Localization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30576](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30576) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Liyao Wang, Ruipu Wu, Haojun Xu, Lei Shi, Linjiang Huang, Si Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cross-View Localization, 3D Vision, Object Detection
- **Summary**: 跨视角目标地理定位依赖2D匹配，缺乏几何元数据和多样提示。构建了含22万对地面/无人机-卫星图像的数据集CVOGL-Bench，并提出GAGeo框架，利用3D基础模型联合预测边界框、分割掩码和相机位姿。性能显著优于现有方法，并实现零样本地面-无人机定位。

<h3 id="430">[430] Words Speak Louder Than Code: Investigating Cognitive Heuristics in LLM-Based Code Vulnerability Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30587](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30587) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Asif Shahriar, Hongyu Cai, Hadjer Benkraouda, Gang Wang, Z. Berkay Celik
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Adversarial Robustness, Software Testing
- **Summary**: 大语言模型在代码漏洞检测中易受认知启发影响，但此前无研究探讨。提出控制框架，固定代码仅改变上下文以触发光环、框架和锚定效应。评估8个模型，平均易感性：框架效应33.2%，锚定效应23.5%，光环效应18.4%；概念攻击可抑制97%漏洞。

<h3 id="431">[431] MESA: Prioritizing Vulnerable Communication Channels for Securing Multi-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30602](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30602) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kunyang Li, Kyle Domico, Jonathan Gregory, Patrick McDaniel
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Cybersecurity
- **Summary**: 多智能体系统通信信道攻击影响不均匀，需主动优先保护关键边。提出Mesa，基于图度量和动态探针的无标签框架，排序边关键性。与经验攻击成功率的相关性达到斯皮尔曼ρ=+0.60；监控前10%边可拦截约3倍于随机选择的攻击。

<h3 id="432">[432] C$^{2}$R: Cross-sample Consistency Regularization Mitigates Feature Splitting and Absorption in Sparse Autoencoders</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30609](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30609) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haoran Jin, Xiting Wang, Shijie Ren, Hong Xie, Defu Lian
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Representation Learning, Sparse Autoencoder
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/hr-jin/Cross-sample-Consistency-Regularization)
- **Summary**: 稀疏自编码器存在特征分裂和吸收问题，损害潜在表示可靠性。提出跨样本一致性正则化C2R，通过惩罚方向相似潜在变量的共激活，鼓励语义特征由统一潜在表示。有效缓解分裂和吸收，同时保持重构保真度。

<h3 id="433">[433] Optimization Dynamics Imprint Semantic Specificity in Contrastive Embedding Norms</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30625](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30625) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziwei Su, Junyu Ren, Victor Veitch
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG; math.OC
- **Tags**: Contrastive Learning
