- **Summary**: 该论文针对对比嵌入模型的向量范数被忽略但实际蕴含语义信息（如概念特异性）的问题，通过分析优化动力学，推导出范数自然编码语义信息的解析公式，并展示了该信号可作为免费校准工具。

<h3 id="434">[434] Pessimism's Paradox: Conservative Offline Training Amplifies Reward Hacking During Online Adaptation in Reasoning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30627](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30627) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Subramanyam Sahoo, Aman Chadha, Vinija Jain, Divya Chaudhary
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: RLHF, Reward Hacking
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 workshop on Decision-Making from Offline Datasets to Online Adaptation: Black-Box Optimization to Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文挑战了保守离线训练能安全在线适应的普遍观点，通过实验和机制分析发现，高保守性反而会加剧奖励黑客行为，并提出了校准的保守性水平以平衡对齐与鲁棒性。

<h3 id="435">[435] GROW$^2$: Grounding Which and Where for Robot Tool Use</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30632](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30632) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhong Deng, Yuyao Liu, David Hsu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Robotics, Tool Learning, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 为解决机器人创造性工具使用中的开放世界功能定位问题，提出GROW^2框架，利用VLM进行语义层工具与部件选择，再通过视觉基础模型进行几何层定位，在基准和真实实验中实现了零样本泛化。

<h3 id="436">[436] LeVo 2: Stable and Melodious Song Generation via Hierarchical Representation Modeling and Progressive Post-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.30642](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30642) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="449">[449] Learning How to Use Tools, Not Just When: Pattern-Aware Tool-Integrated Reasoning</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Tool Learning, LLM Reasoning

<h3 id="450">[450] Echoes of Human Malice in Agents: Benchmarking LLMs for Multi-Turn Online Harassment Attacks</h3>

- **Authors**: Trilok Padhi, Pinxian Lu, Abdulkadir Erol, Tanmay Sutar, Gauri Sharma, Mina Sonmez, Munmun De Choudhury, Ugur Kursuncu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Jailbreak Attack, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 现有越狱研究多关注单轮提示，而真实骚扰常涉及多轮交互。该论文构建了在线骚扰代理基准，包含合成多轮骚扰对话数据集、基于重复博弈论的多智能体模拟、三种越狱方法及混合评估框架。实验表明，越狱调优使骚扰成功率接近100%，且攻击后代理模仿人类攻击模式，如马基雅维利主义和精神变态模式。

<h3 id="451">[451] CostBench: Evaluating Multi-Turn Cost-Optimal Planning and Adaptation in Dynamic Environments for LLM Tool-Use Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.02734](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.02734) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiayu Liu, Cheng Qian, Zhaochen Su, Qing Zong, Shijue Huang, Bingxiang He, Yi R. Fung
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Tool Learning, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 当前LLM代理评估主要关注任务完成，忽视了资源效率和适应性。该论文提出CostBench基准，专注于经济规划和重规划能力，包含旅行规划任务和动态阻塞事件。实验发现，即使在静态环境下，GPT-5的最难任务精确匹配率低于75%，动态环境下性能下降约40%。

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; eess.IV; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Inverse Problem, Image Reconstruction
- **Summary**: 扩散逆问题求解器中先验仅提供初始化，重建主要靠似然项。该论文提出DAPS++，完全解耦扩散初始化与似然驱动优化，使似然直接引导推理。在多种图像恢复任务中实现了高计算效率和稳健重建性能。

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Energy Management, Reinforcement Learning, Agentic AI
- **Summary**: 建筑电网协同仿真因异构仿真器和工作流编排而困难。该论文提出AutoB2G代理框架，利用LLM代理将自然语言意图转化为可执行仿真流程。用户只需指定高层任务，即可自动生成复杂仿真管线，包括组件检索、组合、执行和迭代修复。

<h3 id="465">[465] SciVisAgentBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Scientific Data Analysis and Visualization Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.29139](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29139) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kuangshi Ai, Haichao Miao, Kaiyuan Tang, Nathaniel Gorski, Jianxin Sun, Guoxi Liu, Helgi I. Ingolfsson, David Lenz, Hanqi Guo, Hongfeng Yu, Teja Leburu, Michael Molash, Bei Wang, Tom Peterka, Chaoli Wang, Shusen Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.GR; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Benchmark, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 问题：缺乏评估科学可视化智能体的基准。方法：提出SciVisAgentBench，包含108个案例和多模态评估流程。结果：评估了代表性智能体，揭示了能力差距。

<h3 id="466">[466] QED: An Open-Source Multi-Agent System for Generating Mathematical Proofs on Open Problems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.24021](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24021) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chenyang An, Qihao Ye, Minghao Pan, Jiayaun Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; math.AP
- **Tags**: Automated Theorem Proving, Multi-Agent System
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/proofQED/QED)
- **Summary**: 问题：数学开放问题的自动证明困难。方法：提出多智能体系统QED，分离规划、证明和验证。结果：生成5篇原创研究，专家认为达到专业数学期刊水平。

<h3 id="467">[467] Exploring LLM Agent Designs and Interaction Modalities for Scientific Visualization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.27996](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27996) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jackson Vonderhorst, Kuangshi Ai, Haichao Miao, Shusen Liu, Chaoli Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.GR; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Computer-Use Agent, Scientific Visualization
- **Summary**: 问题：不同科学可视化智能体设计的性能差异。方法：比较三种设计（领域专用、计算机使用、通用编码）和交互模式。结果：通用编码智能体成功率高但成本高；领域专用智能体更高效但灵活性差；持久记忆提升重复任务性能。

<h3 id="468">[468] To Use AI as Dice of Possibilities with Timing Computation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01134](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01134) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Reasoning, World Model, Temporal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 问题：基于名词的建模无法处理时序和可能性空间。方法：提出包含可能性空间、时序计算和因果事实的框架。结果：在乳腺癌EHR数据上实现自动轨迹发现和反事实时序推理。

<h3 id="469">[469] NEURON: A Neuro-symbolic System for Grounded Clinical Explainability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01189](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01189) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Anuradha Chandrasekaran, Dimitrios Zikos, Mutlu Mete, Alan Pang, Brady D. Lund, Kewei Sha
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Explainable AI, Neurosymbolic AI
- **Summary**: 问题：临床AI模型缺乏可解释性。方法：提出NEURON，结合SNOMED CT本体、机器学习、RAG和SHAP。结果：AUC从0.74-0.77提升到0.84-0.88，人类对齐指标从0.50提升到0.85。

<h3 id="470">[470] SearchSkill: Teaching LLMs to Use Search Tools with Evolving Skill Banks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09038](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09038) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jinchao Hu, Meizhi Zhong, Kehai Chen, Min Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Question Answering, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM在开放式问答中使用搜索时查询质量差。方法：提出SearchSkill，通过可复用的搜索技能进行显式查询规划，并维护进化技能库。结果：在知识密集型QA基准上提升精确匹配，减少重复查询，增加原子跳跃查询。

<h3 id="471">[471] Towards Human-Level Book-Writing Capability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17064](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17064) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jan Zierstek, Matteo Batelic, Maya Medjad, Tim Schönenberger
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Creative Writing, Story Generation, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM在创意写作中风格单一、解释过多。方法：从公版小说构建多分辨率规划脚手架，训练模型从提示逐步扩展为书籍文本。结果：在写作质量上超越GPT-5.5和Claude Opus 4.8。

<h3 id="472">[472] Geo-Expert: Towards Expert-Level Geological Reasoning via Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24844](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24844) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 问题：通用LLM在地质推理中容易产生幻觉。方法：通过LoRA微调定制地质指令数据集，构建Geo-Expert模型。结果：8B模型在地质推理上超越70B通用模型和GPT-4o，32B接近前沿推理模型。

<h3 id="473">[473] Severity-Aware Curriculum Learning with Multi-Model Response Selection for Medical Text Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05510) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ahmed Alansary, Molham Mohamed, Ali Hamdi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Curriculum Learning, Text Generation
- **Summary**: 问题：医疗问答模型在不同严重程度案例上表现不一致。方法：提出严重程度感知的课程学习与多模型响应选择框架。结果：BERTScore从86.71%提升到90.30%。

<h3 id="474">[474] Inference-Time Conformal Reasoning with Valid Factuality Control for Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08831](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08831) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Conformal Prediction, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM多步推理中事实不确定性具有结构性。方法：提出推理时共形推理框架ITCR，将CP集成到推理图生成中。结果：验证了经验覆盖的有效性，推理时校准图生成比事后剪枝更准确。

<h3 id="475">[475] Business World Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10044](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10044) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Cecil Pang, Hiroki Sayama
- **Subjects**: cs.AI

<h3 id="476">[476] Agents-K1: Towards Agent-native Knowledge Orchestration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.13669](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13669) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zongsheng Cao, Bihao Zhan, Jinxin Shi, Jiong Wang, Fangchen Yu, Zhijie Zhong, Zijie Guo, Tianshuo Peng, Zhuo Liu, Yi Xie, Xiang Zhuang, Shengji Tang, Yue Fan, Runmin Ma, Shiyang Feng, Xiangchao Yan, Anran Liu, Peng Ye, Wenlong Zhang, Shufei Zhang, Chunfeng Song, Fenghua Ling, Jie Zhou, Liang He, Bo Zhang, Lei Bai
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Information Extraction, Scientific Reasoning
- **Summary**: 问题：研究智能体忽视科学知识编排。方法：提出Agents-K1，将文档转化为智能体原生知识图谱，包含多模态解析器、GRPO训练的4B模型和三源接口。结果：处理246万论文，在信息抽取、知识图谱构建和多跳推理上表现优异。

<h3 id="477">[477] Artificial Intelligence Index Report 2026</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15708](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15708) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sha Sajadieh, Loredana Fattorini, Raymond Perrault, Yolanda Gil, Vanessa Parli, Lapo Santarlasci, Juan Pava, Nestor Maslej, Russ Altman, Erik Brynjolfsson, Carla Brodley, Jack Clark, Virginia Dignum, Vipin Kumar, James Landay, Terah Lyons, James Manyika, Juan Carlos Niebles, Yoav Shoham, Elham Tabassi, Russell Wald, Toby Walsh, Dan Weld
- **Subjects**: cs.AI

<h3 id="478">[478] RoboPIN: Grounded Embodied Reasoning via Pinned Chain-of-Thought</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15753](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15753) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yaoting Huang, Yifu Yuan, Linqi Han, Chengwen Li, Shuoheng Zhang, Xianze Yao, Hongyao Tang, Yan Zheng, Jianye Hao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Vision-Language Model, Chain-of-Thought
- **Summary**: 问题：具身推理中视觉基础不一致。方法：提出Pinned Chain-of-Thought (PinCoT)，通过推理锚点绑定实体，训练RoboPIN。结果：4B参数模型在14个基准上平均提升12%超过7B基线。

<h3 id="479">[479] Looking Is Not Picking: An Attention-Segment Account of Tool-Selection Failures in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16364](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16364) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shiyang Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM代理错误调用工具，直觉认为工具选择失败是由于工具拥挤。方法：通过注意力分析发现模型注意正确工具但选择错误，定位到决策读出阶段。结果：基于注意力的无参考选择器缩小了与有参考基线的差距，在BFCL上提升11.9点。

<h3 id="480">[480] ITNet: A Learnable Integral Transform That Subsumes Convolution, Attention, and Recurrence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.19538](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19538) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ashim Dhor, Rasel Mondal, Pin-Yu Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Neural Architecture, Deep Learning Theory, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 问题：卷积、注意力、循环等网络架构数学上不同。方法：提出积分变换网络ITNet，以可学习核统一这些架构。结果：在ImageNet、GLUE等任务上匹配或超过专用基线。

<h3 id="481">[481] When Web Agents Finish but Still Fail: Reproducible Triggers and Trace Diagnostics for Parallel Web Exploration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20724](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20724) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Aagam Sogani, Botao Rui, Swetha Vaidyanathan, Rishi Agarwal, Minghao Yan, Shivaram Venkataraman
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Web Agent, Agent Evaluation
- **Summary**: 当前长周期Web智能体在完成最终答案后仍可能失败，隐藏于最终答案评估之下。本文提出Parallel WebBench基准，包含1679条验证记录，并训练WebExplorer风格智能体（GRPO训练）。最佳模型将完成率从50.7%提升至96.0%，元素F1从0.2489提升至0.4529，但二元准确率仍远低于完成率。轨迹分析识别出三种持久失败模式：上下文边界搜索循环、部分答案提前终止、证据检索后的合成崩溃。

<h3 id="482">[482] Governance Decay: How Context Compaction Silently Erases Safety Constraints in Long-Horizon LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22528](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22528) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shiyang Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Safety
- **Summary**: 现代LLM智能体依赖上下文压缩来管理长会话，但压缩可能导致安全约束被静默移除，造成治理衰减。本文引入ConstraintRot基准，在1323个片段中，违反率从0%上升到30%（某些模型达59%）。提出Constraint Pinning方法，将治理约束隔离于有损压缩，恢复违反率至0%。

<h3 id="483">[483] Transferability for General Reasoning: An Automated Curriculum for Multi-Domain RLVR</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.25178](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25178) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yongjin Yang, Jiarui Liu, Yinghui He, Lechen Zhang, Bernhard Schölkopf, Zhijing Jin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: RLVR, Multi-Domain Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/YangYongJin/transfer-aware-curriculum)
- **Summary**: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在多领域推理中，训练课程通常固定或手工调整。本文提出迁移感知课程（TAC），利用领域优势估计跨领域可迁移性，在六领域推理套件上，在Qwen3-1.7B和Llama3.2-3B上取得最佳平均准确率，比仅可学习性方法提升高达2.8个点。

<h3 id="484">[484] The Verification Horizon: No Silver Bullet for Coding Agent Rewards</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.26300](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26300) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Binghai Wang, Chenlong Zhang, Dayiheng Liu, Jiajun Zhang, Jiawei Chen, Mingze Li, Mouxiang Chen, Rongyao Fang, Siyuan Zhang, Xuwu Wang, Yuheng Jing, Zeyao Ma, Zeyu Cui
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Agent, Reward Model
- **Summary**: 对于编码智能体，验证比生成更困难。本文从可扩展性、忠实性和鲁棒性三个维度刻画验证信号，研究四种奖励构建（测试验证器、规则验证器、用户验证器、自动化验证器）。实验表明，固定奖励函数无法随策略能力增长保持有效，验证必须与生成器共同进化。

<h3 id="485">[485] Unbiased Canonical Set-Valued Oracles Via Lattice Theory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.26418](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26418) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jobst Heitzig
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Performativity, Decision Theory, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 非智能体预言机报告未来事件概率时存在表现性（performativity）问题，且自洽报告不唯一。本文通过报告信念集（credal set）替代单概率分布，利用格论中的Knaster-Tarski最小不动点作为规范答案。证明了存在性、自洽性及非空性，并展示在非表现性问题下退化为经典点答案。

<h3 id="486">[486] JD Oxygen AI Item Center (Oxygen AIIC) V1: An Industrial-Scale LLM/VLM-Centric Solution for Item Understanding, Management, and Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28070](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28070) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Oxygen AIIC, Chan Long, Chao Liu, Chaofan Chen, Chaohui Dong, Chunyuan Guo, Danping Liu, Debin Liu, Deping Xiang, Fulai Xu, Guangyue Liu, Hao Li, Huichun Hu, Jian Yang, Jianan Wang, Jianbo Zhao, Jiaoyang Li, Jiaxing Wang, Jinglong Li, Jinjin Guo, Jun Fang, Jun Liu, Kai Zhou, Li Wang, Lili Gao, Liying Chen, Luning Yang, Mengdi Zhou, Pengzhang Liu, Qi Lv, Qianyun Wang, Qixia Jiang, Ruyue Li, Shimu Liang, Shuxing Wang, Sijie Zhang, Siqi Li, Tianhao Gao, Wang Ke, Weihu Huang, Wencan Lai, Wenjie Zhang, Xiaohui Zhang, Xiaojing Dong, Ya Liu, Yifeng Zhang, Yixiang Wang, Yongtai Zhang, Yongyi Liao, Zhaoru Chen, Zhen Chen, Zhiyong Ma, Zhiyuan Liu, Zhongwei Liu, Ziyan Xing
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: E-Commerce, LLM Application, Industrial AI
- **Summary**: 京东构建了工业级平台Oxygen AIIC，基于LLM/VLM进行商品知识生产与服务。核心包括高效人机协作的本体工程、语义搜索与判别（S2D）知识识别架构、自进化商品理解模型（精度94.2%，召回82.8%），以及统一商品通道。覆盖数万品类，每天处理数亿商品更新，搜索流量覆盖率80.4%，商品信息质量问题下降37%。

<h3 id="487">[487] Ensemble Learning Based Classification Algorithm Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2101.05993](https://arxiv.org/abs/2101.05993) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Guangtao Wang, Qinbao Song, Xiaoyan Zhu, Jiao Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Meta-Learning, Ensemble Learning
- **Summary**: 针对数据集选择合适分类算法的问题，现有推荐模型通常使用单一学习器和单类元特征。本文提出多视图集成元学习框架，通过异构元特征组构建基础推荐模型，并利用准确率和多样性感知的集成策略。在1090个分类问题上评估，使用13种算法和5类元特征，结果表明集成方法在排名损失、平均精度和首位推荐精度上持续优于单模型。

<h3 id="488">[488] Granular-ball computing: an efficient, robust, and interpretable adaptive multi-granularity representation and computation method</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2304.11171](https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.11171) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shuyin Xia, Guoyin Wang, Xinbo Gao, Xiaoyu Lian, Hongzhi Kuai
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Granular Computing, Interpretability, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 为克服点输入和过细计算的局限，提出粒度球计算作为一种新的人工智能学习范式。使用不同大小的超球体（粒度球）作为介观表示单元，自适应拟合任意数据分布。系统综述了粒度球在监督学习、无监督学习、近似表示、深度学习、图学习和跨学科研究中的进展。

<h3 id="489">[489] TERC: A Transfer Entropy Redundancy Criterion for State Variable Selection in Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2401.11512](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.11512) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Charles Westphal, Stephen Hailes, Mirco Musolesi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IT
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, State Representation, Information Theory
- **Venue**: TMLR
- **Summary**: 强化学习中状态变量选择至关重要。本文提出基于信息论的转移熵冗余准则（TERC），确定训练期间从可观测状态变量到动作的熵转移。基于TERC的算法可以排除不影响策略的变量，得到紧凑状态表示，减少推理时间达2.6倍。在表格Q学习、演员-评论家和PPO等算法上验证有效性。

<h3 id="490">[490] Assortment Planning with Sponsored Products</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2402.06158](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.06158) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shaojie Tang, Shuzhang Cai, Jing Yuan, Kai Han
- **Subjects**: cs.DS; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: E-Commerce, Combinatorial Optimization
- **Venue**: COCOON 2024
- **Summary**: 在零售中，赞助商品的存在使品种规划面临新挑战。本文将赞助商品下的品种规划问题建模为组合优化，目标是在考虑赞助商品战略放置下最大化期望收入。论文被COCOON 2024接收。

<h3 id="491">[491] SSM Meets Video Diffusion Models: Efficient Long-Term Video Generation with Structured State Spaces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2403.07711](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07711) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuta Oshima, Shohei Taniguchi, Masahiro Suzuki, Yutaka Matsuo
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Diffusion Model, State Space Model
- **Venue**: ICLR 2024
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/shim0114/SSM-Meets-Video-Diffusion-Models)
- **Summary**: 视频扩散模型使用注意力层提取时间特征，但计算成本随序列长度二次增长。本文提出使用状态空间模型（如Mamba）作为时间特征提取器，发现双向SSM比单向更有效。在长视频数据集上，SSM模型在256帧下用更少内存达到与注意力模型相当的FVD。

<h3 id="492">[492] Causality for Tabular Data Synthesis: A High-Order Structure Causal Benchmark Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2406.08311](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08311) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zineb Senane, Axel Karlsson, Lele Cao, Oleg Smirnov, Cheng Zhang, Sahar Asadi, Hedvig Kjellström, Gustav Eje Henter, Ruibo Tu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, Data Synthesis, Causal Inference
- **Summary**: 现有表格数据合成评估依赖低阶统计量和下游任务，缺乏多变量因果关系衡量。本文提出基于高阶结构因果信息的基准框架，生成基准数据集并评估合成模型捕获因果结构的能力。实验揭示了理想与实际的差距。

<h3 id="493">[493] ProSpec RL: Plan Ahead, then Execute</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2407.21359](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21359) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Liangliang Liu, Yi Guan, BoRan Wang, Rujia Shen, Yi Lin, Chaoran Kong, Lian Yan, Jingchi Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Model-Based RL, Decision Making
- **Summary**: 模型无关RL无法主动预见未来场景。本文提出前瞻性RL方法ProSpec，通过动态模型预测未来状态（"想象状态"），并引入循环一致性约束，使智能体评估并选择最优动作。在DMControl基准上取得显著提升。但论文因分析存在重大错误被作者撤回。

<h3 id="494">[494] Beyond Spectral Decomposition: Bayesian Contrastive Learning and its Non-negative Formulation via Factor Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2407.21740](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21740) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhibin Duan, Tiansheng Wen, Yifei Wang, Chen Zhu, Bo Chen, Mingyuan Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Contrastive Learning, Factor Analysis, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 因子分析是贝叶斯矩阵分解，但表达能力有限。对比学习与矩阵分解具有数学等价性。本文提出对比因子分析框架，结合因子分析与对比学习，并扩展到非负版本以学习解耦表示。实验展示了表达性、鲁棒性、可解释性和不确定性估计。

<h3 id="495">[495] Interpretable Clustering: A Survey</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2409.00743](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.00743) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lianyu Hu, Mudi Jiang, Junjie Dong, Xinying Liu, Zengyou He
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretable Clustering, Interpretability, Clustering
- **Summary**: 聚类算法研究常以牺牲可解释性为代价追求准确性和效率。本文综述了可解释聚类算法的现状，确定区分不同方法的关键标准，并提供了分类法。在医疗、金融等高风险领域，透明可解释的聚类结果至关重要。

<h3 id="496">[496] FLAME 3 Dataset: Unleashing the Power of Radiometric Thermal UAV Imagery for Wildfire Management</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2412.02831](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.02831) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bryce Hopkins, Leo ONeill, Michael Marinaccio, Mobin Habibpour, Eric Rowell, Russell Parsons, Sarah Flanary, Irtija Nazim, Carl Seielstad, Fatemeh Afghah
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Dataset, Wildfire Detection
- **Summary**: 无人机辐射热成像传感器提供每像素温度估计，但缺乏可用数据。本文提出收集和处理同步可见光谱与辐射热成像的方法，并发布FLAME 3数据集，包含侧对侧可见光和辐射热图像。旨在促进野火检测、分割和评估等机器学习模型。

<h3 id="497">[497] XRAG: eXamining the Core -- Benchmarking Foundational Components in Advanced Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2412.15529](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15529) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qili Zhang, Qianren Mao, Yangyifei Luo, Yashuo Luo, Hanwen Hao, Zhilong Cao, Weifeng Jiang, Zhijun Chen, Junnan Liu, Feng Yan, Xiaolong Wang, Jinlong Zhang, Zhenting Huang, Zhixing Tan, Jie Sun, Bo Li, Jianxin Li, Philip S. Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 该论文针对RAG系统中核心组件性能评估缺乏系统性基准的问题，提出了XRAG开源模块化代码库，涵盖检索前、检索、检索后和生成四个阶段，系统评估了先进RAG模块的性能，并识别了潜在故障点。关键结果：提供了综合基准和针对常见故障点的优化方案。

<h3 id="498">[498] CASE-Bench: Context-Aware SafEty Benchmark for Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2501.14940](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.14940) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Guangzhi Sun, Xiao Zhan, Shutong Feng, Philip C. Woodland, Jose Such
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ICML 2025
- **Summary**: 针对当前LLM安全基准忽略查询上下文的问题，提出CASE-Bench，基于情境完整性理论为不同类别查询分配上下文，并通过充分统计检验评估LLM的安全响应。关键结果：发现上下文显著影响人类判断(p\<0.0001)，商业模型在安全上下文中存在不当拒绝。

<h3 id="499">[499] Bridging Neural Networks and Wireless Systems with MIMO-OFDM Semantic Communications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2501.16726](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.16726) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hanju Yoo, Dongha Choi, Yonghwi Kim, Yoontae Kim, Songkuk Kim, Chan-Byoung Chae, Robert W. Heath Jr
- **Subjects**: cs.IT; cs.AI; cs.NI
- **Tags**: Semantic Communications
- **Summary**: 针对语义通信在实际MIMO-OFDM系统中因噪声和非线性失真导致性能下降的问题，分析了功率放大器非线性和峰均比变化的影响，并提出了缓解策略，使语义系统接近理论性能。关键结果：识别了信道频率选择性是关键因素，验证了缓解策略的有效性。

<h3 id="500">[500] Ontology-Guided Reverse Thinking Makes Large Language Models Stronger on Knowledge Graph Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.11491](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11491) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Runxuan Liu, Bei Luo, Jiaqi Li, Baoxin Wang, Ming Liu, Dayong Wu, Shijin Wang, Bing Qin
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对知识图谱问答中多跳推理困难的问题，提出ORT框架，利用本体知识引导逆向推理，从目的标签反向构建推理路径。关键结果：在WebQSP和CWQ数据集上达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="501">[501] Overcoming Dependent Censoring in the Evaluation of Survival Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.19460](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.19460) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Christian Marius Lillelund, Shi-ang Qi, Russell Greiner
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Survival Analysis, Evaluation Methodology
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/thecml/DependentEVAL)
- **Summary**: 针对生存模型评估中因依赖删失导致常用指标失效的问题，提出了基于阿基米德Copula的依赖Brier分数，并建立了半合成框架。关键结果：在12个数据集上平均降低估计误差12-16%。

<h3 id="502">[502] Distributionally Robust Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.00539](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00539) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Debmalya Mandal, Paulius Sasnauskas, Goran Radanovic
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: RLHF, LLM Alignment
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对RLHF在分布偏移下性能下降的问题，提出分布鲁棒的RLHF和DPO方法，并给出收敛保证。关键结果：在OOD任务上提升了奖励模型和策略的准确率，尤其在推理任务上。

<h3 id="503">[503] Pose-Based Fall Detection System: Efficient Monitoring on Standard CPUs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.19501](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19501) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 该论文提出一种基于姿态估计的跌倒检测系统，使用MediaPipe在普通CPU上实时处理，通过阈值分析和投票机制区分跌倒与非跌倒活动，无需专用传感器或GPU，适用于老年护理场景。

<h3 id="504">[504] Towards Harnessing the Collaborative Power of Large and Small Models for Domain Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.17421](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.17421) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yang Liu, Kejia Zhang, Bingjie Yan, Tianyuan Zou, Jianqing Zhang, Zixuan Gu, Xiangsen Chen, Jianbing Ding, Xidong Wang, Jingyi Li, Xiaozhou Ye, Ye Ouyang, Qiang Yang, Ya-Qin Zhang

<h3 id="505">[505] Mitigating Hallucinations via Inter-Layer Consistency Aggregation in Large Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.12343](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12343) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, LLM Hallucination, Decoding Strategy
- **Summary**: 该论文提出一种无需训练的解码机制DCLA，通过聚合前层表示构建动态语义参考并纠正语义偏离层，增强层间一致性，显著减少大型视觉语言模型中的幻觉，在多个基准上提升性能。

<h3 id="506">[506] Representation Learning for Equivariant Inference with Guarantees</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.19809](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19809) (replaced)

- **Venue**: International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML-2026)
- **Summary**: 针对对称性在统计学习中的保证问题，提出等变表示学习框架，解决回归、条件概率估计和不确定性量化，并提供非渐近统计学习保证。关键结果：在合成数据和机器人学应用中表现优异，不确定性校准良好。

<h3 id="507">[507] Physics-Informed Distillation of Diffusion Models for PDE-Constrained Generation</h3>

- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Physics-Informed Learning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 该论文指出扩散模型在施加PDE约束时存在Jensen Gap问题，提出后蒸馏方法PIDDM，在蒸馏阶段而非扩散过程中施加物理约束，实现单步生成并提升PDE满足度，在多个PDE基准上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="508">[508] Scaling Textual Gradients via Sampling-Based Momentum</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.00400](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00400) (replaced)

<h3 id="509">[509] Multimodal Representation Alignment for Cross-modal Information Retrieval</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 针对传统ABM行为简化且LLM Agent扩展困难的问题，提出Light Society框架，通过混合模型引擎实现超过10亿智能体的大规模社会模拟。关键结果：在信任游戏和观点传播模拟中展示高保真度和效率。

<h3 id="510">[510] Towards Biosignals-Free Autonomous Prosthetic Hand Control via Imitation Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.08795](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.08795) (replaced)

<h3 id="511">[511] Modeling Earth-Scale Human-Like Societies with One Billion Agents</h3>

- **Summary**: 该论文提出Light Society框架，利用LLM驱动的大规模智能体模拟，通过混合模型引擎（完整LLM与蒸馏代理）实现超过十亿智能体的高效模拟，在信任博弈和观点扩散中表现高保真度，支持行星尺度社会现象研究。

<h3 id="512">[512] MGDFIS: Multi-scale Global-detail Feature Integration Strategy for Small Object Detection</h3>

- **Summary**: 该论文提出多尺度全局-细节特征整合策略MGDFIS，用于无人机图像中的小目标检测，通过全局上下文交换、局部细节恢复和像素级前景背景重校准，在VisDrone等数据集上显著提升检测性能。

<h3 id="513">[513] Code Reasoning for Software Engineering Tasks: A Survey and A Call to Action</h3>

<h3 id="514">[514] GeNeRT: A Physics-Informed Approach to Intelligent Wireless Channel Modeling via Generalizable Neural Ray Tracing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.18295](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.18295) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kejia Bian, Meixia Tao, Shu Sun, Tongjia Zhang, Jun Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Wireless Networks, Physics-Informed Learning, Neural Ray Tracing

<h3 id="516">[516] Beyond Correlation: Learning Supervised, Sample-Distinct, and Eigenimage-Interpretable Representations</h3>

- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/WebPAI/EfficientUICoder)
- **Summary**: 针对多模态LLM在UI2Code任务中计算开销大的问题，提出EfficientUICoder压缩框架，实现55-60%压缩比，计算成本降低44.9%，推理时间减少48.8%。

<h3 id="525">[525] Discovering New Theorems via LLMs with In-Context Proof Learning in Lean</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.14274](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14274) (replaced)

<h3 id="526">[526] ArchesClimate: Probabilistic Decadal Ensemble Generation With Flow Matching</h3>

- **Summary**: 针对气候模型大集合模拟计算昂贵的问题，提出基于流匹配的ArchesClimate仿真器，十年自回归生成稳定且物理一致，与IPSL模型可互换。

<h3 id="527">[527] Predicting Effects, Missing Distributions: Evaluating LLMs as Human Behavior Simulators in Operations Management</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.03310](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03310) (replaced)

<h3 id="528">[528] Are LLMs Reliable Rankers? Rank Manipulation via Two-Stage Token Optimization</h3>

- **Summary**: 针对LLM作为排序器易受小提示操纵的问题，提出Rank Anything First方法，通过两阶段token优化生成自然语言扰动显著提升目标项排名，暴露安全漏洞。

<h3 id="529">[529] CLARity: Reasoning Consistency Alone Can Teach Reinforced Experts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.09278](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09278) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reward Design
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Infinite-set/CLARity)
- **Summary**: 针对数据稀缺领域专家LLM训练中，基于结果的强化学习可能降低推理一致性的问题，提出CLARity框架，利用小型通用LLM通过一致性感知奖励和两阶段训练流水线提升推理质量。在实验中，该方法将响应一致性提升16.5%，准确率提升7.5%。

<h3 id="530">[530] Attribution Graphs and Causal Probing for Mechanistic Discovery and Bias Repair in Multimodal Generative Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.12957](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12957) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Noor Islam S. Mohammad, Uluğ Bayazıt
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 为解决生成式模型内部机制不可见及偏差问题，提出归因图（AGs）和因果探针方法，实现电路级表示和因果潜在结构识别，并引入认知对齐分数（CAS）等机制。在多个数据集上达到94.1%准确率，92.3%宏F1，同时将子组偏差减少41%。

<h3 id="531">[531] Emergence of Minimal Circuits for Indirect Object Identification in Attention-Only Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.25013](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25013) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rabin Adhikari
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Transformer Architecture
- **Venue**: ACL (Volume 4: Student Research Workshop)
- **Summary**: 为揭示Transformer在间接对象识别（IOI）任务中的最小计算机制，从头训练小型注意力仅Transformer模型。发现单层双头模型即可完美完成IOI任务，两个头分别充当加性和对比性子电路。

<h3 id="532">[532] Automatic Extraction of Road Networks by using Teacher-Student Adaptive Structural Deep Belief Network and Its Application to Landslide Disaster</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.05567](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05567) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shin Kamada, Takumi Ichimura
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Image Segmentation, Disaster Response
- **Summary**: 针对遥感图像道路网络提取精度不足问题，提出基于教师-学生自适应深度信念网络的集成学习方法，并在边缘设备上轻量部署。在七个城市测试中，检测准确率从40%提升至89%，并成功应用于滑坡灾害后的道路可用性检测。

<h3 id="533">[533] Can Fine-Tuning Erase Your Edits? On the Fragile Coexistence of Knowledge Editing and Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.05852](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05852) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yinjie Cheng, Paul Youssef, Christin Seifert, Jörg Schlötterer, Zhixue Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Editing, Fine-Tuning, Model Editing
- **Venue**: KDD 2026
- **Summary**: 研究知识编辑后的模型经微调后编辑是否留存，发现编辑效果会大幅衰减。AlphaEdit在GPT-J上编辑后经微调，25.27%的编辑失效。仅微调编辑层可有效移除编辑，且下游性能损失较小。

<h3 id="534">[534] Hard-constraint physics-residual networks for hydrogen crossover prediction and high-pressure extrapolation in PEM water electrolysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.05879](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05879) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yong-Woon Kim, Jihyeok Lee, Chulung Kang, Yung-Cheol Byun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Surrogate Model
- **Summary**: 针对PEM电解槽中氢气交叉扩散预测困难问题，提出硬约束物理残差网络（PR-Net），嵌入亨利、菲克和法拉第定律，仅学习残差校正。在1-200 bar范围内R²达99.57%，外推至200 bar时R²仍达94.02%，远超纯数据驱动和软约束PINN方法。

<h3 id="535">[535] SWE-fficiency: Can Language Models Optimize Real-World Repositories on Real Workloads?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.06090](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.06090) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.PF
- **Tags**: Code Optimization, Software Engineering, LLM Agent
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对现有基准侧重修复内容而非修复方式的问题，提出SWE-fficiency基准，评估仓库级别性能优化。包含498个任务，涵盖numpy、pandas等仓库。当前最优智能体仅达到专家加速比的0.23倍，在定位瓶颈和保持正确性方面表现不足。

<h3 id="536">[536] Scalable Synthesis of distributed LLM workloads through Symbolic Tensor Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.10480](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.10480) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Changhai Man, Joongun Park, Hanjiang Wu, Huan Xu, Srinivas Sridharan, Tushar Krishna

- **Summary**: 为解决大规模LLM训练和推理系统的性能建模问题，提出STAGE框架，通过符号张量图合成高保真执行图，支持多种并行化策略。可扩展到128K GPU，并保持张量级精度。

<h3 id="537">[537] Skin-R1: Clinical Knowledge-Guided Dermatological Diagnosis Using Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.14900](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14900) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zehao Liu, Weijieying Ren, Jipeng Zhang, Tianxiang Zhao, Jingxi Zhu, Xiaoting Li, Vasant G Honavar
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对视觉语言模型在皮肤科诊断中可靠性不足的问题，提出Skin-R1，整合基于教材的临床推理监督和强化学习。通过推理生成器、有监督微调和层次化奖励RL，在多个皮肤科基准上持续提升诊断准确性和鲁棒性。

<h3 id="538">[538] SWITCH: Benchmarking Modeling and Handling of Tangible Interfaces in Long-horizon Embodied Scenarios</h3>

- **Authors**: Juntao Cheng, Wanyue Zhang, Zhiwei Yu, Shuo Ren, Zheqi He, Shaoxuan Xie, Guocai Yao, Jieru Lin, Börje F. Karlsson, Jiajun Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Benchmark, Interactive Reasoning
- **Summary**: 为评估具身智能体与实物控制界面的闭环交互推理能力，提出SWITCH基准，包含1170个时间交互视频和结构化标注。评估显示前沿多模态模型在细粒度视觉-时间感知、结果验证和错误恢复方面存在持续弱点。

<h3 id="539">[539] When, How Long and How Much? Interpretable Neural Networks for Time Series Regression by Learning to Mask and Aggregate</h3>

- **Authors**: Florent Forest, Amaury Wei, Olga Fink
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 针对时间序列回归模型黑箱化问题，提出MAGNETS架构，通过学习掩码聚合形成人类可理解的概念，并采用加性结构透明地组合概念以做出预测。该架构无需概念标注即可提供内在可解释性。

<h3 id="540">[540] Experience-Evolving Multi-Turn Tool-Use Agent with Hybrid Episodic-Procedural Memory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.07287](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07287) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 针对多轮工具使用智能体面临不断变化决策情境的问题，提出混合情景-程序记忆策略（H-EPM），通过自适应重用部分重叠的成功经验，并在推理和训练中指导强化学习探索。在多个基准上实现高达50%的推理性能提升。

<h3 id="541">[541] Weighted Contrastive Learning for Anomaly-Aware Time-Series Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.07569](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07569) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Anomaly Detection, Contrastive Learning
- **Summary**: 针对异常条件下多变量时间序列预测可靠性下降问题，提出加权对比适应方法（WECA），通过对齐正常和异常增强表示，保留异常相关信息同时保持一致性。在ATM交易数据集上，异常数据SMAPE降低6.1个百分点，正常数据性能几乎无损。

<h3 id="542">[542] ORCA: Open-ended Response Correctness Assessment for Audio Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.09066](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.09066) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Šimon Sedláček, Sara Barahona, Bolaji Yusuf, Laura Herrera-Alarcón, Santosh Kesiraju, Cecilia Bolaños, Alicia Lozano-Diez, Sathvik Udupa, Fernando López, Allison Ferner, Ramani Duraiswami, Jan Černocký
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Audio-Language Model, Question Answering, Model Evaluation
- **Venue**: TACL
- **Summary**: 为可靠评估大型音频语言模型的开放响应正确性，提出ORCA方法，采用三阶段标注流水线和课程学习。ORCA与人工评分的斯皮尔曼相关性达0.91，泛化至未见基准得分为0.85，且预测方差与人类分歧强相关。

<h3 id="543">[543] AI4EOSC: a Federated Cloud Platform for Artificial Intelligence in Scientific Research</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.16455](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16455) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ignacio Heredia, Álvaro López García, Fernando Aguilar Gómez, Diego Aguirre, Caterina Alarcón Marín, Khadijeh Alibabaei, Lisana Berberi, Miguel Caballer, Amanda Calatrava, Pedro Castro, Alessandro Costantini, Mario David, Jaime Díez Stefan Dlugolinsky, Borja Esteban Sanchis, Giacinto Donvito, Leonhard Duda, Saúl Fernandez, Andrés Heredia Canales, Valentin Kozlov, Sergio Langarita, João Machado, Germán Moltó, Daniel San Martín, Martin Šeleng, Giang Nguyen, Marcin Płóciennik, Marta Obregón Ruiz, Susana Rebolledo Ruiz, Vicente Rodriguez, Judith Sáinz-Pardo Díaz, Viet Tran

<h3 id="544">[544] InsertAnywhere: Geometrically Grounded and Optics-Aware Video Object Insertion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.17504](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.17504) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hoiyeong Jin, Hyojin Jang, Junha Hyung, Jeongho Kim, Kinam Kim, Dongjin Kim, Huijin Choi, Hyeonji Kim, Jaegul Choo
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Diffusion Model, Image Editing
- **Summary**: 针对视频对象插入缺乏4D场景理解和光学交互的问题，提出InsertAnywhere框架，包括4D感知掩码生成、光学感知表示对齐和ROSE++数据集。在复杂真实场景中生成几何合理且光度真实的对象插入，显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="545">[545] Neural Minimum Weight Perfect Matching for Quantum Error Codes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.00242](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.00242) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yotam Peled, David Zenati, Eliya Nachmani
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.IT; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Quantum Error Correction
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文针对量子纠错解码问题，提出了一种混合架构的神经网络最小权重完美匹配解码器（NMWPM），结合图神经网络提取局部特征和Transformer捕捉全局依赖来预测动态边权重。通过代理损失函数实现端到端优化，在去极化噪声下达到接近最大似然界的阈值（17.9%和10.95%）。

<h3 id="546">[546] Value-Action Alignment in Large Language Models under Privacy-Prosocial Conflict</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.03546](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03546) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Guanyu Chen, Chenxiao Yu, Xiyang Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Privacy, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 本文针对大语言模型在个人数据共享决策中隐私和亲社会倾向冲突时评估不足的问题，提出了基于上下文的评估协议，通过顺序问卷测量隐私态度、亲社会性和数据共享接受度，并引入价值-行动对齐率（VAAR）指标。实验发现模型存在特定的隐私-PSA-数据共享轮廓，且价值-行动对齐存在异质性。

<h3 id="547">[547] Lost in Execution: On the Multilingual Robustness of Tool Calling in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.05366](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05366) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zheng Luo, T Pranav Kutralingam, Ogochukwu N Okoani, Wanpeng Xu, Hua Wei, Xiyang Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multilingual Learning, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 本文针对大语言模型工具调用在多语言场景下鲁棒性不足的问题，提出了多语言工具调用基准MLCL，并在中文、印地语和伊博语上进行系统评估。通过细粒度错误分析，发现参数值语言不匹配是主要失败模式，即使模型理解正确，也会因使用用户语言生成参数值导致执行错误，现有策略无法完全恢复英文水平。

<h3 id="548">[548] From Word Sequences to Behavioral Sequences: Adapting Modeling and Evaluation Paradigms for Longitudinal NLP</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.07988](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07988) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Adithya V Ganesan, Vasudha Varadarajan, Oscar NE Kjell, Whitney R Ringwald, Scott Feltman, Benjamin J Luft, Roman Kotov, Ryan L Boyd, H Andrew Schwartz
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Clinical NLP, Mental Health, Time Series Analysis
- **Summary**: 本文针对传统NLP将文档视为独立无序样本的假设在纵向研究中不成立的问题，提出了纵向建模与评估范式，包括按人和时间划分的评估拆分、区分个体间和个体内动态的准确率指标、序列输入以及不同粗粒度的潜在状态模型。在17k份每日日记和PTSD症状数据集上验证，显示传统文档级评估可能得出相反结论。

<h3 id="549">[549] A Comparative Study of Student Perspectives on Technical Writing Feedback Quality: Evaluating LLMs, SLMs, and Humans in Computer Science Topics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.11541](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.11541) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Suqing Liu, Runlong Ye, Christopher Eaton, Bogdan Simion, Michael Liut
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Education Technology, LLM Evaluation, Writing Assistance
- **Venue**: AIED 2026
- **Summary**: 本文针对计算机科学领域技术写作反馈的可扩展性及隐私成本问题，评估了本地小语言模型（SLM）的反馈质量。通过混合方法分析学生感知，发现本地SLM在技术课程中可读性和可操作性评分高于大型商用LLM，但在高度专业化任务中人类反馈更受青睐。表明本地SLM可作为隐私保护且零边际成本的替代方案。

<h3 id="550">[550] CytoCLIP: Learning Cytoarchitectural Characteristics in Developing Human Brain Using Contrastive Language Image Pre-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.12282](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12282) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Pralaypati Ta, Sriram Venkatesaperumal, Keerthi Ram, Mohanasankar Sivaprakasam
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model, CLIP
- **Summary**: 本文针对人工划分人脑组织切片中细胞构筑区域耗时且需专业知识的问题，提出了CytoCLIP，基于预训练CLIP的视觉-语言模型，学习脑细胞构筑的联合表示。包含低分辨率全区域和高分辨率图块两种变体，在胎儿脑组织数据上训练，区域分类加权F1达到0.87和0.91。

<h3 id="551">[551] Diff-MN: Diffusion Parameterized MoE-NCDE for Continuous Time Series Generation with Irregular Observations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.13534](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.13534) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xu Zhang, Junwei Deng, Chang Xu, Hao Li, Jiang Bian
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Diffusion Model, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/microsoft/TimeCraft/tree/main/Diff-MN)
- **Summary**: 本文针对现有时间序列生成方法假设规则采样和固定分辨率的问题，提出了Diff-MN框架，用混合专家（MoE）增强神经控制微分方程（NCDE），并通过扩散模型参数化NCDE的时变动态参数（MoE权重），实现样本特定的连续时间序列生成。在十个数据集上超越强基线。

<h3 id="552">[552] Demonstration-Free Robotic Control via LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.20334](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20334) (replaced)

- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Embodied AI, Robotic Manipulation
- **Venue**: IROS 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/robiemusketeer/faea-sim)
- **Summary**: 本文针对视觉-语言-动作模型需要任务特定演示和微调且泛化性差的问题，提出将通用LLM智能体框架直接用于具身操作，无需修改。FAEA在LIBERO、ManiSkill3和MetaWorld上分别达到84.9%、85.7%和96%的成功率，无需演示或微调，可自主探索生成成功轨迹用于数据增强。

<h3 id="553">[553] Assessing the Business Process Modeling Competences of Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21787](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21787) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chantale Lauer, Peter Pfeiffer, Alexander Rombach, Nijat Mehdiyev
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Business Process Management, Process Modeling
- **Summary**: 本文针对缺乏对LLM生成BPMN模型质量系统性评估的问题，提出了BEF4LLM评估框架，涵盖语法、语用、语义质量和有效性四个维度。对开源LLM和人类建模专家进行对比，结果显示LLM在语法和语用质量上优秀，但在语义质量上人类更优，分数差距较小。

<h3 id="554">[554] Offline Reinforcement Learning of High-Quality Behaviors Under Robust Style Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.22823](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22823) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mathieu Petitbois, Rémy Portelas, Sylvain Lamprier
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Offline RL
- **Summary**: 本文针对离线强化学习中风格与奖励矛盾导致的分布偏移问题，定义了行为风格并提出了SCIQL框架，利用离线目标条件RL技术（如后见重标记和价值学习）和门控优势加权回归，在保持风格对齐的同时优化任务性能。实验表明优于先前离线方法。

<h3 id="555">[555] Agile Reinforcement Learning through Separable Neural Architecture and Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.23225](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23225) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rajib Mostakim, Reza T. Batley, Sourav Saha
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Neural Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文针对深度强化学习中多层感知器参数效率低且样本效率差的问题，提出了基于样条的自适应网络SPAN，通过可学习的预处理层适配KHRONOS框架。在离散和连续控制任务及真实HVAC控制中，SPAN实现30-50%的样本效率提升和1.3-9倍成功率提高，且总训练成本更低。

<h3 id="556">[556] A Large-Scale Dataset for Molecular Structure-Language Description via a Rule-Regularized Method</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02320](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02320) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Feiyang Cai, Guijuan He, Yi Hu, Jingjing Wang, Joshua Luo, Tianyu Zhu, Srikanth Pilla, Gang Li, Ling Liu, Feng Luo
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; q-bio.BM
- **Tags**: Molecular Generation, Drug Discovery, Dataset
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/TheLuoFengLab/MolLangData)
- **Summary**: 本文针对分子结构与自然语言对齐所需大规模高质量标注数据难以获取的问题，提出了全自动标注框架，基于规则化学命名解析器构建结构XML元数据，引导LLM生成精确描述。构建了约16.3万对分子-描述数据集，验证精度达98.6%。

<h3 id="557">[557] Test-Time Detoxification without Training or Learning Anything</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02498](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02498) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Baturay Saglam, Dionysis Kalogerias
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Test-Time Adaptation, Black-box Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文针对大语言模型生成有毒文本的问题，提出了一种无需训练或梯度的测试时解毒方法。通过零阶优化近似完成毒性相对于输入嵌入的梯度，并利用少量下降步数引导生成无毒内容。该方法仅需访问输入嵌入和毒性评分函数，实现稳健的毒性降低，且在多数设置中达到最佳毒性-质量权衡。

<h3 id="558">[558] ImprovEvolve: Basin-Hopping Meets LLM-Guided Evolutionary Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.10233](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10233) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alexey Kravatskiy, Valentin Khrulkov, Ivan Oseledets
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; math.CA; math.MG; math.OC
- **Tags**: Evolutionary Algorithm, Mathematical Reasoning, Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文针对LLM引导的进化计算在解决数学优化问题时认知负荷高的问题，提出了ImprovEvolve，将程序分解为初始化、局部改进和扰动三个专用算子，迭代应用局部改进和强度调度扰动，实现盆地跳跃搜索。在六边形堆积、自相关不等式和球面码问题上发现了新的最优构造。

<h3 id="559">[559] General and Efficient Steering of Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.11395](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11395) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qingsong Wang, Mikhail Belkin, Yusu Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Image Generation, Conditional Generation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/isotrivial/na-rfm)
- **Summary**: 本文针对扩散模型在未见条件下引导需要重训练或每步梯度计算的问题，提出了NA-RFM，结合噪声对齐和递归特征机激活引导，无需推理时梯度计算。在多个数据集上提高了目标准确率和FID，并大幅加速推理。

<h3 id="560">[560] Choose Your Agent: Tradeoffs in Adopting AI Advisors, Coaches, and Delegates in Multi-Party Negotiation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.12089](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12089) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kehang Zhu, Nithum Thain, Vivian Tsai, James Wexler, Crystal Qian
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Negotiation System, Human-AI Interaction
- **Summary**: 本文针对AI在社交环境中辅助多轮谈判时不同交互模态的效果问题，进行了在线行为实验，比较了顾问（主动建议）、教练（被动反馈）和委托（自主执行）三种模态。发现参与者偏好高控制的顾问模式，但只有委托模式显著增加集体总剩余，原因在于人类会过滤AI建议。

<h3 id="561">[561] Mitigating the Safety-utility Trade-off in LLM Alignment via Adaptive Safe Context Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.13562](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13562) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yanbo Wang, Minzheng Wang, Jian Liang, Lu Wang, Yongcan Yu, Ran He
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Reasoning, RLHF
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ybwang119/ASCL)
- **Summary**: 针对大语言模型对齐中安全性与效用性之间的权衡问题，现有方法通过规则蒸馏限制了推理能力。本文提出自适应安全上下文学习（ASCL）框架，将安全对齐建模为多轮工具使用过程，并引入逆频率策略优化（IFPO）来平衡优势估计。实验表明，该方法在整体性能上优于基线。

<h3 id="562">[562] WoVR: World Models as Reliable Simulators for Post-Training VLA Policies with RL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.13977](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13977) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhennan Jiang, Shangqing Zhou, Yutong Jiang, Zefang Huang, Mingjie Wei, Yuhui Chen, Tianxing Zhou, Zhen Guo, Hao Lin, Quanlu Zhang, Yu Wang, Haoran Li, Chao Yu, Dongbin Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model-Based RL, Embodied AI
- **Summary**: 强化学习在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型后训练中需要大量真实交互，而世界模型存在幻觉和长程误差累积。本文提出WoVR框架，通过可控动作条件视频世界模型、关键帧初始化展开和世界模型-策略协同进化，实现稳定的长程想象展开。在LIBERO基准和多种机器人平台上取得优越性能。

<h3 id="563">[563] On the Emergence of Implicit Curriculum in RLVR Learning Dynamics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.14872](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14872) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yu Huang, Zixin Wen, Yuejie Chi, Yuting Wei, Aarti Singh, Yingbin Liang, Yuxin Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.OC; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Reasoning, Grokking
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）如何克服长程推理障碍仍不明确。本文通过傅里叶分析理论揭示，混合难度训练自然产生隐式课程：简单问题先可学习，进而塑造困难问题的前沿。当难度谱平滑时，训练进入中继模式；存在突变时，则出现grokking型相变。

<h3 id="564">[564] How to Train Your Long-Context Visual Document Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.15257](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15257) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Austin Veselka
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Long Context, Vision-Language Model, Document Understanding
- **Summary**: 针对长上下文视觉语言模型的训练 recipe 不可复现的问题，本文系统研究了继续预训练、监督微调和偏好优化，发现匹配评估长度的训练、使用页码索引等关键因素。在MMLongBenchDoc上取得最佳性能，并发布了修正版基准。

<h3 id="565">[565] Spanning the Visual Analogy Space with a Weight Basis of LoRAs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.15727](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15727) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hila Manor, Rinon Gal, Haggai Maron, Tomer Michaeli, Gal Chechik
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.GR; cs.LG; eess.IV
- **Tags**: Image Editing, LoRA, Text-to-Image
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 视觉类比学习中使用单个LoRA模块无法泛化到多种变换。本文提出LoRWeB，通过可学习的LoRA基和轻量编码器动态组合变换原语。实验表明，该方法在未见变换上取得最优泛化性能。

<h3 id="566">[566] Enhanced Diffusion Sampling: Efficient Rare Event Sampling and Free Energy Calculation with Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.16634](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16634) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yu Xie, Ludwig Winkler, Lixin Sun, Sarah Lewis, Adam E. Foster, José Jiménez Luna, Tim Hempel, Michael Gastegger, Yaoyi Chen, Iryna Zaporozhets, Cecilia Clementi, Christopher M. Bishop, Frank Noé
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Molecular Generation, Computational Biology
- **Summary**: 分子动力学中稀有事件采样问题限制了平衡态性质计算。本文提出增强扩散采样，利用引导协议生成偏置系综并通过精确重加权恢复平衡统计。在蛋白质折叠等系统中，该方法可在数分钟内完成准确估计。

<h3 id="567">[567] DyGnROLE: Asymmetric Pretraining for Edge Classification on Dynamic Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.23135](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23135) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tyler Bonnet, Marek Rei
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.SI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Dynamic Graph Learning, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 有向动态图上的边分类需要建模源节点和目标节点的非对称模式。本文提出DyGnROLE，使用分离嵌入表和角色语义位置编码，并引入方向角色对齐（DRA）自监督预训练。在八个数据集上的四个边分类任务中持续优于现有方法。

<h3 id="568">[568] SOTAlign: Semi-Supervised Alignment of Unimodal Vision and Language Models via Optimal Transport</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.23353](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23353) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Simon Roschmann, Paul Krzakala, Sonia Mazelet, Quentin Bouniot, Zeynep Akata
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Optimal Transport, Semi-Supervised Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ExplainableML/SOTAlign)
- **Summary**: 对齐预训练的单模态视觉和语言模型通常需要大量配对样本。本文提出SOTAlign，先通过线性教师从少量配对样本恢复粗略共享几何，再基于最优传输的散度在未配对样本上精炼对齐。在多个数据集上显著优于监督和半监督基线。

<h3 id="569">[569] What Capable Agents Must Know: Selection Theorems for Robust Decision-Making under Uncertainty</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.02491](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02491) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Aran Nayebi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.RO; q-bio.NC; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Learning Theory, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Venue**: UAI 2026
- **Summary**: 智能体在不确定性下实现鲁棒决策需要何种内部结构？本文证明强任务性能迫使世界模型、信念状态和类似情绪的持久状态变量的出现，并具有信息模块性。这些结果在非最优、非确定性条件下成立。

<h3 id="570">[570] Edit in 2D, Verify in 3D: Reinforcement Learning for Multi-view Consistent Scene Editing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.03143](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03143) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiyuan Wang, Chunyu Lin, Lei Sun, Zhi Cao, Yuyang Yin, Lang Nie, Zhenlong Yuan, Xiangxiang Chu, Yunchao Wei, Kang Liao, Guosheng Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Diffusion Model, 3D Editing
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 基于2D扩散先验的3D编辑存在多视角一致性问题，且配对3D编辑数据稀缺。本文提出RL3DEdit，利用3D基础模型VGGT的置信图和位姿误差作为奖励，通过强化学习优化多视角一致性。实验显示编辑质量优于现有方法。

<h3 id="571">[571] The Hidden Cost of Structured Generation in LLMs: Draft-Conditioned Constrained Decoding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.03305](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03305) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Avinash Reddy, Thayne T. Walker, James S. Ide, Amrit Singh Bedi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Constrained Decoding
- **Summary**: 约束解码可能因模型对有效续接的概率低而扭曲生成。本文提出草稿条件约束解码（DCCD），先生成非约束草稿，再在其上应用约束解码。在结构化推理基准上，严格结构准确率提升高达24个百分点，且小型模型可达大型模型水平。

<h3 id="572">[572] Rethinking Role-Playing Evaluation: Anonymous Benchmarking and a Systematic Study of Personality Effects</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.03915](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03915) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ji-Lun Peng, Yun-Nung Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Role-Playing Agent, Persona
- **Summary**: 当前角色扮演评估依赖知名角色，可能利用模型的角色记忆而非真实能力。本文提出匿名基准测试以解耦角色识别与扮演能力，并研究人格增强方法。实验表明匿名化降低性能，而引入人格信息持续改进表现。

<h3 id="573">[573] Longitudinal Lesion Inpainting in Brain MRI via 3D Region Aware Diffusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.05693](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05693) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zahra Karimaghaloo, Dumitru Fetco, Haz-Edine Assemlal, Hassan Rivaz, Douglas L. Arnold
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Inpainting, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 脑MRI纵向分析受病变演化干扰，现有修补方法缺乏3D连续性。本文提出基于去噪扩散概率模型的伪3D纵向修补框架，利用多通道条件和区域感知扩散（RAD）。在感知质量、纵向稳定性和速度（快10倍）上显著优于基线。

<h3 id="574">[574] Proof-of-Guardrail in AI Agents and What (Not) to Trust from It</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.05786](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05786) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xisen Jin, Michael Duan, Qin Lin, Aaron Chan, Zhenglun Chen, Junyi Du, Xiang Ren
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: AI Safety, LLM Agent, Trusted Execution Environment
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/SaharaLabsAI/Verifiable-ClawGuard)
- **Summary**: AI智能体在线服务中，用户需依赖开发者安全性声明，存在虚假宣传风险。本文提出护栏证明系统，在可信执行环境（TEE）中运行智能体和护栏，生成可验证的密码学证明。系统保证了护栏执行的完整性，同时保持智能体私有。

<h3 id="575">[575] HEARTS: Benchmarking LLM Reasoning on Health Time Series</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.06638](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06638) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sirui Li, Shuhan Xiao, Mihir Joshi, Ahmed Metwally, Daniel McDuff, Wei Wang, Yuzhe Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Time Series Forecasting, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 现有健康时间序列基准覆盖模态和任务有限。本文提出HEARTS基准，整合16个数据集、12个健康领域、20种信号模态和110个任务。评估16个LLM发现，LLM显著落后于专用模型，在多步时间推理上表现困难。

<h3 id="576">[576] Feature-level Interaction Explanations in Multimodal Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.13326](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13326) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yeji Kim, Housam Khalifa Bashier Babiker, Mi-Young Kim, Randy Goebel
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Explainable AI, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/dut0817/FL-I2MoE)
- **Summary**: 多模态Transformer的解释方法难以指明跨模态特征对的协同或冗余关系。本文提出特征级I2MoE层，在特征级别分离唯一、协同和冗余证据，并引入蒙特卡洛交互探测。在三个基准上产生更集中的重要性模式，且成对掩码实验证明因果相关性。

<h3 id="577">[577] FlatLands: Generative Floormap Completion From a Single Egocentric View</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.16016](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16016) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Subhransu S. Bhattacharjee, Dylan Campbell, Rahul Shome
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO; eess.IV
- **Tags**: Floorplan Generation, Visual Navigation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对单视角俯视地图补全问题，提出了FlatLands数据集和基准，包含27万多个观察数据。方法比较了多种生成模型，并建立了端到端的单目RGB到地面图的管线。在生成式室内地图补全和具身导航方面提供了严格的测试平台。

<h3 id="578">[578] DiscoGen: Procedural Generation of Algorithm Discovery Tasks in Machine Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.17863](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17863) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alexander D. Goldie, Zilin Wang, Adrian Hayler, Deepak Nathani, Edan Toledo, Ken Thampiratwong, Aleksandra Kalisz, Michael Beukman, Hannah Erlebach, Alistair Letcher, Shashank Reddy, Clarisse Wibault, Theo Wolf, Charles O&#39;Neill, Uljad Berdica, Nicholas Roberts, Saeed Rahmani, Roberta Raileanu, Shimon Whiteson, Jakob N. Foerster
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Procedural Generation, AutoML, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/AlexGoldie/discogen)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对算法发现任务套件的局限性，提出了DiscoGen，一种程序化生成机器学习算法发现任务的方法，可生成数十亿个不同难度的任务。并发布DiscoBench用于评估。通过自动化提示调整实验展示了其用途。

<h3 id="579">[579] Em-Garde: A Propose-Match Framework for Proactive Streaming Video Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.19054](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19054) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yikai Zheng, Xin Ding, Yifan Yang, Shiqi Jiang, Hao Wu, Qianxi Zhang, Weijun Wang, Ting Cao, Yunxin Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Multimodal Agent
- **Summary**: 该论文针对主动式流视频理解中每帧触发决策的效率-精度矛盾，提出了Em-Garde框架，将语义理解与流感知解耦。通过指令引导的提议解析和轻量匹配模块实现高效触发。在基准测试中显著提升了主动响应的准确率和效率。

<h3 id="580">[580] UniMotion: A Unified Framework for Motion-Text-Vision Understanding and Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.22282](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22282) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ziyi Wang, Xinshun Wang, Shuang Chen, Yang Cong, Mengyuan Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Motion Generation
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有统一多模态模型只处理部分模态且离散分词导致误差的问题，提出了UniMotion框架，将运动视为连续模态。通过跨模态对齐运动VAE、双后验KL对齐和潜在重建对齐实现任意模态间的理解、生成和编辑。在七个任务上达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="581">[581] Grounding Sim-to-Real Generalization in Robotic Manipulation: An Empirical Study with Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.22876](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22876) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ruixing Jin, Zicheng Zhu, Ruixiang Ouyang, Sheng Xu, Bo Yue, Zhizheng Wu, Guiliang Liu

<h3 id="582">[582] CAPTCHA Solving for Native GUI Agents: Automated Reasoning-Action Data Generation and Self-Corrective Training</h3>

<h3 id="583">[583] FD$^2$: A Dedicated Framework for Fine-Grained Dataset Distillation</h3>

<h3 id="584">[584] Sustainable Hybrid Document-Routed Retrieval for Financial RAG: Resolving the Robustness-Precision Trade-off</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.26815](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26815) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhiyuan Cheng, Longying Lai, Yue Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: RAG, Financial AI, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 该论文针对金融RAG中块检索的跨文档混淆与语义文件路由精度不足的权衡，提出了混合文档路由检索HDRR。两阶段架构先路由文档再在限定文档内进行块检索，消除了跨文档混淆。在FinDER基准上平均得分7.54，失败率降至6.4%，同时保持低token消耗。

<h3 id="585">[585] Steerable Visual Representations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.02327](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02327) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jona Ruthardt, Manu Gaur, Deva Ramanan, Makarand Tapaswi, Yuki M. Asano
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Vision-Language Model

- **Summary**: 该论文针对预训练ViT特征只能关注显著线索的问题，提出了可引导的视觉表示。通过早期融合将文本注入视觉编码器层，实现用自然语言引导特征。在可引导性基准上表现良好，并在异常检测和个性化目标区分上媲美专用方法。

<h3 id="586">[586] Internalized Reasoning for Long-Context Visual Document Understanding</h3>

- **Summary**: 该论文针对视觉长文档理解未探索推理能力的问题，提出了合成数据管线生成思考轨迹，并通过SFT和模型融合实现内部推理。在Qwen3 VL 32B上超越7倍大的模型，且内部推理相比显式推理减少12.4倍输出token。

<h3 id="587">[587] How Alignment Routes: Localizing, Scaling, and Controlling Policy Circuits in Language Models</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 该论文针对对齐训练产生的拒绝行为机制，定位了策略路由电路：中间层注意力门和放大器头。通过交换测试和消融实验证实门是因果必需的，并发现跨模型一致的电图案。连续调控可实现从拒绝到有害指导的转变，密码攻击可绕过路由。

<h3 id="588">[588] UniMamba: A Unified Spatial-Temporal Modeling Framework with State-Space and Attention Integration</h3>

<h3 id="589">[589] Towards Modality-Agnostic Medical Image Anomaly Detection: A Training-Free Manifold Refinement Approach</h3>

<h3 id="590">[590] Semantic Prompting: Agentic Incremental Narrative Refinement through Spatial Semantic Interaction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.19971](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19971) (replaced)

<h3 id="591">[591] Explainable AI in Speaker Recognition -- Making Latent Representations Understandable</h3>

<h3 id="592">[592] Defeasible Conditional Obligation in a Two-tiered Preference-based Semantics (Extended Version)</h3>

<h3 id="593">[593] Beyond SFT-to-RL: Pre-alignment via Black-Box On-Policy Distillation for Multimodal RL</h3>

<h3 id="605">[605] FBOS-RL: Feedback-Driven Bi-Objective Synergistic Reinforcement Learning</h3>

<h3 id="607">[607] Are Frontier LLMs Ready for Cybersecurity? Evidence for Vertical Foundation Models from Dual-Mode Vulnerability Benchmarks</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
